		Constantinople Fell to the Ottoman Turks : 1453
		
		Prince Henry of Portugal a.k.a Navigator==> Obsession with finding a new trade route to India 
		
			- Pope Nicholas V ne ise, 1454 me ek Bull diya---> 'Right to navigate the sea to the distant shores of the orient'. 
			
			
		1487 : Portuguese Navigator, Bartholomew Dias--> Rounded the Cape of Good Hope in Africa
		
		Treaty of Tordesillas(1494) ---> Portugal and Spain divided the non-christian world between them by an imaginary line in the Atlantic
		
		
		May 1498 :---> VDG arrived at Calicut at Sumurthi(zamorin)'s Port---> It was an entrepot.

	
	
	


	Europeans in India

           Portuguese
 
 		- VDG-> In May 1498, at Calicut(3 mahine ruka); came back again in 1501 and setup a factory at Cannanore and Cochin.
  
  		- Pedro Cavaral established a Factory at Calicut in 1500. (Cochin became their HQ)
  
  			Isne Arabs se lode liye. 
  			
  			Saale ne Calicut par bombardment ki. 
  			
  			Cochin and Cannanore ke rulers ke saath treaty ki. 
  
  		
  		- 1st Governor in India in 1505--> Francisco Almeida- Blue Water Policy(Cartaze System)--> To make them king in Indian Ocean. Fought naval battles with combined fleet of Egyptians and Gujrat.
  
  		- 2nd GG--> Alfanso Albaqurque- Real founder of Portuguese Power in the East		
  						
  				Established strongholds in Malabar, Malacca and East Africa 
  
   				Introduced permit system for non-portuguese ships
  				
  				Ship-building centres in the region par kabja kiya. 
  				
  				Acquired Goa in 1510 ---> 1st bit of Indian Territory to be in European Hands after, Alexander the Great
  				
  				Abolished sati
  				
  				New Crops--> Cashew, Tobacco; Coir plantations
  
  				
  				
  		- Next-> Nino-de-Cunha(1529 me Gov appoint hua)- Changed HQ to GOA 
  		
  			 Got island of Bassein from Bahadur Shah of Gujrat (Is bhai ke, Humayun lode le raha tha)
  			 
  			 Baad me, Bahadur Shah ko, 1537 me, Ship par bulake, uske gotte kaat diye. 
  			 
  			 Expansion in Bengal with Hoogly as HQ.
  

			
		Mangalore, Cannanore, Coachin and Calicut---> Ye sab inke trading ports the 
		
		East coast me : San Thome(Chennai)  and   Nagapatnam(Andhra) 
		
		
		
		1579 :---> Akbar ne Goa me letter bheja, 2 learned priests ke liye.---> Father Rodalfo Aquaviva  and   Antonio Moserrate
		
		1592 me 2nd mission
		
		1595 me 3rd mission  at Lahore. ---> Father Jerome Xavier  and  Emanuel Pinheiro
		
		
				Vedor-Da-Fazenda--> Revenue + Cargo officer.
		
				Forts were under Captains assisted by Factors.

				Introduced the system of Drilling groups of Infantry (on the spanish model)---> 1630s. ---> Ise pehle French ne adopt kiya, fir Brits ne, Fir Marathas and Sikhs



		1608 : Capt William Hawkins with his ship Hector, reached Surat. ----> brought letter from James I, King of England. ---> To mughal court of Jahangir. 
		
			- 1609 me, ye jahangir se mila, Even though Father Pinheiro ne bohot koshih ki rokne ki. 
			
			- Jahangir cutiye, ne Hawking ko Mansab bhi diya, Salary bhi di, or Mubarak Shah ki beti bhi de di. 
			
			- Lekin, Portuguese ne, Brits ko, Surat port me enter karne se rok diya. 
			
			- 1611 me Hawkins gussa hoke, court chor ke bhaag gaya. 
		
		
		1612 : Captain Best (Ship-Dragon) ne, Portuguese ki maa chod di----> Jahangir is se kafi impress ho gaya. ---> Lost Surat to the Brits. 
		
		
		1613 : Portuguese chutiye, Mughal ships agwa karne lage. 
		
		
		1632 : Mughals ne, portuguese se, Hoogly capture kar liya, jo inhe, 1579 me ek farman se mila tha.  In saalo ne, Mumtaz Mahal ki 2 ladkio ko randi bana liya tha. 


		1663 : Malabar Coast ke saare Portuguese coast, par Dutch logo ne kabja kar liya. 

		
		1683 : Marathas Invaded Goa
			
						
		1739 : Marathas ne, Salsette and bassein capture kar liya inse. 



	

	 Dutch
	 
	 	1596 : 1st Dutchman to reach Sumatra---> Cornelis De Houtman
   		
   		1602- East India Company of Netherlands.
   
   		1605- 1st factory in Masulipatnam in Andhra. Captured Nagapatnam(from portuguese), near chennai and made it their main stronghold in South India.
   		
   		
   		1609 : Factory in Pulicat(north madras).    
   		
   		1616 : Surat
   		
   		1645 : Karaikal 
   		
   		1653 : Chinsura
   		
   		1658 : Kasimbazar, Patna, Balasore, nagapatam
   		
   		1663 : Cochin
  
	  		 Carried Indigo maufactured in Yamuna valley, Silk from Bengal Gujrat and Coromandal, Saltpeter from bihar and Opium-Rice from ganga valley.
	   
	   		Monopolised trade in Black pepper and spices. 
		   
   		1667- Got ass kicked by British and left India.
 
 
 		1759 : Battle of Hoogly me Brits se haare. ---> Crushing blow to their ambitions in India. 




	English

		   1580 : Francis Drake---> Voyage around the world
		   
		   1558 : English victory over the Spanish Armada
		
			
		   1600- Queen Elizabeth I gave charter to EIC to carry trade in Indies for 15 years.( In 1609 extended to infinte years ).

		   1609- Captain Hawkings arrived in Court of Jahangir. But portuguese cock-blocked his ambitions to setup surat factory.

		   1611- Masulipatnam me trade karna shuru kar diya tha. 1616 me waha Factory banai. 	

		   1612- Thomas Best defeated portuguese at sea and got permission to establish factory at surat.(1613)(Under Thomas Aldworth)

		   1615- Thomas Roe came to court of Jahangir as Ambassador of King James I. Got permission to setup factories at Ahmadabad, Agra and Broach.

		   1632- "Golden Farman" by Golconda Sultan allowed them to trade duty free in Golconda for some Annual payment.

		   1639- Francis Day got permission from ruler of Chandragiri to built factory at Madras-- Later became fort st. George which replaced HQ from Masulipatnam for South. 

		   1651- Shah Suja of Bengal allowed them to trade for annual payment. Started factory at hoogly, Kasimbazar, patna and Rajmahal. 	 

		   1662- Britain Got Bombay from Portugal as Dowry. Given to EIC in 1668. In 1687, it was made the HQ for western Presidency(earlier-Surat).( Made peace with Portuguese ).

		   1686- Hooghly was sacked by Shaista Khan. 

		   1690- Job Charnok ne negotiatiion karke, Sutanuti naam ki jagah par lot aane ke liye raai kar liya. 
		   
		   1696- Zamindar revolt ke bahane, Settlement ko fortify kar liya. 

		   1698- Bought zamindari of 3 villages in Bengal- Sutanuti, Gobindpur and Kalikata. Fortified the settlement and named it Fort Williams(1700).
		   		 
		   		 Became seat of east, Sir Charles Eyre as it's 1st president.
   
   		   1715- Farukhsiyar issued 3 farmans- "Magna Carta of Company" ---> English Mission led by John Surman---> Bengal, Gujrat and Hyderabad. 
   		   
   		   	i) Duty free Import and Export in Bengal, duty free trade in Surat and Hyderabad for some annual payment 
   		   	
   		   	ii)Issue dastak(passes) for transportation of goods. 
   		   	
   		   	iii) Mint coins in Bombay, allowed in all mughal India.
   		   	
   		   	iv) Duty free trade in Hyderabad and Surat. 
   		   	
   		   	
   		   1708 : Whigs wali company EIC me merge ho gayi---> 'United Company of Merchants of Enland Trading to the East Indies'. ---> In saalo ne hi fir, Brit Political power Establish ki India me. 	
   		   	
   
   
   
   
 	 French
 
 		  Last to come to India. 1664- French east India company was started. (King- Louis 14th). 50 year monopoly to trade in Indian and Pacific oceans. 
 		
 		  1st factory in Surat in 1667 by Caron. 1669- In masulipatnam. 1673- Setup a township in Chandranagar near Calcutta. 
 		
 		  1674- Pondicherry was founded by Francois Martin, who replaced caron as the Governor. 
 		
 		  Mahe, karaikal, Kasim-Bazar and balasore--other coastal factories. 
 		
 		  1693- Dutch(Allied with Brits) captured pondicherry. Got it back after Treaty of Ryswick. Power grew for the next 30-40 years then :-
   
 		  Spanish war of succession ki wajah se French chodo ko apne, Surat, Masulipatnam and Bantam wale factories ko abandon karna pada tha. 
 		  
 		  Dec 31, 1706 ko Martin bhaiya, expire ho gaye. 
 
 
 
      Anglo-French Struggles:-
    
    1st carnatic war--> Due to Austrian war of succession. French seized Madras. Concluded in 1748 with Treaty of Aix-La-Chapple, Brits got back Madras. French got back their North American Territories. 
     (1740 - 1748) 
    (A small French army deafeated a Britsh ally(Indian guy) with much bigger army in Battle of st.Thome).
	
     Dupleix invloved tha isme, or 2nd wale me bhi.     
    	
    	- Ye bhai pehla European tha, to get involved in the randi-rona of Indians. 
    	
    	- Ise, 2nd Carnatic war ke baad, wapas bula liya gaya tha. He was replaced by Godeheu who was a faggot.

    2nd Carnatic war--> French Supported -  Muzaffar jang(GranSon of Nawab)          Chanada Sahib(Son-in-law of Old Nawab of Carnatic)  	 	--> Ye dono appoint hone chahte the. 
     (1749 to 1754)           
                        Brits  Supported -  Nasir Jang(Son of Nizam)            	 Anwar-Ud-din(Appointed as new Nawab)				--> In dono ko appoint kiya gaya tha. 
    
    		- Muzaffar + Chanda + French ne,  Battle of Ambur (1749) me, Anwar-ud-din ke tote uda diye. 
    			
    			Muzaffar bhai, Deccan ke Subahdar ban gaye or inhone, Dupleix sir ko, Krishna River ke south ki sari territories ka Governor appoint kar diya. 
    			
    		- Clive ko gussa aa gaya and he captured Arcot.  Chanda sahib ki gardan utar di, kisi or mulle ne.  Marathas came to the rescue of Clive and Stinger Lawrence.
    
    	       - Brits won under Robert Clive, French lost under Dupleix. French and British agreed not to interfere in quarrels of native princes. 
   
      (Dependency of Indian rulers on foreigners, they became clients of foreigners ).

    
    
    
    3rd Carnatic War--> Due to 7 years war in Europe.
     (1758 to 1763)
     
    Count-de-lally captured Fort.St David in 1758. Battle of Wandiwash--> 1760, Sir Eyre Coote defeated Lally. Treaty of Peace of Paris in 1763 restored French possessions, but their political influence was gone.





	 Danes

		   Danish East India Company was started in 1616; Founded a factory neat Tanjore, Their principle settlement was near Calcutta(Serampur) . Better known for their missionary activities. 





													BRITISH CONQUEST OF INDIA
 
   Aurangzeb's Successors--> 

     i) Bahadur Shah I(1709-12)--"Shah-e-Bekhabar". Banda Bahadur attacked during his reign. Released, Shahu the Maratha Prince. 
     
     ii)Jahandar Shah for 1 year after him. He introduced the "Izara" system to improve financial conditions of empire.  Abolished Jiz-oh-yeahhh. 
     
     iii)Farrukhsiyar(1713-19)--Sayyid Brothers(The king makers). Abolished Jizya and Piligrimage taxes. Farman to Brits in 1717. He was killed by his nobles.
     
     iv) Muhammad Shah Rangeela Beti****(1719-48)-- Killed Sayyid Brothers with help of Nizam-ul-mulk(in 1724 he became the vizir and founded the independent state of Hyderabad ). Nadir Shah fucked him.
     
     v)Ahmad Shah(1748-54)--Chutiya who let Uddham Bai( Queen Mother ) to rule.
     
     vi)Alamgir II(1754-58)--Battle of Plassey was fought. 
     
     vii)Shah Alam II(1759-1806)--3rd BOP + Battle of Buxar. Treaty of Allahbad- Came under British protection. Gave them Diwani of BBO.    
     
     viii)Akbar II(1806-37)-- Title of Raja to Rammohan Roy. In 1835 coins bearing mughal name were stopped.
     
     ix)Bahadur Shah II--Sent to rangoon where he died in 1862. 


 Independent States of Hyderabad, Awadh, Bengal and Punjab came up during the reign of Muhammad Shah Rangeela Beti****( 1719-48). 

 Nadir Shah attacked in 1738.
 
  Ahmad Shah Abdali came between 1748-67. In 1761, defeated Marathas in 3rd BOP.

 	Items of Imports : 
 	
 		1) Persian Gulf Region : Pearls,  Raw Silk,  Wool,   Dates,  Dried Fruits  and  Rose Water
 		
 		2) Arabia : Coffee,  Gold,  Drugs and Honey
 		
 		3) China : Tea,  Sugar,  Porcelain  and Silk
 		
 		4) Tibet : Gold, Musk and Woollen Cloth
 		
 		5) Africa : Ivory and Drugs
 		
 		6) Europe : Woolen Cloth,  Copper, Iron, lead and Paper
 		
 	
 	Items of Exports : 
 	
 		 Cotton Textiles,   Raw Silk,  Silk fabrics,   Hardware,   Indigo,   Saltpetre,  Opium,     Rice,     Wheat,   Sugar,   Pepper   ,  Precious stones and Drugs.

		BRITISH ANNEXATIONS THROUGH DIRECT ACTION




	Anglo-Bengal


		- 60% of British imports form Aisa consisted of Goods from Bengal. 
		
		- 1630s me---> Established factories at, Balasore, Hooghly, Kasimbazar, Patna and Dacca. 
		
		- 1690s---> Calcutta was founded. 
		
		- 1756 tak, Alivardi khan ne rule kiya----> stopped paying tributes to the Mughal Emperor. 
		
		- Bhai ke marne ke baad---> Siraj-ud-daula aa gaya
		
			- Defeated Shaukat Jang or iski gardan utari
			
			- Fucked Ghasiti Begum
			
			- Mir Jafar ko dismiss karke, Mir Madan ko appoint kiya . 
			
			- Mohan Lal naam ke Kashmiri bande ko, Overall Admin lagaya---> He acted as the Prime Minister. 
		
		
		Battle of Plasey-
		
			- Misuse of Dastaks
			
			- Fortification of Calcutta
			
			- Asylum to Krishna Das
		
			 i) Mir jafar was made nawab ii) Company got zamindari of 24 prarganas. iii) Resident posted at nawab's court.
		 
         		Mir Jafar hatched a conspiracy with the Dutch at Chinsaura + Failed to pay the company---> Replaced with Mir Kasim. (Bedara me Brits ne Dutch ka Gand Mara in 1759)
	
		       In return company got--> i)Burdawan, Mindanpur and Chittagong.ii) Money for fighting in South.


	Mir Kasim : Signed a treaty with Brits in 1760, jiske baad, Mir Jafar ke badle, ise Nawab bana diya gya. 
	
				- Gave them the districts of Burdwan, Midnapur and Chittagong
				
				- Sylhet ki Chunam trade ka 50% share milega company ko. 
				
				- Mir Kasim's enemry were company's enemy. 
				
		
		He was the ables nawab, among the successors of Alivardi Khan. 
		
		- Shifted capital, from Murshidabad to Munger in Bihar. 
		
		- Reorganised bureaucracy.   And  remodelled the army		




	Battle of Buxar--> Major Hector Munro ne, 22nd October, 1764 ko, teeno ko koda kar liya, Buxar me. 
	
		i) Misuse of Dustak by company officials

		 ii) Ram Narayan of Bihar's refusal to listen to Nawab

		 iii)Mir Kasim made a Confideracy with Awadh's Sujah-Ud-daula and Mughal Shah Alam II. 
		 
	British won under Hector Munro. 
	
	Mir Jafar was made the nawab. 
	
	Treaty of Allahbad--i)Duty free trade in Bengal ii) Diwani of Bengal, Bihar and orissa iii) Rs 53 lakh annually for Nizamat of these province as well.
	
	Awadh was made a Buffer State. Shah Alam was made to reside in Allahbad under Company's protection.



	Dual Government in Bengal (1765 to 1772)
	
		- Robert Clive ne introduce kiya----> Rule of 2--> EIC and Nawab
		
		---> Both, diwani(collecting revenue) and Nizamat(Police and Judicial functions)--> Came under, Brits. 
		
		- Exercised diwani rights as the diwan  and   Nizamat rights as its rights to nominate the deputy subahdar. 
		
		- Acquired, Diwani functions from the emperor and  Nizamat functions from the subahdar of Bengal. 
		
		- Company ne 2 dy Diwans appoint kiye---> Mohammad Reza Khan for Bengal     and      Raja Sitab Roy for Bihar. 




  Anglo-Mysore 


	1565 : Battle of Talikota
	
	1612 : Mysore me aaya, Wodeyars ka kingdom
	
		- Chikka Krishnaraj Wodeyar II ruled from 1734 to 1766. 


	
	1761 : Hyder Ali became de-facto ruler 
	
		- Haidar Ali setup an Arms factory in Dindigul(TN) with French help. He was defeated by Marathas in 1764,66,71 under Madhavrao. 
	
	
	
    1st AM war(1767-69) -->
	 
	 - British+Nizam+Maraths vs Haider over Arcot.
	  
	 - Haider reached Madras and signed the TREATY OF MADRAS.
	  
	 - British promised to protect Mysore if Attacked by someone else. No gain,No loss.
	 
	 
   2nd AM war(1780-84)--> 
	
	Marathas attacked and Brits dindu Nuffin + French(through their base in MAHE) were siding with Haider + AMerican War of Independence bhi shuru ho chuka tha. 
	
	So British tried to capture Mahe(French clay on Malabar coast). Haider suffered Defeat at Porto-Novo.
	
	He died of cancer(Gutka-Mukesh RiP). 
	 
	Tipu signed a TREATY OF MANGALORE. No gain, No loss.


   3rd AM war(1790-92)--> 
	
	Tipu attacked Travancore on April 1790. ---> Kyunki, Travancore ne, Dutch logo se, Jalkottal and Cannanore khareed liya tha, in the Coachin state (Jo Tipu ke under tha) 
	
	Brits under cornwallis won and TREATY OF SERINGAPATNAM was signed--i)Half of Tipu's territory gone ii) Rs 3 crore war damage.


   4th AM war(1799)--> 
	
	Tipu was accussed by Wellesly to conspire against Brits, Marathas and Nizam. 
	
	Tipu got REKT in 18 days.

	      - Inhone, Colonel kate Upton ko bheja, apne bobe dikha ke, nayi treaty sign karne ke liye---> Treaty of Purandhar, 1776.

	      - Mahadji Scindia defeated British and Treaty of Wadgaon was signed in 1779---> 1775 ke baad jo bhi territories acquire ki thi wo wapas karo. 
	      
	      - But Hastings defeated Marathas and signed Treaty of Salbai in 1782- Brits got salsette, Marathas got Bassein.

    Anglo-Sindh


	- 1758 me, Brits ne, Thatta me ek Factory banai. ---> Ghulam Shah ki marji se. Ye Kallora Raja hua karte the.   
	
	- 1761 me, Ghulam Shah ne Brit resident ke saath kuch agreements bhi kiye. TO not allow foreigners etc. 
	
	- Lekin, 1775 me, Sarfraz Khan ne Factory ban karwadi. 
	
	- Iske baad, Balochi, Talpuras aa gaye or unhone, Kallora logo ki gaand par laat maar ke unhe bhaga diya. 

	- Sindh in 1780s ruled by 4-yaar Talpuras( Baluchis ). British feared Invasion by Napoleon( joint invasion plan with Alexander I of Russia in 1808). Envoys were sent to Kabul, lahore and Tehran.
	
	- Nicholas Smith ne, Treaty sign karli --> i) No Frenchies in Sindh    
	
	- Treaty of 1832-- Bentnick-Amirs. i)Free passage on Indus ii) No English merchant would settle in Sindh .

	- Lord Auckland in 1836 feared Russain Invasion +  wanted Influence over Afghanistan. Signed treaty with Amirs( feared Ranjit singh). Sindh turned into British protectorate. Resident was placed. 
s
	- Tripartie Treaty in 1838 among Brits, Sikhs and Shah-Suja(wannabe Afghan Ruler) under which SS was supposed to give up his soverign right over Sindh.

	- Sindh accepted Subsidiary Alliance in 1839.

	- Sindh was acquired in 1843, under GG Elenborough. Charles Napier was appointed it's governor.

	  - After ASA's raid, Sikhs organised themselves in Misls( Military Brotherhood/Democratic setup).

	  - Ranjit Singh- Sukarchakiya Misl, appointed as Governor of Lahore in 1799 by Afghan ruler Zaman Shah. In 1805 acquired Jammu and Amritsar.

	  - In 1809 he signed Treaty of Amritsar with the Brits--i)Accepted Satluj as the boundry b/w sikhs and british India.

STRUGGLE FOR INDEPENDENCE
   
    Pre-Congress Associations:- Demanded:- i) Admin reforms ii)Associations of Indian in admin iii)Spread of Education
   

   Bengal
     1)Bangbhasha Prakahika Sabha :- 1836, by Associates of RRR
     2)Zamindari Association/ Landholder's society:- 1st instance of Political activity and constitutional agitation
     3)Bengal British India Society:- 1843, To spread awareness about coditions of British India, Later merged with 2) to create British Indian Association
     4)British Indina Association:- 1851, Sent petitions to Britih Parliament :- 
                                             i)Seperate legislature
                                            ii)Seperation of Executive and judiciary
                                           iii)Reduction of salaries                             (Partially accepted in Charter act 1853)
                                            iv)Abolition of stamp duty, salt duty
       
     5)East India Association:- 1866, By Dadabhai Naoroji in london
     6)Indian League:- 1875, Sisir Kumar Ghosh to stimulate sense of Nationalism in Indians.
     7)Indian Association of Calcutta/Indian national Association:- Superceded 6), in 1876 by Surendranath banerjee and Anand Mohan Bose(Fed up with British India Association--Pro-landlord)
                i)Protested against reduction of age limit in 1877
               ii)Simultaneius holding of civil service exam and Indianisation of Admin. 
              iii)Campaign against Arms act and Vernacular press act
               iv)All India conference in 1883, Merged with INC in 1886.
     
    
   Bombay
     1)Poona Sarvajanik sabha:- 1867, MG Ranade, to act as bridge bw gov and people.

INDIAN NATIONAL CONGRESS
    1st Session:- 1885, Sanskrit College, Bombay, 72 Delgates, President=WC Bonerjee
    Presidents:- DadaBhai(3), B.Tyabji, P.Mehta, P.AnandCharlu, SN Banerjee, RC Dutt, AM BOSE, GKG
    1st women Graduate of Calcutta Uni, Kadambini Ganguly addressed the session.
    
    Objectives:- 
       i)Found a Democratic Nationalist Movement
      ii)Politicise and politically educate people
     iii)Establish HQ for movement
      iv)Promote friendly relation bw Nationalists from all part of India.
       v)Develop and promote Anti Colonial nationalist Ideology
      vi)Unify people over common economic and political programme
     vii)Develop feeling of national unity
    viii)Promote and Nurture Indian Nationhood

             i)Leaders:- Dada, Mehta, Wacha, Bonnerjee, Banerje
            ii)Believed in Liberalism, and moderate politics. Consti. Agitation within confine of law.	
           iii)British Committee of INC organised in London in 1899
            iv)Economic Critique:- Dada, Dutt, Wacha---"Drain Theory"

            vi)Didn't- Widened the base; Never included masses and women, never advocated Voting for all

        Leader Log : Dum Lagao Damodar Shah and Jagannath Dalla  Mindanpore wale.

  20) Ahom Revolt ( 1828) 
  
      
          
       Brits ne 1st Burna war ke baad apni aukat dikha di, to Ahom log kud pade, under the leadership of Gomdhar Konwar. 
       
       
         Brits, ne fir dar ke maare, upper assam ka kuch part, Maharaja Purandar Singh ko lota diya. 



  21) Surat Salt Agitation(1840s) 
  
      Pehle to Salt duty badhane par. 
      Fir, Bengal Standart Weights and measures introduce karne par. 
      
      
      Boycott and Passive resistance, koi khun kharaba nahi. 



  22) Kohlapur ke Gadkari, kud pade, 1844 me. 
  
  
  23) Wahabi Revolt  : Pehle Sikh Empire ke against, uske baad 1849 me Brits ke against. 
  
  
  24) Haye ni Tera Kuka Kuka Kuka Movement :- 1840 me Bhagat Jawahar Mal (a.k.a Sian Saheb) ke under. 
  
      Bhagat ke baad, Baba Ram singh kud pada. Ye wo Namdhari sect wala choda tha. 
  
     Inhone, Swadeshi or Non-cooperation ka naara lagaya tha sabse pehle. 
  
  
       1872 me, Raam singh ko Rangoon bhej diya gaya. 
  
  
  
  
  25) Narkelberia Uprising : Mir Nathar Ali/ Titu Mir ne Mullo ko bhadka diya, Hindu landlords ke against.   Ye baad me Wahabi se mil gaye. 
  
  26) Pagal Panthis : Bengal ke Mymensign District ke Hajong and Garo Tribes ka Semi-Religious movement. 
   
          Karam Shah naam ke bande ne start kiya. Iske baad, iske bete Tipu ne lode liye.   
          
           
           1825-1835, me oppressive zamindars ke against. 
  
  
  27) Faraizi Revolt :- Dadu Mian ne 1838-1857 tak. Ye saale bhi Wahabi se mil gaye. 
  
  
  
  
     
                    1857 revolt me peasant log, bas Western UP me active the. 
                    Revolt ke baad, inke or bhi lode lag, gaye, jab british ne Zamindaro ko support karna shuru kar diya. 
                    Ex : Bengal Rent Act, 1859 se Taluqdars ko to zameen wapas mil gayi, lekin peasants ke haath, bas unka loda aaya. 
  
  
  
  
  
  
 Mainland ke  Tribal Movements: 
  
  
     1) Pahariya's Rebellion : Raj Mahal pahadio ke Pahariyas ne 1778 me revolt kar diya. 
     
        Brits ne inke area ko Damni-kol area declare kiya, peace lane ke liye. 
  
  
  
  
    2) Chaur Uprising : Mindanpore ke Jungle mahal ke Chaur logo ne hathiyar utha liye, 1766- 1772 ke beech. or Ek baar fir, bw 1795-1816. 
  
       Durjan Singh sabse Durjan chor tha inme se, 1798 me lode liye the isne. 
  
  
          Kuch, pussy historians ko Chaur term pasand nahi hai, isliye wo ise Jungle Mahal ka revolt bhi bolte hain. 
  
  
  
    3) Kol Mutiny(1831) 
    
       Chotanagpur ke area wale.      1831 me outsiders ko land transfer kar dene ki wajah se. 
  
  
        Buddhe Bhagat ki leadership me inhone 1000 se jyada outsiders ko jala dala. 
        
        
        
    4) Ho and Munda Uprising(1820-1837) 
    
    
         Parahat ke Raja ne Ho log, ko bhadkaya, Singhbhum ki occupation ke against(Jo jharkhand me hai) 
         
        Ye wala revolt, 1827 tak chala. 
        
        Iske baad, Chotanagpur ke Mundas kud pade, revenue policy ke against. 
        
        
        
        Munda ek baar fir, 1900 ke aaspaas, Birsa Munda ke under josh me aa gaye. 
        
        Is period ko Ulgulan kehte hain. 1860-1920 ke beech. 
        
         Money lenders, zamindars, forest contractors, Doraemon, BC kisi ko nahi chora, sab khu-khaan kar ta.     
  
  
  
  
   5) Santhal Rebellion (1855-56) 
   
   Bihar ke Rajmahal Hills par rehne wale, Santhals, Zamindars ke against kud pade.   Baad me ye Anti-british ban gaya, jab ye zamindars or moneylenders Police bula laye. 
  
  
     Sidhu and Kanhu, 2 bhaio ke under Autonomous zone bana liya inhone, Bhagalpur or Rajmahal ke beech ke area me. 
     
     1856 me inki maa chud gayi. 
  
  
  
  6) Khond Uprising(1837-56) 
  
  
      Odisha se Vishakhapatnam tak hilly areas me rehte hain khond log. 
      
      Chakra Bisoi ne inhe chutiya banakar, Human Sacrifice ke roke jaane par, British se lode le liye. 
  
  
  
  7) Koya Revolt
  
      Andhra ke Eastern Godavari Tracts me rehne wale Koya, 1879 me Tomma Sora ke under kud pade.       Tomm Sara Koya log, Hamesha hamari juuti ke neech....

     3) Prarthana Samaj
     
         1867 me, Peshab se milne uska ek dost aaya mumbai se-> Atamaram Pandulund.    Atmaram Pandulund, ko bhi bengalan babes ka paseena nikalna achaa lagta tha.

             Ye sunke, Pandu ki aankho se aansu aa gaye, or use Prarthna ki ki uska naya kotha kamyab hoga. Or isi liye, usne naye kothe ka naam, Prarthana Samaj rakha. 
         
         
         Justice Goving Mahalund Randwe ek din wakalat karke wapas ghar ja raha tha, tab Prarthana Samaj ke aage se nikalte hue use andar se Jor Jor se kisi ladki ke 'prarthana' karne ki awaaze suni. Usne andar jhank ke dekha to uske hosh ud gaye-- Andar,  Pandu, Peshab ki jawan ladki ki chut maar raha tha.  Justice ka ye dekh ke khada ho gaya, wo khud ko rok nahi paya or wahi pant khol ke muth marne laga. Justice ko is baat ka khayal hi nahi aaya , ki pandu ke 2 sipahi, uski ye gandi harkat dekh rahe hain.  Wo, justice ko pakad kar, Pandu ke paas le gaye. 
         
          Tab tak, pandu jhad chuka tha, or Peshab ki ladki behosh ho chuki thi.  Pandu, justice ko dekh ke bolta, " Dekho Justice, Galti to tumne ki hai. Lekin main tumhe maaf kar sakta hu ek shart pe, ki tum bhi Prarthana Sabha join karoge".   Ye sunke Justice ke chehre pe khushi ki lahar dod gayi or wo bola, "Jusitce Govind Mahalund Randwe".
          
          
           Justice apne saath apne kai tharki dost le aaya : i) R. G. Bhand hokar chodne wala
                                                            ii) N. G. Chada ikatha kar kar chodne wala
                                                          
                                                          
           Justice ne Prarthana kar karke, Samaj ko Pure India me famous kar diya.        Samaj me Lund Bhakti hone lagi.

                     Initial Stages me Congress ke objectives the :
                     
                        1) Ek Democratic Nationalistic Movement tayyar karna (Basically, ye pressure group, jo power hadapna chahta hai British logo se)
                        2) Jiske baad, logo ko politicise or politically educate kiya jayega (Basically propaganda failana)
                        3) Movement ke liye HQ tayyar karna
                        4) Pure desh ke baaki 'Shyaane' Nationalist leaders ke saath freindly relations banana,(Basically, khareed lena unhe Rande supply karke).
                        5) Anti-colonial nationalist ideology develop or propagate karne ( Basically, Propaganda material tayyar karna))
                        6) Logo ko common economic or political programme ke unify karne ke liye, Governemtn ke saamne popular demands rakhna (Matlab, logo ko slogans/placards pakadwake, police ke dandde khane ke liye lagana)
                        7) Religion, Caste or Province se upar uthkar, ek National Unity ki feeling ko consolidate karna ( basically, tarah tarah ke chutiyo ko sapne deekha ke apne peeche laga lena)
                        8) Indian Nationhood ko bade dhyan se Promote or nurture karna
                        
                        
                        
                        
                        
              Ye sab kaan karna jin log ne shuru kiya wo Moderate leaders the, or ye sab drama chala kuch 20 saalo tak (1885-1905) 
              
                 Isme Important moderate leaders the :- Dadabhai Naoroji, P. Mehta, D.E Watchout, Mota Bonnerjea, Fuddu begalee
                 
                    -Ye chode, 'liberalism' or 'moderate' politics me yakeen rakhte the. 
                    
                    - Ye kaanun ke dayre me rehte hue, Constitutional agitations kiya karte the.    Is se slow but orderly political progress hoti thi. 
                    
                    - In chutiyo ko lagta tha ki British log just hain, bas unhe apni needs ke baare me samjhana hai
                    
                    - GoI tak, Resolutions, Petitions, Meetings etc ke through apni public demands present karni hai. 
                    
                    - Ye sab karne ki inki 2 pronged strategy thi - i) Strong public opinion banao, national spirit ko arouse karne ke liye, taki log common political questions par ek ho sake
                     
                                                                    2) Nationalists jo line dikha rahe hain, us par chalne ke liye, fir British gov ko persuade karo, taki wo reforms introduce kar sake
                                                                    
                                          - Iske liye wo ' Prayers' and 'Petitions' karte the, or wo fail ho jane pe , 'Constitutional Agitation' kiya karte the. 
                                          
                                          
                    - 1889 me in log ne, London me, INC ki ek British committee bhi banayi thi. Dadabhai, ye sab kaam me aage rehte the.                                                                           
                                                       
                                                       
                    - Inhe lagta tha, ki Brits ke saath political connections Indians ke fayde me hai, or agar is stage pe directly challenge kiya to Britis hamari maa chod denge. 
                    
                    
                    
                    
                                                       
                                                       
         Kya kya Ukhaad paye ye log : 
         
         
             1) Dadabhai, RC Dutta, Dinshaw Watchout, ne "Drain threory" tayyar ki.
             
                  All-India public opinion tayyar kiya ki, BC India ki poverty or economic backwardness ka main cause, British rule hai. 
                  
                  
             2) Ye bolte ki, India ki development ke liye, Indian Capital or Enterprise ko promote karna chahiye. 
             
             3) Inhone, Land Revenue me reduction demand ki,   Salt tax ko abolish karne ko bola,      Plantation labour ki condition improve karne ko bola,     Military expenss kam karo,    Moden Industries ko encourage karo
             
             
             4) Imperial Legislative council me reform ki baat ki : 
             
                      a) Indians ki jyada participation ke liye, councils ko expand karo
                      
                      b) Councils ko jyada power do, takki, especially finance jaisi cheezo par jyada control ho sake. 
                      
             5) Inka long term goal tha---> Democratic self government
             
             
             6) Inke kaafi saari demands man li gayi, through 1892 ke Indian councils act se. 
             
             
             7) Lekin, is se Inka pet nahi bhara to ye demand karne lage : 
             
                   a) Elected Indians ki majority ho 
                   
                   b) Budget par contol ho
                           (vote karne ki, or amend karne ki power ho) 
                                
                  "No Taxation, without representation" ka naara de daaala BC.
                  
                  
                  
            8) Iske baad to ye saale, apni aukat se badhkar Self governemt on the lines of Self-governing colonies of Canada and australia, jaisi baate karne lage. 
             
                i) Dadabhai Naoriji ne 1905 me
               ii) GKG ne 1905 me
              iii) Tilak ne 1906 me
              
              
             
           9) In councils me khade hokar, leaders ne jo policies ki dhajjiyaan udai hain, use se i) Unka Political stature badha  ii) National movement build hua   iii) Imperialist rule ka moral influence kam hua
           
           
        
        
        
              Government ki INC se gaand fati to usne, Syed Ahmed Khan or Raja Shiv Prasad of Benras jaise tatto ko ikatha kar, United Idnian Patriotic Association banaya, Congress ke propaganda ko counter karne ke liye. 
        
        
        
        
        
        1905 aate aate, Militant Nationalism ka ubhar hone laga. 
        
            Reasons :     1) British demands par muth maar ke lota dete the, to Logo ka british rule ka true nature samajh aane laga. 
        
         
                          2) 1896 or 1900 ke beech famines me 90 lakh log mar gaye. 
                          
                          3) Deccan me Bubonic plague aa gaya. 
                          
                          4) Rights dene ki jagah, GOvernment to rights cheen rahi thi : 
                          
                                    i) 1892 me Indian council act, jo satisfy nahi kar paya
                                    
                                   ii) 1897 me Natu brothers ko bina trial ke hi deport kar diya gaya, or Tilak par Sedition ka aarop laga, jail me fek diya gaya
                                   
                                  iii) 1898 me IPC Section 124A ke under repression ki aandhi layi gayi
                                  
                                   iv) 1899 me Calcutte coroporation me Indian members ka number kam kar diya gaya.                [Lord Curzon Badelundwale, 1899 me aaya tha] 
                                   
                                    v) 1904 me Officials secrets act, se freedom of press curb hui
                                    
                                   vi) 1904 me Indian Universities act, se GoI ka Unviersitites par control badh gaya 
                            
                            
                         5) British, ab mass or techinal education ke spread ko bhi supress karne lage the. 
                         
                         6) Tialk, Aurobindo or Bipan Chandra Pal, Logo ko self-respect sikha rahe the, or masses ko involve karne ki baate kar rahe the. 
                         
                         7) Education ke spread se, ek taraf to awareness badhi, lekin saath saath Padhe likhe, berojgaars ka number bhi badha. 
                         
                         8) Japan ne 1868 ke baad jo progress ki thi, use dekh sab bhayanak prabhavit ho gaye. 
                         
                         9) Ethiopians(1896) , Boer Wars(1899), Japan Vs Russia(1905), se European Master race and shieeet ka bhram khatam ho gaya. 
                         
                        10) Swami vivekananda, Bankim chadra, Swami Dayananada Saraswati jaise logo ne kai young nationalists ko inspire kiya, ancient india ki richness describe karke. 
                        
                                                              (Dayananda ne message diya- " India for Indians "
                                                              
                                                              
                       11) Younger Nationalists , Moderated ke 3Ps(prayer, petition, protes) ke method se tang aa gaye the                                       
                         
                       12) Curzon ka 7 saal ka rule, that was FUBR --> Official Secrets act, Indian Uni acts, Calcutta corporation act, Partition of Bengal 
                       
                       13) Naye Nationalist leaders :- Raj Narain Bose, AK-47 jaise lund wala Datta, Aurobindo ghosh, Bipin Chandra pal, Vishnu shastri Chiptunemaker, BGT, Lala
                         
                             Ye sab : a) Foreign rule se nafrat
                                      b) End Goal - Swaraj
                                      c) Direct political Action chahiye
                                      d) Masses ki capacity me yakeen, to challenge the authority
                                      e) Personal Sacrifice dene ke liye hardam tayyar
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                 Swadeshi Movement:
                 
                 
                    Ye start hua tha, Bengal ki partition ke against start hue, Anti-partition movement se.
                    
                    - 1903 me hi brits ne bol diya tha, ki wo saale Bengal ko partition karenge
                    - 2 provinces ban ni thi- i) West Bengal(jisme Bihar or orissa bhi honge) + ii) East Bengal
                    - Bengal, jo nerve centre tha Indian nationalism ka, use weak karne ke liye. 
                    - 2 alag alag admins me bengalis ko rakhne se unhe divide kiya ja sakta tha : 
                    
                                                                           i) Language ke basis pe : Bengalis, Bengal me hi minority ban jaate
                                                                          ii) Religion ke basis pe : West- Hindu, East- Muslim majority
                    
                    
                    - Curzon bhadwe ne, Mullo ko  bola ki Dacca, Naye muslim majority province ka naya capital ban ke ubharega
                    
                    
                       1903 se 1905 ke beech, Moderates ne chalaya Anti-Partition Campaign
                       
                       
                          - Leader the : Fuddu Begalee, K.k Mitra, Prithwishchandra ray
                          - Methods : Govt ko petitions, Public meetings, memoranda and propaganda(using pamphlets and newspapers like Hitbadi, Sanjibani and Bengalee)
                          
                          - July 1905 me GoI ne partition ka plan, sab ho halle ke bavjood pass kar diya
                          - Isi ke baad, jo shor sharaba hua, usme koi darudiya uthke bola, " BC hame, Boycott resolution pass kar dena chahiye "--> 7th August, 1905---> Calcutta Townhall ki meeting me. 
                          - Boycott of Machester Cloth and liverpool salt
                          
                          - October 16, 1906 ko partition came into force----> Ise Day of mourning ki tarah manaya gaya pure bengal me. 
                          - Log, Bande Matram gaaate hua, Ganga nadi me dub ke mar gaye. 
                          - Rabindranath Tagore, ne is liye, 'Amar Sonar Bangla' likha. 
                          - Log, Unity dikhane ke liye, ek dusre ka lund chusne lage, or jo milta use rakhi bandhne lage. 
                          - Shaam hone par, Fuddu bengalee, or Uske param mitra, Bhadwa Bose, ne kaafi badi meeting ke beech ek dusre ke lund chuse or Rs 50 K ikatthe kar,Swadeshi Bitcoins me invest kar diye
                          
                          - Ye bhadwapana, dheere dheere, pure desh me fail gaya
                          
                          - Tilak, poona and Bombay me udhham machane laga
                          - Lala or Ajit Singh, Punjab me gadar failane lage
                          - Delhi, me Syed Haider Raza naam ke mulle ne kamaan sambhali
                          - Madras me Chidambaram Pillai ne bola, " Bengal partition karke, tum ham me foot dall loge, or hum khade khade dekhte rahenge, aisa tumne sochta tha, ye mujhe pata.... " 
                          
                          
                           
                          
                          
                          Bhadwi Congress in 1905 
                          
                          
                          
                             - 1905 me Gokhale ke under INC ki meeting hui :    Benaras Session
                             
                                    a) Partition ko Condemn kiya
                                    b) Anti-Partition and Swadeshi movement of Bengal ko support kiya
                                    
                             - Lekin militant leader, chahte the ki curzon ki gand me bomb fode jayen, taki movement pure India me fail sake, or bas kapde boycott karne ki jagah, hame Swaraj ki demand karni chahiye. 
                             
                             - Lekin, Moderates ki ye sunke fat gayi. Wo coucil ke boycott ke bilkul khilaf the.   Wo bas constitutional methods se protest karna chahte the. 
                              
                            
                             
                          
                          Bhadwi Congress in 1906
                          
                            
                            - 1906 me INC ne Calcutta me session kiya, under the leadership of Dadabhai Naoroji (Extremist chahte the, Tilak ya Lala me koi bane, lekin buddhe ki respect karte the to jyada chain chain nahi ki)
                            
                               a) Decide hua ki, Congress ka goal hai--> "Self-gov/Swaraj like the UK and colonies of Australia and Canada". 
                               
                          
                            - Extremists ka ye sunke khada ho gaya. To unhone josh josh me, Swadeshi + Boycott ke baad ke nayi demand bhi rakh dali--> Passive resistance
                            
                            - PR ke under, Government schools, colleges, Gov Services, courts, legislative councils, muncis, govt titles etc, sabka boycott karega re tera tilak. 
                          
                          
                          
                                   (Moderates, ko goro ne Council reforms ki batti pakda di thi, jiski wajas se wo Calcutta session ke programmes ko tone down kar rahe the)
                                       (England me is time Liberals power me the)              
                          
                          
               
                          
                                                      
                            - Extremist log, Foreign cloth ko publically burn karne lage, Foreign made salt/sugar ka boycott karne lage, Dhobi log, pant-shirt dhona band karne lage, jis se Dhobi khaaj fail gayi, or log Haigure rak..
                            
                            - AK-47 jaise lund wale datta ne, Swadeshi Bandhab Samitis banayi, mass mobilisation karne ke liye. 
                            
                            - TN ke Tirunelveli shahar me, Chidambaram Pillai, Subramania Siva etc ne milke, Swadeshi Sangam banaya, jis se local masses inspire ho gaye. 
                          
                            - Tilak, apne Shivaji or Ganpati festivals se Swadeshi propaganda failane laga. 
                            
                            - Aurobindo Ghosh ne Bengal National College start kiya, jo Tagore ke Shantiniketan se inspired tha. (Oh My ghosh bro, why you setting up a fucking college, when all you had to do was boycott the damn..)
                            
                            - 15th August, 1906 ko National Council of Education ko bhi setup kiya gaya. 
                          
                            - Bengal Institute of Technology ko setup kiya gaya, for technical training. 
                            
                            - Swadeshi ke naam pe logo ne khoob note bhi chaape - i) Swadeshi Textile mills, soap and match fatories, tanneries, banks, insurance companies, shops, etc
                            
                                                                                 ii) V.O chidambaram pillai ne Swadeshi Steam Navigation Company khol li, at Tuticorin
                                                                                 
                            - Rabindranath Tagore, Ranjnikat Sen, Dwijendralal Ray, Mukunda Das, Syed Abu Muhammad ke songs se inspiration mili. 
                            
                                - TN me, Subramania Bharti ne Sudesha Geetham likha, jo bhayanak hit ho gaya. 
                             
                             
                             - Abanindranath Tagore ne Ajanta, Mughal or Rajputana style se inspiration leke, victorian domination ko challenge kiya. 
                             
                             - Nandalal Bose---> 1st recipient of a scholorship offered by Indian Society of Oriental art, founded in 1907. 
                             
                             - Science me, Jagdish Chandra Bose , prafullachandra ray etc ne pioneering research ki. 
                             
                             
                             -Students or Women ne participate kiya, lekin Mulle Door rahe. 
                             
                             - Subramania Siva or Chidambaram Pillai ne Tuticorin and Tirunelveli me Foreign owned mills me strike karwai. 
                             
                             
                             
                             
                             
                          1907 ka Session : When it all came crashin down : Surat-SPLIT
                          
                          
                             - Extremist chahte the, 1907 ka session, Nagpur me ho, or Tilak/Lala me se koi president bane + Swadeshi/Boycott/National Education resolution ko reiterate kiya jaye. 
                             
                             - Moderates chahte the, session Surat me ho taki Tilak na ban paye prez.  Wo Rashbehari Bose ko president chahte the +  Swadeshi/Boycott/National Education resolution ko drop karna chahte the
                             
                             
                                Aakhir kaar Moderates ki jeet hui. 
                                
                                
                         1907 se 1911 ke beech, GoI ne, 5 naye laws pass kiye, or Extrmeists ki maa chod di.        
                             
                             
                                                           i) Seditious Meetings act, 1907
                                                          ii) Indian Newspapers(Incitement to Offences) act, 1908
                                                         iii) Criminal Law Amendment act, 1908
                                                          iv) Indian Press act, 1910
                             
                             
                                       - Tilak ko 1908 me Kesari me likhe gaye apne ek article ke liye, Sedition trial bhukatna pada. 
                             
                                            Tilak was found guilt or bhai ko 6 saal ke liye Burma Jail bhej diya gaya. (BC 1000 Rs ka fine bhi laga)
                                            
                                        - B C Pal to retired hi ho gaya politics se
                                        
                                        - Lala Videsh chala gaya, sir pakka karne ke liye.      
                             
                             
                                          - Youth , Extremists ke saath thi, to moderates ki bhi maa chud gayi. 
                             
                         1911 me Partition ko annul kar diya gaya. Or Capital ko Delhi Shift kar diya gaya( Mullo ko khush karne ke liye) 
                            
                              Bihar or Orissa ko Bengal se alag kar diya gaya. Assam ko bhi ek alag province bana diya gaya.     
                             
                             
                            
                          
                          
                          
                       1909 ke Morley-Minto Reforms
                       
                             ( 1906 me Mullo ka ek group, Simla Deputation, led by agha khan, Lord Minto se mila. Inhone, Seperate electorate demand kiye, mullo ke liye )
                             
                               Nawab salimullah of Dacca ne jo 1906 me jo Muslim league banai thi, uspar, isi group ne kabja kar liya.    
                          
                          
                            Isi beech, GKG bhi England gaya, Lord Morley se milne, Jo Secy of State for India tha.
                          
                          
                          Moderates or Mullo, dono ko placate karne ke liye, Morley-Minto Reforms laye gaye, in the form of Indian Councils Act of 1909. 
                          
                          
                          
                          
                 1) Councils ki non-official Membership ke liye, Elective principle ko recognise kar liya gaya. 
                                (Indians ko allow kar diya gaya, elections me participate karne ke liye, lekin on the basis of class and community)
                 2) Mullo ke liye seperate electorates. 
                             
                 3) Imperial Legislative council or Provincial Legislative councils me Elected Members ka number increase kiya gaya. 
                             
                    a) Provinces me to Non-Official Majority de di gayi. 
                    b) Par, kyunki, Ye sale non-officials me se bhi kuch nominated the(and not elected), isliye, non-elected majority abhi bhi bani rahi.     
     
                    c) Imperial Legislative council me, total 69 me se 37 official or 32 non-official  the. 
                    d) 32 me se, 5 nominated the, or 8 seat millo ke liye reserved thi, 4 seats british capitalists ke liye or BC 2 landlords ke liye. 
                                     
                          
                          
                 4) Elected members ko indirectly elect kiya jana tha. Local bodies, electoral college ko elect karti, jo fir baad me provincial legislatures ko elect karte, or jo fir Central legislature ke members elect karte. 
                             
                 5) Legislatures ki powers ko badha diya gaya. Ab legislatues, resolutions pass kar sakte the, questions puch sakte the, supply bhi, budget ke seperate items par vote kar sakte the(Pure par nahi)
                 
                 6) Viceroy ki executive council me 1 Indian ko appoint kiya jana tha. 
                 
                              ( Satyendra Sinha was the 1st Indian to be appointed in 1909) 
                              
                              
                          
                          
                          
           Brits ke randaap se tang aakar, 1907 se 1917 ke beech ke 10 saal me khub khoon kharaba hua---> 1st Phase of Revolutionary terrorism
           
                - Russian nihlists or Irish Nationalists se prabhavit hokar, Revolutionaries kud pade, goro par prahaar karne. 
                - Direct assassinations of officials, traitors, 'Swadeshi Dacoities'. 
                - Britain ke enemies ke saath military conspiracies. 
                
                
                 -Objectives :- Rulers ke dilo me terror strike karne ke liye ,  Logo ko arouse karne ke liye,   Logo ke minds se authorities ka fear hatane ke liye. 
                 
             
                 
                 
                 
        Bengal : 
        
        
            - 1870s, tak student communities me secret societies bani hui thi, lekin wo kaafi active nahi the. 
            
            - Pehla revolutionary group organise hua, 1902 me, in Mindanpore, under Jnanendranath basu or Calcutta me under 'Promote the mitter, Jatindranath Banerjee, Barindra Kumar Ghosh etc-Jise Anushilan samiti khte the       
                          
            - 1906 me, Anushilan ke hi ek group(Baindra kumar Ghosh or Bhupendranath Dutta) ne ek Weekly 'Yugantar' shuru kiya, or kuch abortive operations conduct kiye.               
                          
            
            
            - Rashbehari Bose or Sachin Sanyal, to sabke baap nikle, inhone, Punjab, Delhi , UP jaise areas tak me secret societies bana daali. 
            
            - Hemchandra kanungo, bhai to inka bhi dada nikla, or Videsh chala gaya training lene. 
            
                          
                          
                          
            - 1907 me Yugantar ne, Sir Fuller naam ke retired judge ki gaand me ghoche diye. 
            
            - 1907 me hi BC, inhone, ek train palatne ki koshish ki jisme , LG Sir Andrew Fraser ja raha tha.               
                          
                          
            - 1908 me, Prafulla Chaki or Khudiram Bose, ne 2 british aurato ko bomb se uda diya( RiP Bitches)
            
            - Iske baad, sara anushilan group hi arrest ho gaya. ----> Inke upar ALIPORE CONSPIRACY CASE chala. 
            
               -Chitraranjan Das ne Aurobindo Ghosh ko defend kiya tha case me.  Ghosh bach nikla.
               - Barindra Ghosh, bechare ko bomb banane ke liye, latka diya gaya. 
               
               
            
            
            - 1908 me, Dacca Anushilan ne, Barrah Dacoity organise ki. ---> Under Pulian Dass ( 12 dacoity kiye hain, hamre pulian bhaiyaaa)
            
            
            
            - In sab kaand se josh me aaye, Rashbehari bose or Sachin Sanyal to BC, Viceory Hardinge ke upar Bomb feka. 
            
                - Iske baad, Delhi Conspiracy case chala--> Basant Kumar Biswas, Amir Chand or Avadh Behari---> In teeno ko latka diya gaya.( RiP Dudes) 
                - Rashbehari bhaiya, nikal gaye bach bachaake. 
                
                
            - Western Anushilan ka leader, jiske baad ban gaya Jatindranath Mukherjee a.k.a Bagha Jatin----> Ye group Jugantar ke naam se jana jaane laga. 
            
            - 1st World War ke time, Jugantar Pary ne, German Arms import karne ka plan banaya, Under Zimmerman Plan. 
            - Lekin, kisi suar ne plot leak kar diya, or Bagha jatin, Balasor(1915) me Guns ki delivery lete time, Police Encounter me shaheed ho gaye. (RiP Sweet Prince)
            
                "We shall die to awken the Nation"- Bagha Jatin
                
                
            - Bengal me revolutionary acitivity ko promote karne wale newspaper the, Sandhya or Yugantar
            - Maharashtra me kaal. 
            
            
            
            
            
       Maharashtra : 
       
           - Sabse pehla kaand kiya tha, Vasudev Balwant Phadke ne , 1879 me. Ye itna jor se phadke ki, Ramosi peasant Force bankar tayyar ho gayi---> Inka plan tha armed revolt karne ka. 
           
           - 1890s me, Tilak ne Militant Nationalism propagate kiya, through Ganapat and Shivaji Festivals and his journals, Kesari and Maharatta. 
           
           - Tilak ke 2 saathiyo---> Chaplekar bhais--> Damodar and Balakrishna ne Poona ke Raand naam ke Plague Commissioner ki Gaand maar di, in 1897. 
           
           
           
           - Savarkar or unke bhai ne Mitra Mela organise kiya--> Ye ek secret society thi, in 1899---> Ye baad me Abhinav Bharat ke saath merge ho gayi in 1904. 
           
           - Iske baad, Nasik, Poona or Bombay---> Emerge hue, as centres of Bomb Manufacture. 
           
           
           - Abhinav bharat ke ek member, Anant Lakshman Kanhere ne A.M.T Jackson naam ke Indologist, ko India ke baar me gyan de diya, uski gand maar ke. 
           
           
           
           
           
           
           
           
      Punjab
      
          - Lala ne 'Punjabee' start kiya, or Bhagat singh ke uncle, Ajit singh ne Anjuman-i-Mohisban-i-watan naam ki extremist organisation start ki, in LaWhore. 
          
          - Agar haider, Syed Haider Raza, Bhai Parmanand, or radical Urdu poet, Lalchand Falak, bhi aise hi leaders the. 
          
          - Lekin, 1907 me hi Government ne Extremeism ki hawa nikaal di. 
          
          - Political meetings par ban lag gaya or Lala and Ajit ko deport kar diya gaya.      
           
          - Iske baad, Ambarprasad, Lalchand, Bhai Parmanand, Lala Hardyal etc, Full-scale Revolutionaries ban gaye. 
          
           
           
          1st World War ke time, Rashbehari Bose Ghadr Revolution ka leading figue tha. 
           
              1913, me Bose or Jatin mile, to discuss an armed uprising like 1857. 
              
             
             
             
             
             
              
           
           
           
      Abroad me kaand : 
      
      
          1) 1905 me London me Shyamji Krishnavarma ne---> Indian Home Rule league start ki, Jise India House bhi kehte the. 
          
              Indian students ke liye.   Radical youth ko scholorship par, India se bulate the. 
              
              Indian Sociologist naam ki journal chaapte the. 
              
              Savarkar or Hardyal, member the iske. 
              
              Inhi ka ek banda tha Madanlal Dhingra, jisne India Office ke babu, Curzon-Wyllie ko maar daala tha in 1909.
