      GHADR
    
         
         
             San-Fransico me ek weeky newspaper publish hota tha--> The Ghadr
         
             Is group ne US coast ke along kai branches kholi. 
             
             Mainly ex-soldiers or peasants member the, jo Punjab se aaye the USA. 
             
             Lala Hardyal, 1911 me  pahoch gaye waha. 
             
             Is se pehle, Vancouver me 'Swadesh Sevak Home' or Seattle me 'United India House' establish ho chuka tha.
             
             Finally, 1913 me Ghadr establish hua.
             
             
             Members the :- Lala  Hardyal, Ramchandra, Bhagwan Singh, Kartar Singh Saraba, Barkatullah or Bhai Permanand. 
             
             Ye India me revolt lekar aane wale the. 
             
             Inhe encouragement mile 2 events se :- i) Komagata Maru   and   ii) WWI
             
             
             Komagata wale kaand se gusse me aake, Kartar Singh Saraba or Rahubar Dayal Gupta India aa gaye.   Yaha inhone Bengalee Revolutionaries se contact kiya---> Bose or Sanyal ko lead karne ko bola. 
             
             
             21 Feb ko Lahore, Rawalpindi or Ferozepur me armed revolt hone wala tha, Plan leak ho gaya. 
             
             
             Authorities ne Defence of India Rules, 1915 ke basis pe bohot gaand mari.    45 logo ko taang diya.   Bose Japan chale gaye .      Sachin Sanyal was transported for life. 
             
             
             
             
             
             
                  
             
             
             
    Europe 
        
         
         
           1915 me, Virendranath Chattopadhyay, Bhupendranath Dutta, Lala Hardyal etc ne German Foreign Office ke saath milke, 'Zimmerman Plan' ke under, Berlin Committee for Indian Independence banai thi.  
         
            NRI Indians + POWs ko uksake, India bhejo or revolt karwao. 
         
            1 mission--> Raja Mahendra Pratap Singh, Barkatullah or Obaidullah Sindhi ke under, Kabul gaya ek 'Provincial Indian Government' establish karne----> Afghan crown prince Amanullah ki madad se. 
            
            
        Singapore ki Mutiny
        
            Feb 15, 1915 ko ---- i) Punjabi Muslim 5th Light Infantry or ii) 36th Sikh Battalion ne Mutiny kar di--> Under, Jamadar Chisti khan, Jamadar Abdul Gani and Subedar Daud Khan. 
            
                
         
         
       First World War ke baad, log thode thande ho gaye, kyunki i) Prisoners ko release kar diya gaya ii) Montagu ne reforms ki guthli pakda di iii) Gandhi ki Entry
       
       
       1st WW me, Dono Moderates or Extremists ne Brits ka support kiya. Bas bechare revolutionaries the, jo ladte rahe. 
       
       
       
                        Lekin, Revolutionaries, ke alawa ek or group bhi tha, Jisne Brits ko oppose kiya, lekin Thang se, Organize hokar----> Home Rule League Moment. 
       
                   
                                 Leaders:- Tilak, Besant, G.S. Khaparde, The Hindu wala Iyer, Joseph Baptista and Jinnah(Yes, the snake himself)
                                 
                                       Alliance on the lines of Irish Home rule league             
                                 Objective: Home Rule, within British Commonwealth
       
       
                          Lekin kyu :
                          
                              
                               i) Nationalists ke ek section ko lagta tha ki, Government se consensus lene ke liye, Popular pressure dalna padega
                               
                              ii) Moderates, dillusioned ho gaye the Morley Minto reforms se 
                              
                             iii) Wartime miseries se logo ki mari padi thi
                             
                              iv) Wartime propaganda se Muh White Superiority ki myth khatam ho gayi thi
                              
                               v) Tilak ko release kae diya gaya tha jail se.
                               
                              vi) Annie Besant
                              
                               
                              
                              
                              
                              
                              
                       1915 se hi, Annie ne, India me self-GOv demand karne ke liye, Campaign lauch kar di thi. 
                       
                       'New India' or 'Commonweal' jaise newspapers + Publi Meethings ke through + Logo ko apni gori chut dikha dikhake, Annie ne convince kar diya tha, Self Rule ki demand karne ko. 
                       
                       1915 me INC ke annual session me, Tilak or Besant ne logo ko sex karne ka naya tareeka sikhaya. Tilak ne jail me rahke annie ko yaad kar, muth se dewaare bhar di thi. 
                       
                          Tilak ke naye moves dekh, moderates ka bhi khada ho gaya, or unhone Extremists ko wapas congress me bharti kar liya. 
                          
                          Lekin, COngress walo ko Annie ki chut itne pasand nahi aayi, isliye, uunhone Home Rule League ki scheme ko approve nahi kiya. 
                          
                          Lekin, Congress walo ne, Tilak ke naye chudai ke tareeke ko pure desh me sabko sikhane ke liye, Educaticve Propaganda ka programme start kiya, or local-level congress committees revive ki. 
                          
                          
                       
                       
                       
                       Ye sunke annie ki gand me aag lag gayi, or usne ultimatum de daala ki, BC agar tumne ye commintments implemnet nahi kiya, to wo khudki league start kardegi.  
                       Or jab koi response nahi aaya Khangresi kutto se, to Annie ne waisa hi kiya. 
                       
                    Annie ki chut marne ke liye, Tilak ne bhi aise show off ke liye apni League banai. 
                    
                    
                    
                    Tilak's league : 
                    
                    
                        i) Tilak ne 1916 me Indian HRL setup ki.  Iska HQ tha, Poona ka Belagaum shahar, jaha ke log, Be lagam hokar, chudai karte the. Tilak ne unhe lund pe contol karna sikhaya, even in the presence of White hoes. 
                        
                       ii) Tilak ki league, Maharashtra, Karnataka, CP or Berar me chalti thi.  
                       
                      iii) Lekin, Tilak ko Bombay me chudai se dar lagta tha, kyunki waha parsi log, apni aurato ki choot me Dhansak daal diya karte the chudai ke baad. 
                      
                      iv)  6  branches thi, or Demands thi : Swarajya, Formation of Linguistic states and Education in Vernacular.    Tialk ki English kamjor thi, isliye wo Linguistic states ki demand kartat tha. 
                      
                      
                      
                   Annie ki league :
                   
                      i) Annie ne, September 1916 me Madras me ALL-India HRL banai, kyunki Tilak Indian HRL par copyright claims ki strike karne laga tha. 
                      
                     ii) Iska coverage pure India me tha, even in Bombay, kyunki Annie ko dhansak khana pasand tha. 
                    
                    iii) Iski 200 Branches thi, kyunki Indian logo ko Gori aurat ki baate sun ne me maja aata tha, as comapred to some boring old dude with daddy issues. 
                    
                     iv) Annie ne apne Lover, George of the Jungle, Arundalle, ko league ka secy bana diya tha, malai maar ke. 
                     
                     v) Ek din jab George, jungle me bandar pakadne gaya tha, to piche se B.W. Wadia or C.P.Ramaswamy aa gaye, or Annie ki panties sungh muth marne lage.
                      Ye dekh Annie inta prabhavit hui, ki usne, in dono mutthal logo se hi league ka saara kaam karwaya. 
                     
                     
                        
              Home Rule league ka programme
              
              
                 1) Main kaam tha, logo ko Home rule as self-gov, ke sapne dikhana. 
                 
                 2) Ye Gujrat or Sindh ke backward areas tak, ye message le gaye. 
                 
                 3) Political Education,  Reading rooms, Libraries, dicussions, conferences, Classes for students, Newspapers me propaganda, Posters,  Plays, Religious songs etc ki madad se Annie ne randaap kiya. 
                 
                 4) 1917 ka Russian Revolution bhi bilkul theek time pe hua. 
                 
                 5) Ye sab dekh ke, baaki Muthmaru log bhi annie ki chut marne aa gaye :- 
                 
                                                                             a) Bada nehru aaya bade lund ke saath
                                                                             b) Chota Nehru aaya, chote lund ke saath
                                                                             c) Bhulabhai Desai, Annie ko bhul bhulaiya me le gaya, or gum hone ke bahane, annie ki panties sungh, muth maar ke so gaya
                                                                             d) Chittaranjan Das : Chitra banata tha, annie ki nangi body ko imagine karke
                                                                             e) K.M.Munshi : Paperwork karta tha, or kehta tha," Munshi hu ladki, chut me likh dunga apna naam, K.M naam hai mera, K.M naam hai mera " 
                                                                             f) B.Chakravarti : Ye bolta tha, ki ye pure India pe kabja kar Annie ko India ki rani bana dega, agar wo chut dene ke liye tayyar ho jaye to .
                                                                             g) Saiuddin Kitchlew : Ye mulla kitchen ware supply karne aaya tha annie ko, lekin annie ko kitchen me le jaake kuch or hi dikha gaya
                                                                             h) MMM : Ye Annie ko yaga or Shastra sikhane ke bahane, uska haath pakad apni lungi me daal diya karta tha or bolt tha," Mmmmmm" 
                                                                             i) Tej Bahadur Sapru : "Tej Bahadur Shaabji, Annie Madam, luli lelo, Annie madam lulli lelo"
                                                                             j) Lala Lajpat Rai : Ye MMM ka yaar tha, jise MMM ne bataya tha, "ja wo gori aayi hai, badi murkh hai, use yoga woga bolke, lode pe baitha le"
                                                                             
                                                                             
                6) Snake Jinnah bhi member tha, or ye Bomaby division a head ban gaya tha. 
                
                7) Bohot saare khangresi or Gokhle ke servants bhi, annie ki panty se aane wali khushbu sunghe ke liye, agitation me shamil ho gaye. 
                
                8) Lekin, Anglo-Indians, Mulle or Neech jaati ke log, Annie ki chut se aane wali khushbu ko kabhi appreciate hi nahi kar paye. 
                
                
                
                
                9) Govt. ko ye bilkul pasand nahi aaya ki koi Gori Randi, apni chut ki khushboo ke nashe me Indians ko Home Rule ka paath padha rahi hai.  Isliye, they came down with severe repressions. 
                
                10) Madras me students ko political meetings attend karne par ban laga diya. 
                
                11) Tilak ko Punjab or Delhi me enter hone se mana kar diya gaya, kyunki usne Naye farm laws ki bhu burai kar di thi, or khalistan Refrendum Tonti Tonti ki T-Shirt pahan ke bhashan de diye the. 
                
                12) 1917 me Annie, B.P Wadia or George Arundalle ko arrest kar liya gaya. 
                
                13) Sir Subramaniya Aiyar itna udas ho gaya ye sunke  ki ab use annie ki chut sunghne ka moka nahi milega to usne gusse me apna Knighthood renounce kar diya, bina ye soche ki BC use kabhi knighthood mila hi nahi tha. 
                
                14) Montagu ne wo shiva ko katne wali baat boli.                                                            
                                                                                       
                        
                        
                       
                       
                       
                         Lekin 1919 tak, Home Rule leage feeka pad gaya. 
                         
                         
                   1) Koi Effective organistaion nahi thi, kyunki jyadatar log, bas annie ki chut ki khusboo sunghne aaye the. 
                   
                   2) Mulle, Chut ki smell se bhadak uth te te, isliye, 1917-18 me communal riots hue. 
                   
                   3) Moderates, Montagu's statement of August 1917 se khush ho gaye the, or Goro ne bhi annie ko jail se chor diya, to khangress bolte, ab kya kareeinn...duhh. 
                   
                   4) Sept 1918 ke baad, extremist Passive resistance ki baate karne lage thi, jise sunke Moderates ki fat gayi. 
                   
                   5) Tialk case ke chakkar me, 1918 me Abroad chala gaya. 
                   
                   6) Gandhi ke aane se logo ka dhyan divert ho gaya. 
                   
                   
                             Gandhi bhi, 1920 me annie se milne aaya. Annie ne aate hi gandhi ko apni chut sunghane ki koshis ki, lekin Gandhi ne kaha, ye kya kar rahi hai kutiya.
                              Fir Gandhi ne, Annie ki chut par Anti-Fungal powder chidak diya, jis wajas se annie ki chut se smell aani band ho gayi.
                               Iske baad gandhi ne bola, " Abse ye Rand hui meri, or abse ye league bhi meri". 
                             
                               Gandhi ne Presidentship sambhali or league ka naam change kar---> Swarajya Sabha kar diya. 
                               
                               Or ek saal ke andar andar, Gandhi ne Silicon Valley ke challak VC ki tarah, league ko INC me merge kar diya. 
                               
                               
                  To aakhir league kya kya ukhaad payi : 
                  
                  
                     1) League, ek transition tha, Congress ke deliberative and Inactive nature or Gandhi ke mass involvement wale phase ke beech ka. 
                     
                     2) Is se emphasis shift ho gaya Masses par (educated elites se) 
                     
                     3) Kattar Nationalists ki puri ek generation tayyar hui. 
                     
                     4) Gandhian style politics ke liye logo ko tayyar kiya
                     
                     5) Montford reforms of 1917 bhi Home Rule agitation se influenced the. 
                     
                     6) Tilak-Annie ke karnaame ki wajah se moderates-extremists saath aa gaye the,in Luckon 1916. Jis se congress revive ho saki,as an effective instrument of Indian Nationalism  
                     
                     7) Is movement se National Movement me ek sense of urgnecy aaya. 
                     
                     
                     
                     
                     
            1916 wala wo Lucknow session : 
            
               - Annie ne Ambika Churan khila(Ambika charan majumdar tha president), Extremists ki entry karwai.
               
               - Iske pehle, P.Mehta or GKG mar gaye the. 
               
               - Is session me League ke saath Pact bhi hua: 
               
                                a) Dono ne Government ko common demand ki
                                
                                b) Is time, league me Younger militant nationalists dominate kar rahe the---Ye increasingly anti-imperialists the. 
                                
                                c) Britain ne Turkey ki madad karne se mana kar diya tha in Balkan wars(1912-13) and It's war with Italy(1911), jis se mulle bhadak uthe the. 
                                
                                d) Bengal ki partition ko bhi 1911 me annul kar diya gaya tha. 
                                
                                e) GoI ne Aligarh me University bananae se mana kar diya tha. 
                                
                                f) Young wale, purane ALigarh school se dur jana chahte the.          
                     
                                g) 1st WW me, GoI ne inki bhi gand mari thi :-   i) Maulana Azad ki Al Hilal , or Mohammad ali ki Comrade par ban laga.  ii) Ali brothers, Hasrat Mohani jaise leaders ko jail bheja
                                
                         Pact: 
                                 i) Congress ne seperate electorates accept kar liye---> Would continue till one community demand joint electorates.  Mullo ko fixed proportions me seat bhi mil gayi thi dono levels pe.        
                     
                     
                     
                     
            Montagu ne 20 August 1917 ko House of Commons me statement di :- Ise August decleration of 1917 kehte hain. 
            
               
                    " Govt ki policy hai, Increasing particitpation of Indians in every branch of administration and gradual development of self-governing insists, jo aage chalke, responsible gov. in India as an integral part of British empire ban sakte" 
                    
                         
                        Iske baad se, 'Self-Gov' or Swaraj ki baat seditious nahi rahi.                                              
                       
                       Lekin, itne sab ke baad bhi INC ko to lode dikhane hi the kyunki : 
                          
                              a) Koi specific time frame nahi diya gaya tha
                              b) Akeli gov ko hi decide karna tha, nature, timing of advance , responsible gov ki taraf. Matlab, gore decide karenge, Indians ke liye, kya sahi hai kya galat. 
                       
                       
                       
                       
                       
                       
                       
             1919 se 1939 ke time ko : Era of Mass nationalism kehte hain.           
                          
                          Reason: 
                          
                                  a) 1st WW ke bbad, nationalist activity me resurgence hua, India or baaki colonies me bhi. 
                                  
                                  b) Gandhi ke aane ke baad, Indian struggle, decisively broad based popular struggle ban gayi. 
                                 
                                  c) Sab sections ki chudi padi thi, due to hardship after the war : 
                                  
                                        i) Industry : Price increase hone se, Recession aane se, Foreign investment se kaafi industries closure ya loss ke brink pe thi. 
                                        
                                       ii) Workers/Artisans: Berojgari, High price
                                       
                                      iii) Peasantry : High Taxation + Gareebi
                                      
                                       iv) Soldiers : Ye puri duniya ki sair kar aaye, or aake logo ko bhadkaya
                                       
                                        v) Educated urban classes : Berojgari + Racism 
                                        
                                 d) India ne jo contribution kiya tha, uski wajah se expectations kaafi high thi.        
                                                                    
                                 e) Ladai ke time, ek dusre par colonial powers ne khoob keechad uchala. 
                                 
                                 f) Lekin Paris Peace conference etc se clear ho gaya ki BC Ye sab milke pagal bana rahe hain randi ke bachhe. 
                                 
                      
                      
                      
                     
                     
                     
                     
                  Government of India act, 1919
                  
                  
                     1) Provinces me dyarchy introduce kar di gayi--- For the executive 
                     
                                     - Rule of 2 = Executive councillors and popular ministers.    Governor, province ka executive head tha
                                     
                                     - Subjects 2 list me devided the: 'Reserved'---> Law and order, FInance, Land Revenue, Irrigation etc 
                                                                       ' Trnasfered'-> Education, Heath, local government, Industry, agri, excise etc
                                                                       
                                                                       
                                                                       Reserved subjects ko bas governor admin. karta tha, through his executive council of bureaucrats
                                                                       
                                                                       
                                                                       Transfered subjects--> From among the elected members of legislative council. 
                                                                       
                                                                       
                                     - Ministers responsible honge, legislature ko----> resign karna hoga, agar no-confidence motion pass ho gaya to 
                                     
                                     - Executive councillors, responsible nahi the. 
                                     
                                     - Consit. Machinery ke fail hone par, Governoe, transfered subjects ka admin bhi apne under le sakta tha. 
                                     
                                     - Secy of state for India or GG, reserved subjects me lund ada sakte the, lekin transfer wale me lund adane itna aasan nahi tha. 
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                     
                       2. Provincial legislative councils ko expand kar diya gaya, or 70% members, elected hone the. 
                       
                       3) Class + communal legislature ke system ko or bhi consolidate kiya gaya. 
                       
                       4) Aurato ko vote karne ka adhikar
                       
                       5) Governor bills ko veto kar sakta tha, or ordinances issue kar sakta tha. 
                       
                       6) Legislative councils, budget ko reject kar sakti thi, lekin Governor chahta to, restore kar sakta tha. 
                       
                       7) Legislatures ko Freedom of speech de di gayi. 
                       
                       
                       
                       
                       8) Central Government me koi Responsible Government nahi aayi. 
                       
                       
                           1 Governor General hi Chief Executive authority tha
                           
                           2 Admin. ko 2 lists me divide kar diya gaya-  Central and Provincial 
                             
                                 Viceory ki 8 logo ki executive council me 3 indians hone the. 
                                 
                                 GG ke pass, provinces me reserved subjects par full contol tha
                                 
                                 GG, ordinances issue kar sakta tha, Bills ko certify ya reject kar sakta tha etc.
                                 
                                 
                      9) Central Legislature : Bicameral kar di----> Lower house ko Central Legislative assembly kehte the---> isme 145 members the---> 104 elected
                                                                     Upper house ko Council of State bolte the---------------> Isme 60 members the----> 34 elected                             
                                      
                                       Council of State ka tenure 5 saal ka tha, or bas Aadmi log allowed the. 
                                       										[uLTA bc]
                                       Central Legislative council ka tenure 3 saal tha. 
                                       
                                       
                              Legislatures, questions puch sakte the, supplies bhi, Adjournment motions pass kar sakte the, Budjet ke ek part par vote bhi kar sakte the( LoL, only 25% part par)
                              
                                     
                                     
                    10) Britain me bhi kuch changes hue is act se: 
                    
                           a) Secretary of State, ki tankhwa, British log pay karne wale the. 
                           
                        
                        
                        
                        Itna sab dene ke baad bhi, bohot kuch nahi diya tha : 
                        
                           i) Limited franchise:- 26 crore population thi, lekin electorate bas 10 lakh logo tak extend kiya gaya tha. 
                           
                          ii) Centre me legislature ka Viceory ki gandi harkato par koi contol nahi tha, VIceroy, Parliamnet me khada hokar, lund dikhata tha logo ko
                          
                         iii) Division of subjects bhi satisfactory nahi the. 
                         
                          iv) Provinces ko unki importance ke hisab se Central legislature me seats di gayi thi: Punjab- Military ; Bombay- COmmercial importance. 
                          
                           v) Provincial level pe to kya gand failaya tha: Irrigation, Finance, Police, press, justice jaise subjects bhi 'reserved' the. 
                           
                          vi) Provincial ministries ka, Finances or babu log, par sala koi contol hi nahi tha.                  
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                                                       
                COngress wale, ye 'Reform' dekh ke aag se bhar gaye.   August 1918 me ek special session organise kiya gaya, under Hasan Imam's presidency. --> "Disappointing" and "Unsatisfactory".
                
                                                                       
                                                                          
                     
     Gandhi : 
     
      
         Birth: 2 October, 1860---> Bahadur Shah zafar ke marne ke 7 saal baad. 
         
         Stayed in South africa till 1914                
                     
                     
                     South Africa me baapu ne kai kaand kiye : 
                     
                        I. Moderate Pahse of Struggle, 1894-1906
                        
                            - South Africa or Britain me authorities ko petitions/memorials bheje. 
                            - Indians ko unite karne ke liye, Natal Indian Congress setup ki, or Indian Opinion naam ka paper start kiya. 
                            
                            
                     
                      II. Passive resistance ki baari, Bitches (1906-1914)
                      
                      
                             - Passive resistance or Civil disobediance karna start kiya, jise Satyagraha ka naam diya gaya
                             
                             - Registration Certificates(1906) ke khilaf satyagraha :  Govt. ne sabko jail me fek diya, jo koi bhi khudko register nahi karwa raha tha. 
                              
                                                                                        Jab govt. ne jab jabardasti register kiya, to Gandhi etc ne milke, Registration certificates ko publicaly burn kar diya. 
                                                                                        
                                                                                        
                             - Indian migration par resitriction ke against Campaign.  
                             
                             - Poll tax and Invalidation of Indian Marriages ke against campaign. 
                             
                             - Transvaal Immigration act ke against Protest. 
                             
                             
                             
                     Gandhi ko South Africa se kya seekh mili : 
                     
                     
                         i) Masses ke pass, immense capacity thi, ek cause me participate or sacrifice karne ki, that moved them. 
                         
                        ii) Bohot saare sections ko unite kiya jaa sakta hai. 
                        
                       iii) Kai times, aise aate hain, jab leaders ko aise decions lene padte hain, jo uske supporters ke liye unpopular hote hain. 
                       
                        iv) Khudki style of leadership and politics develop kar li bhai ne.         
                             
                             
               Satyagrah:  Based on Truth and non-violence ---> Indian elements + Christian requirements of Turning the other cheek  +  Tolstoy ki philosophy---> Evil could be best countered by non-violent resistance
               
                  
                  
                                    1) Satyagrahi, kisi bhi cheej ke liye submit nahi karega, jo use galat lage-----> But hamesha, Truthful, non-violent or fearless rahega. 
                                    
                                    2) Satyagrahi, Cooperation or boycott ke principle par kaam karega
                                    
                                    3) Methods of Satyagrahi : Non-Payment of taxes, Declining honours and positions of authoriy. 
                  
                                    4) Wrong doer ke khilaf nafrat nahi karni hai. 
                                    
                                    5) Satyagrahi, kahi evil ke aage nahi jhukega, whatever the consequences
                                    
                                    6) Bas, Brave or Strong hi Satyagraha ko practice kar sakte hain. 
                                    
                                    7) Thoughts ko practice se seperate nahi kar sakte---> Matlab, ends could not justify means. 
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
                          Gandhi wapas aa gaya, 1915 me India.  Agle ek saal tak, Bharat Darshan kiya.    Is pure time, koi position nahi li, on any political matter. 
                          
                             Lekin ye Home Rule League ke khilaf tha, kyunki World War I chal raha tha. 
                             
                             Bhai ne bola, ki wo koi bhi political organisation nahi join karega, jabtak, wo organisation bhi non-violent satyagraha ke method ko accept nahi kar leti. 
                             
                             
                    
                    
             1917 or 1918 ke beech, bapu 3 struggles me involved the : CAK
             
             
                    
                    
                 Champaran: 1st Civil Disobedience : ---->        Rajkumar Shukla ke kehne par, Bihar ke Champaran chale gaye, to look into the problems of Indigo farmers. 
                 
                                                                  Euro Planters, tikathia system force karte the--> Grow Indigo on 3/20 part of total land.    
                     
                                                                  19th CE ke end me, jab Synthetic dye aa gayi, to sale planters, high rent or illegal dues demand karne lage. Or saale fix price pe kharred te the. 
                    
                    
                    
                      Bapu ke saathi : Rajendra Prasad, Mazhar-ul-Haq, Mahadeo Desai, Narhari Parekh or J.B.Kriplani
                      
                      Waha pahonchne par, Authorities ne order kiya to leave the area at once. 
                      
                      Iski order ko defy kiya and faced the punishment---> Yahi tha passive resistance/ Civil disobedience
                    
                      Iske baad Authorities ne U Turn le liya--> Allowed gandhi to make an enquiry. 
                      
                      Gov ne ek committee apoint ki , or Gandhi ko uska member bana diya. 
                      
                      
                      
                      
                      
               Ahmedabad Mill Strike(1918) : Pehli Bhookh hadtaal :   Cotton mill owners or workers ke beech ka dispute, over discontinuation of plague bonuses.                           
                    
                                                                      Gandhi ne workers ko bola, 50 nahi, 35% increase in wages demand karo. Jab demand met nahi hui, to bapu bhi bhook hadtaal par chale gaye. 
                                                                      
                                                                      
                                                                      
                    
               Kheda Satyagraha(1918) : Pehla Non-Cooperation :---> Sukhe ki wajah se crops fail ho gaye.   Revenue code ke hisab se agar yield, 1/4th of normal produce se kam the, to farmers ko remission mil sakta tha.      
                    
                                       
                                                                     Sarkar chahti thi, ki tax na dene par properties sieze kar li jaye. 
                                                                     
                                                                     Bapu ne bola tax mat dena. 
                                                                     
                                                                     
                                                                     Bapu ke saathi: Sardar patel, Narhari Parikh, Mohanlal Pandya, Ravi Shankar Vyas 
                                                                     
                                                                     
                                                                     
                                                                     
                                                                     
               Montford reforms ke just 6 mahine pahle, Brits ne Defence of India Regulations act, 1915 ka ek extension pass kiya--> Jise Aanarchial and Revolutionary Crimes  Act, kehte the .    
               
                  Lekin, Iska popular naam tha---> Rowlatt act.                                      
                                
                                
                             Ise Rowlatt commssion ki recommenation par pass giya gaya tha, jiska chairman tha Sir Sidney Rowlatt--> To Investigate seditious conspiracy of Indian people.                                         
                                                                     
                              Imperial legislative assembly ke saare Elected members ne ise oppose kiya tha, lekin wo majority me nahi the, isliye jhant nahi ukhad paye. 
                              
                              Iski wajah se---All the elected members including MMM, Mazhor Ul Haq and even the Snake Jinnah himself, resigned in protest.                                   
                                                                     
                              Bina trial ke arrest karo, or bina juries ke trials.   Bina warrant ke arrest karo, on the mere suspicion of 'treason'.                                        
                                                                     
                                                                     
                                                                     
                   WWI ke baad Indians soch rahe the, sala ab to finally kuch achaa karenge, lekin unhe badle me Rowlatt act mila. Is wajas se sabki gaand me aag lag gayi. 
                   
                   
                   Gandhi ne ise "Black act" kaha, or ALl India level pe mass protest karne ko bola. 
                   
                   
                   Lekin, protest me logo par khub lath mare gaye. Isliye, Gandhi ne Satyagraha Sabha bulwai, or Home Rule league ke logo ko bhi ikattha kar liya. Even pan-islamists tak ko bula liya gaya. 
                   
                   Form of Protest: Nation wide hartal + Fasting and Prayer + Civil disobedience against specific laws + Courting arrests and imprisonment
                   
                   
                        i) Is sab se masses ko direction mil gaya 'act' karne ke liye
                        
                       ii) Ab se Peasants, artisans or urban gareeb bhi, struggle me increasingly important role play karne wale the. 
                       
                      iii) National Movement ka orientation, puri tarah se masses ki taraf turn ho gaya. 
                      
                      
                      6th April,1919 ko satyagraha launch hona tha.  Lekin is se pehle hi, specially punjab me , log bhayanak bhadak gaye.   Punjab ka LG, aircrafts se logo par bomb fenkne laga. 
                      
                      
                      Amritsar was the worst affected, therefor Jallianwala Bagh massacre took place on 13th April, 1919. 
                   
                                                  - 2 leader (Saiuddin kitchlew and Dr Satyapal) ko arrest kar liya gaya tha.                   
                   
                            Rabindranath Tagor ne protest me apna knighthood lota diya, ye soche bina ki use to kabhi knighthood mila hi nahi tha. 
                            
                            Gandhi ne Kesar-i-Hind ka title lauta diay, ye soche bina hi ki Pure kathiawar ke kisbhi bhi insaan ko aisa koi title kabhi diya hi nahi gaya tha. 
                   
                            Jallianwala Bagh massacre ke time, Bhagat singh was only 11. 
                   
                            Uddham singh ne 1940 me, Michael O'Dwyer ko goli mari. 
                   
                   
                            Enquiry ke liye, Hunter committe bani. Ise officially, Disorders inquiry committee kahte the.  Is committee me 3 dalle bhi the :- Chimanalal ki chut lal, Harilal Setalvad
                            
                                                                                                                                                               Pandit Faggot narayan
                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                               Sardar bhi Sahibzada bhi, Sultan lulla Ahmed khan
                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                               
                                           Hunter committee ne Dyer par koi Penal ya Disciplinary action bhi nahi liya.                                                                                                                
                                                                                                                                                               
                                     Or BC proceedings start hone se pehle hi, Government ne Indemnity Act pass kar diya tha, apne officers ko protect karne ke liye.  Ise Moti ne khub criticize kiya tha.             
                   
                   
                                                                     
                                                    Lekin, And I kid you not, Golden Temple ke Clergy Arur Singh ne Dyer ko Sikh Declare kar diya tha. 
                                                    
                                                       (Or ye ek bada reason tha, ki Management of Sikh shrines ko reform karne ki demand intense ho gayi---> Is se hi Gurudwara reform movement start hua tha).

              Swarajists kya sochte the: 
              
                 1) Coucil entry apni taraf hai, or Non-Cooperation apni taraf hai.=====> Ye bas hum ek naya Front khol rahe hain. 
                 
                 2) Political vaccum ke time me, Council work se logo ka morale boost hoga. Plus, GoI apne chode, nahi thoos payegi councils me. 
                 
                 
                 3) Councils, political struggle ka ek arena ban jayegi
                 
                 
                 
                 
           No-Changers kya sochte the: 
           
              1) Parliamentary work me lag gaye, to constructive work tumhara baap karega. 
              
              2) Revolutionary zeal khatm ho jayega. 
              
              3) Political Corruption aa jayega. 
              
              4) Constructive work se hi hum, next stage ke liye prepare kar sakte hain.

           5) NCM ke sudden withdrawl se, bohot saare disillusioned ho gaye.   Ye Swarajists ke parliamentary kaam se nafrat karte the, or No-Changers me se inhe badbu aati thi. 
           
           6) War ke baad jo Trade Unionism start hua, ye sab uski energy ko harness karna chahte the, for Nationalist revolution

           8) Marxism , Socialism

                   Chittagong armoury raid( April, 1930)
                   
                     - Surya Sen bhai, Chittagong ke national school ke teacher the, or NCM me participate kiya tha. 
                     
                     - 1926 se 1928 tak jail bhi hui thi, revolutionary activities ke liiye.  LEkin jail se nikalke, firse Congress ke liye kaam karte rahe. 
                     
                     - Chittagong district congress committee ke head the.  "Humanism is a special virtue of a revolutionary". 
                     
                     - Tagore or Qazi Nazgul..sorry Nazrul Islam ki poetry pasand thi. 
                     
                     - Armed revolt organise kiya with his sathi log( Anant singh, Ganesh Ghosh, Lokenath Baul)----> Ye dikhane ke liye, ki British empire ko challenge kiya ja sakta hai. 
                     
                     - 2 armouris ko seize karne ka plan banaya + To destroy telephone and Telegraph lines + To dislocate railway link to Chittagong. 
                     
                     - 1930 ko raid hui, 65 activists ke saath---Under the banner ho Indian Republican Army- Chittagong Branch.   Sen ne national Flag bhi hoist kiya.  Or ek Provincial Revolutionary Governemt proclaim kar di. 
                     
                     - Feb, 1933 ko bhai ko arrest kar liya gaya, or 1934 ko latka diya gaya. 
                     
                     - Lekin, is raid ki wajah se sab revolutionary bhaio ko boner aa gaya.

       In sab Revolutionaries logo ki Ideology : 
       
       
          1) HRA ka jo main organ tha 'Revolutionary' or unke HRA Manifest(1925) me the ki wo, Sab tarah ke systems ke abolition chahte hain, jin se man se man ka exploitation possible hota hai. 
          
          2) HRA wale, Railways, Heavy industries ka nationalisation chahte the. 
          
          3) HRA, Peasant and labour movements tayyar karna chahti thi, taki, Organised and armed revolt ki taraf kaam kiya ja sake. 
          
          4) Lekin, end me ye log, Individual heroic actions se Mass politics ki taraf jaane lag gaye the. 
          
          5) Bismil ne bhi, bola tha ki Gun Wun chor, open movement me kaam karo. 
          
          6) Bismil ne Hindu-Mulla unity ko bhi strength karne ki baat kahi thi. 
          
          7) Bismil ne communism me faith affirm ki thi, or kaha tha ki 'Sab human beings ko equal rights hain, over the prodicts of nature'. 
          
          8) Bhagwaticharn Vohra ne 'The Philosophy of the Bomb' likhi. 
          
          9) Bhagat singh ne 1926 me Naujawan Bharat Sabha banane me madad ki thi---> youth, peasants or workers me political work karne ke liye. 
          
         10) Bhagat and Sukhdev ne LaWhore Student's union organise ki thi, Open, Legal work among students ke liye. 
         
         11) Bhagat was a full blown socialist---> Socialim = Abolition of Capitalism and Class domination. 
         
         12) Bhagat was also completely secular. 
         
         
         
         
         
      Simon Commission : 
      
      
           1) GoI act me hi ek provision thi, ki 10 saal baad, ek commission appoint kiya jayega, to study the progress of governance scheme and suggest new steps. 
           
           2) ALl-White 7 membered Indian Statutory commission setup ki gayi, in 1927 ---> Simon Commission.  (Is time Stanely Baldwin prime minister tha)
           
           3) Lekin, ise 2 saal pehle hi bana diya gaya, kyunki conservative govenment nahi chahti thi ki liberals decide kare ye sab. 
           
           4) Is se pehle, Lee Commission ki report ne bataya tha- ' Failure of Raj to recruit enough british officers'. 
            
                           Mudiman Commission ne bataya tha -----  ' Diarchy is fucked up'
                           
                           Linlithgow comission ne bataya tha----- ' Indian Agriculture me crisis hai' 
                           
                              
         
           5) Is time Secy of State the-  Lord Dickhead,...I mean Lord Birkenhead----> Ye bolta tha, ki Indian were unable to draft a consit on their own that will have the support of wide sections of Indian political opinion. 
           
           
           
               Indian Response : 
               
               
                  Indians sab goro ko dekh bhadak gaye. ----> Bunch of british fags were going to discuss India's fitness for self-government. 
                  
                     Ye principle of self-determination ka violation tha.  And it was a deliberate insult to the self-respect of Indians. 
                     
                     
                     
               Congress Response : 
               
               
                   Madras ke congress session (December 1927) undet the presidency of M.A.Ansari---> Commission ka boycott karenge bhailog, at every stage and in every form. 
                     
                      Chota nehry bhi kamyab ho gaya to get a snap resolution passed--> Declaring complete independence as the goal of Congress. 
                      
                      
                      
               Other groups ka response :
               
               
                   Boycott ka support kiya : Hindu Mahasabha ne + Snake Jinnah ke under, Mulla league ke majority faction ne.    Punjab ke unionists + South ki Justice party ne Support kiya. 
                   
                      Mulla league ke 2 session hue in 1927 :  1) Under Jinnah At calcutta---> Oppose simon    2) LaWhore under Mohammad Shafi---> Support Government    [Dichotomy of mulla Nation]
                      
                      
                   Bombay Legislative Council ne BRA ko appoint kiya, to go and work with Simon Commission. 
                   
                      Bhai Argued for : i) Universal Adult Franchise for both Mard and Nari alike. 
                         
                                       ii) Provinces me Provincial Autonomy
                                       
                                      iii) Centre me Dyrarchy
                                      
                       
                      Bahiskrit Hitkarni Sabha ke behalf pe BRA ne Submit kiya : Memorandum on the rights and safeguards, that were required for Depressed classes. 
                      
                      BRA ne bola ki- Depressed classes or Hindu Community ke beech koi link nahi hai. 
                      
                                    - Depressed classes ko distinct and independent minority ki tarah treat kiya jaye. 
                                     
                                    - Depresed classes ko baki sab minorites se jyada protection ki jarurat hia, apni educational backwardness or Gareeb conditions ki wajah se. 
                                    
                                    - Hame bhi, mulla community ke mafik, Political protection and Representation milna chahiye. 
                                    
                                    - Agar Universal Adult Franchise nahi diya gaya to 'He would campaign for seperate electorates for the depressed classes'. 
                                    
                                 
                                                                           
                      
                Public Ka response : 
                
                
                    Commission Bombay me land ki, 1928 me----> Desh bhar me hartal se swagat kiya gaya. 'Simon Go Back' was the nara. 
                    
                    
                    Ye protest wagarh se bohot saare Youth log, pehli baar politicise hue.   Youth league and conferences got a real fillip. 
                    
                    
                    Subhash Bose and Nehru emerged as leaders of this New wave of Youth and students.              
                      
                      
                    Isi chutiyaap me naye radical ideas jaise Socialism wagarh youth me fail gaye. -----> Punjab Naujawan Bharat Sabha, Workers and Peasansts pary and Hindustan Sewa Dal in Karnataka, ye sab ne kaafi kand kiye. 
                    
                    
                    
              Police Response : 
              
              
                   Police ne Sir fodne ki nayi technique develop ki. 
                   
                   Chote Nehru or G.B Pant ko lucknow me koda kiya gaya. 
                   
                   Lala ko October, 1928 me Luth mare gaye, jis se bhai ne dam tod diya. 
                   
                   
                   
                   
                   
                   
             Simon Commission ka National Movement par kya impact hua : 
             
                i) Na sirf, complete indepence ki baat ne jor padka, Lekin, Socialist lines par major Socio-Economic reforms ki baato ne bhi jor pakad liya. 
                
               ii) Congress jo tab, shaant padi thi, Ek baar fir mass movemnet karne ko tayyar ho gayi. 
               
              iii) Lord Dickhead ki baat sunkar, Indians ne khudka consti banane ka faisla kar liya. 
              
              
              
              
              
        Simon Commission ke recommendation :
        
        
           1) May 1930 me 2 volume report publish ki : 
           
                 1) Dyarchy wale chutiyaap ko abolish karo. 
                 
                 2) Provinces me Representative government establish karo, jise Autonomy mili ho.  
                 
                 3) Governor ke pass lekin, fir bhi discretionary powers honi chahiye,  Internal Security or Different Communities ko protect karne ke liye. 
                 
                 4) Provincial Legislative councils ke members ko increase karna chahiye. 
                 
                 5) Report ne Centre me Parliamentary responsibilty ko reject kar diya. 
                 
                 6) Governor General ke pass full power honi chahiye===> To appoint the members of the Cabinet. 
                 
                 7) GoI ka complete control hoga, High Court par. 
                 
                 8) Seperate electorates ache hain---> Extend karna chahiy or communities ke liye bhi, Lekin tab tak, jab tak Hindu-Mullo me tensions khatam na ho jaye. 
                 
                 9) Koi Universal Franchise nahi hogi. 
                 
                10) Federalism acha hai---> Lekin abhi nahi, baad me.   Ek consultative coucil of Greater India banayi jaye---> Jisme, British Provinces or Princly States included ho
                
                11) NWFP or Baluchistan ko local legislatures milne chahiye, or in dono ka centre me representation badhaya jana chahiye. 
                
                12) Indian army ko Indianise kiya jaye. Lekin, British forces present rehni chaiye, jabtak India got fully equipped. 
                
             
             
             
          Lekin, Jabtak ye report aayi, Iska relevance hi khatam ho chuka tha, kyunki tabtak bohot saare kaand ho chuke the. 
          
          
          
          
          
       Nehru Report: 
       
       
           Lord Dickhead ke challenge ko accept kar----> Feb, 1928 me ek All Parties Conference hui----> Ek Sub-committee appoint ki gayi, under the Chairmanship of Moti----> To draft a consti. 
           
           Committe me the : i) Tej Bahadur Saanp Ru   ii) Subhash bose   iii) M.S. Aney   iv) Mangal Singh   v) Ali Imam    vi) Shuab Qureshi    vii) G.R.Pradhan
           
           
           1928 me hi report finalise kar di gayi.   Puri Report ke recommendation unanimous the, except 1 : --> Majority ne Dominion Status favour kiya tha, lekin ek section tha jo "Complete independence" chahte tha. 
           
           
           
       Main Recommendations: 
       
       
          1) British India tak hi confined thi---> Lekin future me, Princely States ke saath link-up ho sakta hai, on a federal basis. 
          
          2) Dominion ke liye recommend kiya : 
          
                  a) Dominion Status on the lines of Self-Governing Dominions as the form of Governemnt desired by Indians. 
                  
                  b) Rejection of Seperate Electorates---> Joint, with seats reserved for Mullas at centre and provinces jinme wo minority me hain, in proportion to their population 
                  
                  c) Linguistic Provinces
                  
                  d) 19 Fundamental Rights--> Equal rights for women, Right to form unions, UAF
                  
                  e) Centre or Provinces, dono me responsible government. 
                  
                  f) Centre me Indian Parliament jisme honge, 500 member House of Representative (Elected on basis of UAF/ 5 year tenure) and 200-Member Senate (Elected by Provinces ke councils/ 7 year tenure) .   
           
                  g) Central Government to be headed by Governor General, jise British Government appoint karegi, lekin use Indian Revenues se pay kiya jayega.  
                     
                     Ye central Executive council ke advice pe kaam karega, jo Responsible hoga Parliament ke. 
                     
                  h) Provincial Councils ka 5-year tenure hoga.  Inhe Governor head karega, on the advice of Provincial Executive council 
                  
                  i) Mulle ke cultural and Religious interests ko full protection diya jayega
                  
                  j) Complete dissociation of State from Religion. 
                  
                  
                     
                     
      Mulla and Dindu Communal Response  
      
          1) Mulla League ke Delhi Proposals
          
              - 1927 me Mulla Leaders, Delhi me mile, Mulla league ke session me----> 4 propsal diye, to be incorporated into draft consti. 
              
              - In propsals ko Congress ne Madras session me accept kar liya
              
                    i) Joint electorates with Reserved seats
                    
                   ii) Central Legislative Assembly me 1/3rd representation
                   
                  iii) Punjab and Bengal me Mullo ko representation in proportion to their population 
                  
                   iv) 3 naye Mulla Majority provinces ka formation :----> Sindh    Balochistan     NWFP      
           
           
           
           
           2) Dindu Mahasabha ke Demanda
           
           
                   - Ye, naye mulla majority provinces banane ke bilkil opposed the. 
                   - Ye, mullo ko Punjab or Bengal jaise mulla majority states me reservation dene ke bhi bilkul against the. 
                   - Ye ek strict unitary structure chahte the. 
                   
                   
                   
                   Inke is attitude se matters complicate ho gaye. 
                   
                                      
                  
           
           3) Compromise : 
           
                Moti Dilema me pad gaya :-     Mullo ki communal demand mani to Dindu apna support hata denge and vice-versa
                
                 Concession : i) Joint electorates, lekin Mullo ke liye seats reserved only where they are in minority. 
                 
                             ii) Sindh ko Bombay se detach kar denge, lekin only after dominion status has been granted---> Or wo bhi tab, jab Sindh ki Hindu minority approve kare. 
                             
                           
                           
           4) Snake Jinnah ke proposed amendments : 
           
             
                    1928, me Calcutta ki All Parties conference to Consider Nehru Report me Snake Jinnah ne Mulla League ke behalf par 3 amendments propose ki :
                    
                          i) Central Legislature me mullo ka 1/3rd representation
                          
                         ii) Bengal or Punjab legislature me mullo ko, unki populaiton ke proportion me reservation, jab tak Universal Sufferage establish na ho jaye. 
                         
                        iii) Residual powers, Provinces ke pass ho.
                        
                        
                    Lekin, Ye demans, accomodate nahi ki gayin. 
                    
                    
                    
          5)  Snake Jinnah ke 14 points. 
          
          
               Snake Jinnah, naraj hokar, dobara Mulla league ke Shafi Faction ke under chala gaya, or March 1929 me 14 points lekar aaya : 
               
                  1) Federal consti, jisme residual powers provinces ke pass hongi.
                  
                  2) Provincial autonomy
                  
                  3) Indian Federation me States, ki concurrence ke bina, Centre koi consti amendments nahi kar sakta
                  
                  4) Sab legislatures or elected bodies me me Mullo ke liye, adequate representation
                  
                  5) Services or Self-Governing bodies me bhi mullo ko representation
                  
                  6) Central Legislature me mullo ko 1/3rd Representation
                  
                  7) Centre or Provinces ki Cabinets me 1/3rd Mulle. 
                  
                  8) Seperate Electorates
                  
                  9) Koi bhi bill/Resolution tab tak pass nahi hoga, jabtak, Minority community ke 3/4th log, us bill ko apne interests ke against samjhe. 
                  
                 10) Koi bhi territorial redistribution se Punjab, Bengal or NWFP me Mulla Majority samapt nahi ki jayegi. 
                 
                 11) Bombay se Sindh ko seperate kiya jayega
                 
                 12) NWFP or Baluchistan me Consti reforms. 
                 
                 13) Sab communities ko full religious freedom 
                 
                 14) Religion, Culture, education or language rights of Mullas ko protection
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
         Matlab, Saala Nehru Report se sab gusse me the : Mulle, Dindu, Sikh or even Younger Nationalists bhi
         
                 Nehru or Bose to itna gussa ho gaye ki, Independence for India League setup kar li. 
                 
                 
                         
                 
                 
                 
                 
      Civil Disobedience Movement and Round Table Conferences
      
      
         Congress ke Calcutta Session : 1928---> Nehru Report ko approve kiya gaya.    Lekin Bose, Nehru or Satyamurthy Purna Swaraj ki baate kar rahe the. 
         
         
             - Moti or Gandhi, lekin Dominion se hi khush the. 
             - Ye chahte the ki 2 saal ka grace period diya jana chahiye GoI ko, Dominion status ki demand accept karne ke liye. 
             - Lekin, jab Bose/Chote Nehru ne nas kaatne ki dhamki di, to ye period ek saal kar diya gaya. 
             
           Congress ne decide kiya ki, Ki saala ek saal ke andar andar, agar dominion status nahi diya gaya, to Civil Disobedience movement launch kar diya jayega. 
           
           
           
                        
                 
         1929 me Gandhi, pura desh ghuma, or logo ko Direct political action ke liye, tayyar kiya. 
         
         CWC ne Foreign Cloth Boycott committee banai. 
         
         Gandhi, kapde jalane ka plan, 1929 me calcutta se initiate karne wala tha, lekin aisa karne se pehle hi jail ho gayi. 
         
         
         
         
         Isi saal, Meerut Consipiracy case chala ,    Central Legislative assembly me Bhagat and BK Dutt ne bomb feka,    England me Labour GOvernment aa gayi, under Ramsay MacDonal, or Wedgewoon Benn Secy of State bana
         
         
         
         
     Irwin ki 31st October, 1929 ki decleration: 
     
        Simon Commission ki report aane se pehle, Irwin ne ye declare kiya ki, " Saala 1917 me hi hamne bola diya thia, ki Dominion status dena hamara goal hai". 
        
        Par BC, fir saalo ne koi Time Frame nahi diya. 
        
        Irwin, ne ek Round Table Conference karne ko bhi bola, after the simon commission report has been submitted. 
        
        
        
        
        
     Iske baad, 2nd November, 1929 me Prominent national Leaders ne "Delhi Manifesto" issue kiya :  Certain conditions for attending the Round Table Conference
     
     
        1) RTC ka purpose hona chahiye: Formulate a consti for implementation of the dominion status (Matlab RTC ek Consti assembly ki tarah act kare) 
        
        2) Congress ko RTC me mojority representation di jani chahiye
        
        3) Political Prisoners ke liye General Amnesty issue karo, jaise Taliban ne ki hai. 
        
        
        
        
            Gandhi, baaki Khangresio ke saath, December 1929 me Irwin se mila, uska haal chaal puchne (Irwin ki train par thode time pehle hi bomb feka gaya tha)
            
            
               Irwin ne bola, " Dekh ganje, RTC ka purpose koi Dominion status ke liye consti scheme tayyar karna nahi hai, or ye Delhi Manisfesto ko apni gaand me daal le "
               
               
               Ye sunke, baapu ne Irwin ke mu par peshab kar diya, or waha se bhaag gaya.    
               
               
               
               
               
     December, 1929 me LaWhore me INC ka Dhamaakedar Session Hua : Chote Nehru ko bada kaam karne ke liye, President banaya gaya (kyuni Baapu ka aashirwaad hasil tha)
     
     
        1) RTC ko Boycott kiya jayega
        
        2) COngress ka aim hoga, complete independence
        
        3) CWC ko authorise kar diya gaya Civil disobedience programme launch karne ko ----> Including Non-Payment of Taxes  +  Legislature ke sab members were asked to resign          
               
        4) January 26, 1930--> 1st Independence Day==> To be celebrated everywhere. 
        
        
        
        
    December 31, 1929 : Ravi Nadi ke kinare, Newly adopted Tricolour flag of freedom ko hoist kiya gaya by Chota Nehru. 
    
    
    
    Januray 26, 1930 : Independece Pledge
     
         Pure desh me public meetings organise ki gayi, or Independence pledge padhi gayi, local languages me or National Flag ko hoist kiya gaya. 
         
         
    
    Gandhi ne fir, GoI ke aage 11 demands rakhi, or 31st January, 1930 ka ultimatum diya to accept or reject these demands : 
    
    
      General Interst me : 1)  Army or Civil services ka expenditure 50% tak kam karo
      
                           2)  Nasha Mukti
                           
                           3) CID me reforms karo
                           
                           4)  Arms act ko change karo, mujhe pistol chahiye
                           
                           5)  Political prisoners ko release karo
                           
                           6)  Postal Reservation bill ko accept karo
                           
     Specific Brijwasi demands : 7) Rupee Sterling Exchange ration 1s 4d karo
     
                                 8) Textile Protection introduce karo
                                 
                                 9) Coastal Shipping Indians ke liye reserve karo
                                 
          Specific Peasant Demands :  10) Land Revenue 50% tak kam karo
          
                                      11) Salt Tax abolish karo, or Governmen's salt monopoly bhi
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                    Ye demands meet na hone par, CWC ne Gandhi ko full powers de di, to launch the CDM, jab man kare, jaha man kare.                   
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
            12 March 1930 ko Gandhi ne Dandi March start kiya, or 6th April ko salt law break kiya .                           
                                      
                                      
                      Gandhi ne jo baaki instructions diye the, unke basis pe , akele Gujrat ke hi 300 village officials ne resign kar diya.                 
                                       
                                      
                Bas fir Indians NPCs ki tarah Namak Namak karte hue, Pure desh me Salt law break karne lage. 
                
                
                  - Nehru ko April 1930 me Namak bechte hue police ne range haatho pakad liya. Iske baad, Madras, Calcutta or Karachi me huge demonstrations hue. 
                  
                  - Gandhi ko 4th May ko arrest kiya gaya----> Jab bapu ne bola ki wo, Dharsana salt works par chadhai karne wala hai. 
                  
                  
                      Gandhi ke arrest ke baad CWC ne sanction kiya : 
                      
                          a) Ryotwari areas me non-payment of revenue
                          
                          b) Zamindari areas me koi chowkidara-tax na de
                          
                          c) CP me forest laws ko violate kiya jaye. 
                          
                          
                          
                          
                 Tamil Nadu : 
                 
                            1) April, 1930 :   C. Rajagopalachari ne Thiruchirapalli se Vedaranniyam tak (On Tanjore coast) me march organise kiya or Salt law toda.                                
                                      
                                               Iske baad, Foreign cloth shops ki picketing hui,   Anti-Daru campaign fail gaya Coimbatore, Madura, Virdhangar jaise inerior regions me bhi. 
                                               
                                               Gudiyattam me berojgaar weavers ne daaru ke theeko par attack kiya
                                               
                                               
                                      
                                                            
                  Malabar :   
                   
                                     K.Ke;appan (Vaikom Satyagraha se famous hua nair khangressi) ne Salt march organise kiya.     
                                      
                                     Kerala Communist party ka future leader- P. Krishna Pillai, ne Calicut beach par National Flag ko police lathi charge ke beech defend kiya, in November 1930. 
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                     
                  Andhra Region : 
                  
                   
                                         East/West Godavar, Krishna or Guntur regions me District salt marches organise kiye gaye. 
                                         
                                         Salt Satyagraha ke HQs ke liye, bohot saare military style camps setup kiye gaye, jinhe Sibirams kehte the. 
                                         
                                         Kamma or Raju cultivators, jo dominant castes thi---> Inhone Repressive measures ko defy kiya. 
                                         
                                         Lekin, yaha 1921-22 ke NCM jaisa Mass support mission tha                   
                                     
                                     
                                     
                   Orissa : 
                   
                                   Gopalbandhu Chaudhuri ke under Salt Satyagraha organise kiya gaya, Balasore, Cuttack or Puri Districts ke coastal regions me. 
                                   
                                   
                                   
                                   
                                   
                   Assam :   
                    
                                     Yaha alag hi chutiyaap chal raha tha, Hindu-Mullo or, Ahomiya-Bongs ke beech, isliye feeka hi tha CDM. 
                                     
                                     Lekin, Cunningham Circular ke against, ek successful student strike organise ki gayi. 
                                     
                                     Chandraprabha Saikiani, ne December 1930 me Aboriginal Kachari Villages ko bhadkaake, Forest laws break karwaye. 
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                     
                  Bengal :  
                  
                                       Yaha Bengal Congress 2 factions me divide ho gayi thi : Bose and J.M. Sengupta.                   
                                     
                                       Dacca me communal riots ho gaye.   To mullo ne participate nahi kiya. 
                                       
                                       Is sab ke bavajood bhi, Bong sabse jyada arrest hue + Sabse jyada violence ki. 
                                       
                                       Chaukidari tax ke around powerful movements organise hue. 
                                       
                                       Isi time, Surya Sen ne bhavuk hokar, Chittagong armoury raid ki. 
                                       
                                       
                                       
                                       
                   Bihar :     
                   
                                      Champaran or Saran, pehle 2 districts the, jaha Salt Satyagraha start hue. 
                                      
                                      Patna ke Nakhas pond me Salt law break kiya gaya, under Ambika kant sinha. 
                                      
                                      
                                      Lekin jaldi hi, ek kaafi powerful no-chaukidari tax agitation start ho gaya, jisne salt wale chutiyaap ko replace kar diya. 
                                      
                                      
                                      Munger ke kuch regions me to sala administration hi collapse ho gaya. 
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      Jharkhand ke Chhotanagapur ke tribal belt me to lower-class militancy dekhne ko mili. 
                                      
                                      Bonga Majhi or Somra Majhi naam ke 2 gandhian leaders, ne Hazaribagh me movement lead kiya--> Isme socio-religious reforms combine kiye gaye along 'sanskritising' lines. 
                                      
                                      Chote zamindar bhi movemnet me participate kiye. 
                                      
                                      
                                      
                 Peshawar :  
                 
                                   Khan Abdul Gaffar Khan ne Pathans me educational and social reforms kiye the, or unhe politicise kar diya tha. 
                                   
                                   KAGK ko Badhshah khan a.k.a Frontier gandhi kehte the. 
                                   
                                   Is bhai ne pehla Pashto political monthly start kiya tha- 'Pukhtoon' or Khudai khidmatgar naam ki volunteer brigade bhi start ki thi.(red shirts) 
                                   
                                   23 April, 1930 ko congress leaders ke arrest hone se, Peshawar me mass demonstrations hue. 
                                   
                                   Iske baad waha martial law laga diya gaya. 
                                   
                                   Yahi par, Garhwal rifles ke ek section ne Unarmed crowd par fire karne se mana kar diya. 
                                   
                                   
                                   
                                   
               Sholapur : 
               
                                         South Maharshtra ke is industrial town me, Gandhi ke arrest hone se Log aag babula ho gaye. 
                                         
                                         7th May ko Textile workers strike pe chale gaye, or Daaru ke thekko ke aag laga di. 
                                         
                                         Fir inhone, police stations, Munci buildings, Courts etc ke aag laga di. 
                                         
                                         Ye kuch time ke liye, ek parallel government hi start ho gayi thi, jab tak Martial law na laga diya gaya. 
                                         
                                         
                                         
                                         
              Dharsana : 
              
                                    Gandhi ke bete Manilal, Sarojini Naidu or Imam Sahib ne Raid ki salt works pe. 
                                    
                                    320 logo ke sir fode gaye. 
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
                                    
               
             Gujrat : 
             
                               Kheda District ke Anand, Borsad or Nadiad areas me ,  Surat ke Bardoli me, or Bharuch ke Jambusar me impact dekha gaya

                               Log, police ke dando se bachne ke liye, baaju wale princly state of Baroda me bhaag gaye. 
                               
                               Police ne unki properties ke aag laga di, or land confiscate kar li. 
                               
                               
                               
            Maharashtra, Karnataka and CP : 
                                                          Forest laws break kiye gaye, Timer restrictions or grazing restrictions bhi,,   Illegaly acquired forest produce ki khule me sale ki gayi. 
                                                          
                                                          
                                                          
                                                          
                             Uinited Province :    
                             
                             
                                                    No-Revenue Campaign organise kiya gaya.   Zamindars ko bola gaya ki saal khabardaar hai, jo governemnt ko revenue pay kiya to
                                                    
                                                    No-rent campaign organise kiya gay, jisme tenants ne zamindars ko kiraya dene se mana kar diya. 
                                                    
                                                    Agra or Rai-bareilly me specially intense thi
                                                    
                                                    
                                                    
                                                    
                      Manipur and Nagaland : 
                      
                      
                                             13 saal ki Naga leader, Rani Gaidinliu ne foreign rule ke against revolt ka banner utha liya. 
                                             
                                             Logo ko kaha ki brits ko tax mat dena.
