                                             iske bhai ko agle saal brits ne latka diya or bahan ko jail me daal diya. 1946 me Interim gov ne jail se bachaya. 
                                             
                                             
                                             
                                             
                                             
                                             
                July 1930 me Lord Irwin ne Dominon status ka lalch diya, or RTC karne ki baat ki. 
                
                    Isi time, Tej Bahadur Sampru or M.R.Jayakar, aage aake bole, ki hum peace karwate hain na Sir, COngress or aapke beech. Tension mat lo. 
                    
                    
                    
                    
                August 1930 me Moti or Chote Nehru ko Yeravad Jail bheja gaya, Gandhi se milne.     
                    
                                                 
                                             
                   January 25, 1931 ko Gandhi or CWC ke baaki sab members ko jail se chor diya gaya.                                                                                                         
                                                           
                   Feb 14, 1931 ko Gandhi-Irwin ek baar fir mile.  Gandhi ne promise kiya tha, ki wo Irwin par dobara peshaab nahi karega. 
                   
                   Or in dono ke beech, Gandhi-Irwin Pact sign hua : 
                   
                   
                          1) Political Prisoners ka release, jinhone violence na ki ho
                          
                          2) Remission of all fines
                          
                          3) Return of all lands 
                          
                          4) jin gov servants ne resign kar diya tha, unka lenient treatment
                          
                          5) Personal Consumption ke liye, namak banane ki ajaadi
                          
                          6) Peaceful picketing karne ki ajaadi
                          
                          7) Emergency ordinances ko withdraw kar diya gaya. 
                          
                          
                    Gandhi ne agree kiya to : 
                    
                             1) Suspend CDM 
                             
                             2) Participate in next RTC  
                             
                 Meeting khatam hone par, Irwin jaise hi Gandhi se haath milane gaya, Gandhi ne apni dhoti uthayi or Irwin ke mu pe dobara peshab kar diya, or Jaldi se waha se bhaag gaya. 
                 
                 
                 
                 
        CDM vs NCM
        
        
            1) Objective : Complete independence vs 2 specific wrongs and a vaguely worded swaraj
            
            2) Shuru se hi violation of laws and not just non-cooperation with foreign rule. 
            
            3) Intelligentisia jaise Lawyers giving up practices,  Students giving up Government schools, colleges etc    me decline aaya. 
            
            4) Mulle to aaye hi nahi
            
            5) Koi major labour upsurge bhi nahi hua
            
            6) Lekin is baar 3 guna jyada logo ko jail hui
            
            7) Congress jyada tagdi thi, organisationally
            
            
            
            
            
       March 1931 me, Karachi me Congress ka special session organise hua to endore Gandhi-Irwin. 
       
       
          Iske bas 6 din pehle hi, Bhagat singh, Sukhdev or Rajguru ko latkaya gaya tha. 
          
          Karachi me Punjab Naujawan Bharat Sabha ne Gandhi ko kaali jandhe dikhaye, or maa bahan ki gali di. 
          
          
          Congress ka karachi resolution : 
          
                 1) Teeno martyrs ki bravery or sacrifice ko admire kiya gaya
                 
                 2) Gandhi-Irwin pact / Delhi Pact ko endorse kiya gaya
                 
                 3) Purna Swaraj ke goal ko reiterate kiya gaya. 
                 
                 4) 2 resolution adopt kiye gaye :- Pehla Fundamental Rights par,                                            Dusra National Economic programme par. 
                                                          - Speech or free press                                                   - Rent or revenues me reduction
                                                          - Association banane ka right                                            - Agri karje ke relief
                                                          - Assemble hone ka right                                                 - Usury par control
                                                          - UAF                       						   - Industrial workers ki dekhbhaal
                                                          - Caste, creed or sex         		  	                   - Unions banane ke rights
                                                          - Secular state                                                          - Key industries, jaise mines, transport par state ka control
                                                          - Dree and compulsory primary education
                                                          - Culture, language, script of minorities ka protection
                                                          - Linguistic groups ka bhi
                 
                     
             Ye pehla time tha, jab congress ne bataya ki sala Swaraj dikhega kaisa. 
             
             Ye Karachi Resolutionm hi aage tak congress ka Political and economic programme rehne wala tha. 
             
             
             
             
             
             
       1st RTC
       
           - November 1930 se January 1931 tak
           - Chairman- Ramsay MacDonald
           - Ye pehli conference thi, jisme Brits or Indians equal mile the
           - Congress + Bizness leaders milne nahi gaye. 
           
           
           Princly States wale chode gaye the. 
           
           Mulla league ne bheja : Aga Khan III,  Shafi, Jauhar,  Snake Jinnah,  Zafarullah Khan, Hidayat ullah,   
           
           Dindu Mahasabha : B.S. Moonjee,  M.R. Jayakar and Diwan Bahadur Raja Narendra Nath
           
           Sikhs : Sardar Ujjal Singh and Sardar Sampuran Singh
           
           Parsis: Phiroze Sethna, Cowardji Jehangir   and   Homey Modi
           
           Women :  Begum Jahanara Shahnawaz   and   Randibai Subbaran
           
           Liberals :  J.N. Basu,   Tej Bahadue Saanpru  ,  C.Y. Chintamani,   V.S.Srinivas Shastri     and CHimanlal ki gandlal Setalvad
           
           Bhangio :  BRA and Rettamalai Srinivasan
           
           Justice Party: Arcot Ramasamy Mudaliar,  Bhaskarrao Vithojirao Jadhav and Sir A.P. Patro
           
           Labour:  N.M.Joshi and B. Shiva Rao
           
           Indian Christians: K.T.Paul
           
           Anglo Indians:  Henry Gidney
           
           GoI : Narendra Nath Law,  Bhupendra nath Mitra, C.P. Ramaswami Iyer and M.Ramachandra Rao
           
           
           Loda kuch achieve nahi hua
           
       2nd RTC
       
          INC ne bheja : Gandhi as sole representative.  A. Rangaswamy Iyenger and MMM bhi chale gaye, london ghumne. 
          
          Is se bhi loda kuch achieve nahi hua.
          
             - Irwin ko replace kar diya gaya tha just pehle, with Lord Willingdon as Viceory
             - Labour Govt replace ho gayi thi, Labour or Conservative ek ghatiya se coalition se called national governement. 
             - British log waise bhi gusse me the
             - Churchill choda, fannaya hua tha. 
             - Neech, mullo, Christians ne minority pact kar liya
             - Princes bhi federation wagarh me koi interest nahi rakhte the. 
             
             
               End me announce hua : 
               
                  1) 2 mulla majority provinces ka formation - NWFP and Sindh
                  
                  2) Indian Consultative commiittee ka setup
                  
                  3) 3 expert committee ka setup- Finance, Franchise and states
                  
                  4) Agar Indians agree nahi kar paye, to Unilateral British Communal Award. 
                  
            Gandhi ko Gussa aa gaya, or usne Table par chadhke, APni dhoti uthai or Sabke saamne Peshab kar diya, or 28th December, 1931 ko Police se bachta bachata wapas India aa gaya. 
            
            
            
            
      3rd RTC 
      
        
         November 1932 se December 1932 tak.     Isme Koi INC wala nahi gaya. 
               
            
            
               Iski recommendation par, ek white paper issue kiya gaya. 
               
               Ek joint committe banayi, Brit Parliament ne, ye recommendations analyse karne ke liye, or India ke liye, ek naya act formulate karne ke liye. 
               
               Is committee ne Feb 1935 me ek draft bill formulate kiya, which was endorsed as GoI act of 1935. 
            
            
            
            
            
            
            
      2nd RTC ke fail hote hi, CWC ne 29th December, 1931 ko CDM resume kar diya.       
            
                  
                  
              4th January, 1932 ko Gandhi ko arrest kar liya gaya.     
                  
                  
              Government ne ek civil martial law laga diya.   
              
              Congress organisations at all levels were banned. 
              
              Activists, leaders, sympathisers wagarh ko jail me patak diya. 
              
              Press gag kiya, Nationalist literature bann kad diya gaya. 
              
                  
         Finally, Gandhi ne April 1934 me CDM withdraw kar liya. 
         
         
         
         
     Communal Award and Poona Pact
     
     
        - Ramsay McDonald ne August 16, 1932 ko Communal Award announce kiya. 
        
        - Ise Lothian Committee (Indian Franchise Committee) ki recommendation par finalise kiya gaya tha. 
        
        - Is se, Minorities, including mulla and Bhangi jaati ke liye, Seperate electorates establish kiye gaye. 
        
        - Matlab, Ab---> Mulla, Europeans, Sikhs, Christians, Anglo-Indians, Depressed Classes or Bombay me Marathas ke liye bhi seperate electorates diye gaye. 
        
        
        
        
        Depressed class ke liye ye provisions 20 saalo ke liye the. 
        
        NWFP ke alawa, Sab provinces me 3% seats, women ke liiye reserved thi. 
        
        
        
        
        
        Congress even though to opposed thi, seperate electorates ke, lekin fir bhi, Congress decided neither to Accept it nor to reject it. 
        
        
        Gandhi ko lagta tha, ki seperate electorates se Achoot puri life ke liye, Achoot ban ke rah jayenge. 
        
           Is liye, Gandhi, Sept 20 me Indefinit fast par chala gaya.           
             
             
              Gandhi ko manane ke liye- BRA, M.C.Rajah,  MMM aaye----> Poona Pact
              
                                                                         - Joint electorates with reserved seats---> Provincial Legislature me seat 71 se badhkar 147 kar di gayi, or Central wale me 18 %. 
                                                                         
                                                                         
             
             
             
             Lekin, ALl India Scheduled Caste Federation ki Working committee ne bola : Is poona bakchodi or Joint Electorates ki wajah se---> Neech log apni pasand ke legislature nahi chun paate.    Hindu majority, apni pasand ka neech banda elect karke, legislature me bhej deti hai. 
             
             BRA bhadwa, Poona Pact sign karke ke baad bhi, 1947 tak ise denounce karta raha. 
             
             
             
             
       Gandhi ki Harijan Campaign
       
         - Gandhi me Jail ke andar se hi, GoI ke devisive agenda ko counter karne ke liye, Untouchability ke against campaign start kar di. 
         
         - September, 1932 me jail ke andar se hi---> ALl India Anti-Untouchability league setup kar di. 
         
         - 1933 me, 'Harijan' naam ka weekly start kiya. 
         
         - Jail se nikalkar, Satyagraha Ashram ko Wardha shift kar diya, kyunki 1930 me pran liya tha, ki Swaraj milne tak Sabarmati Ashram wapas nahi aayega. 
         
         - Wardha se fir bhai ne, Harijan tour start kiya, pure desh ka, Between 1933 to July 1934-----> Paise ikatthe kiye, Harijan Sevak Sangh ke liye. 
         
         - Dindu bhadwe, Gandhi ke khilaf khade ho gaye : Saale GoI ko support karne lage. 
         
                   GoI ne bhi Dindu bhadwo ko khush karne ke liye : Temple Entry Bill of 1934 ko defeat kar diya. 
         
         
         
         
         
         
     C.D.M gaddhe me jaane ke baad, debate chid gayi, ki BC ab kya karein
     
     
        3 ideas the :-  1) Gandhi ki tarah constructive work karne ka dhong karo
        
                        2) Elections me participate kar, Constitutional Struggle karo===> M.A.Ansaro,  Asaf Ali,  Bhulabhai Desai, S.Satyamuthry and B.C. Roy    
         
         
                        3) Congress ke andar ek strong leftist trend ubhar ke aa raha tha---> Led by Chota Nehru====> Non-Consti mass struggle ko resume karo. 
                        
                        
           Ye chahte the, ki Struggle-Truce-Struggle wale chutiyaap ki jagah Continous direct action hona chahiye(Specially after Lahore Purna Swaraj) and he suggested, A Struggle-Victory (S-V) Strategy. 
           
           
           
           
      Congress me split na ho jaye, isliye, Gandhi ne allow kar diya, Council entry ko. 
      
      1934 me All India COngress COmmittee(AICC) Patna me mili---> Parliamentary Board setup kiya---> To fight elections under the aegis of the congress itself. 
      
      
      October 1934 me Gandhi ne congress se resignation de diya. 
      
           
      November 1934 me Central Legislative Assembly ke jo elections hue, unme Congress ne 75 me se 45 seats jeet li.    
           
           
           
           Government of India act, 1935
           
           
           
              1) All India Federation 
              
              
              2) Centre me Dyarchy-> Reserved or Transferred subject
              
              3) Central Legislature:   Upper house (Council of States- 260 members), and Lower house ( Federal Assembly- 375 members) 
              
                                     CoS--> Partly elected from British Indian provinces and 40% nominated by the princes. 
                                     
                                     FA---> Partly indirectly elected from British Indian Provinces and 33% nominated by the princes. 
                                     
                                        (BC, kya chutiyaap hai---> Council of States ke election direct hain, or Fereal Assembly ke indirect)
                                        
                                        
              4) CoS--> Permanent body, 1/3rd members retiring every 3rd year.     FA---> 5 year duration
              
              5) Federal, Provincial and Concurrent lists. 
              
              6) FA ke members, Ministers ke against no-confidence motion pass kar sakte the. 
              
              7) Reliion or Caste based electorated ke system ko or bhi extend kar diya gaya. 
              
              8) 80% of budget, votable tha. 
              
              9) Governor-General ke pass residuary powers thi. 
              
             10) GG ke pass veto or ordinance powers bhi thi,or , ye saala chahta toGrants me cuts ko restore kar sakta tha,or Legislature ne agar bills ko reject kiya ho, to bhi unhe cerify kar sakta tha.                           
           
             11) Provinces se Dyarchy wala randaap khatam ho gaya, or Provincial Autonomy de di gayi. 
             
             12) Provinces ki legal assistance Secy of State or GG se hatakar, seedhe British Crown ke haatho me de di gayi. 
             
             13) Governor, direct crown ka representative tha. 
             
             14) Legislature ke saare members, directly elected the.    Aurato ko voting ka adheekar de diya gaya. 
             
             15) Responsible Ministers. 
             
             16) 40 % of budget, abhi bhi non-votable tha. 
             
             17) Governor chahta to Bill ko assent dene se mana kar sakta tha, Ordinace promulgate kar sakta tha,     Governor's act enact kar sakta tha. 
             
             18) Act, se laghbhag 14% Indians ko franchise mila. 
             
           
         
         
         
         Dindu Mahasabha or National Liberal Foundation ke alawa laghbhag sab Naionalists ne iska khandan kiya.       
             
            Congress boli, " Sala, Ab tum Consti assembly banao, to frame a constitution for Independent India ". 
            
            
            
             
             
              
     1937 me Provincial assemblies ke election announce kiye gaye, or ek baar fir debate start ho gayi. 
     
     
      - Nehru, Bose or baaki socialists, bilkul opposed the, office acceptance ke. 
      
      - Inhone, purane swarajists ki tarah, councils me ghuske, unki andar se maa chodne ke tareeke ko sahi bataya. 
      
                 
             
             
             Inke opposite, Office acceptance wale kehte the, ki Abhi mass movement ka time nahi hai, isliye chupchaap office accept karo as a short term tactic. 
             
             
             
             
          
          
          
          Gandhi bhi shuru me office acceptance ke against the, lekin 1936 tak, Bhai ne bhi congress minitries ke formation ko try karne ke liye thoda bohot raaji ho gya. 
          
          
          
       1936 ke Lucknow Sessio or 1937 ke Faizpur session me Congress ne decide kiya ki, Election fight karnenge, or Office Accept karne hai ya nahi karne, ye post election phase me decide karenge. 
       
       
       
       
       
       February, 1937 me provincial assemblies ke election hue---  11 provincs me. 
       
             Ye pehle election the, jisme kaafi saare Indians eligible the participate karne ko.  Laghbgah 15 million logo ne vote daale. 
             
             
             Congress ne jin 1161 seats par contest kiya tha, unme se wo 716 me jeet gate. 
             
             Sab provinces me majority mili except:- Bengal, Assam, Punjab and Sindh and NWFP. 
             
               Bengal, Assam and NWFP me bhi largest party yahi hi thi. 
               
               Is performance se Congress ki prestige badhi, or Chote Nehru ko man na pada ki S-T-S strategy hi theek hai. 
               
               
               
         Congress ne ministries form ki in Bombay, Madras, CP, Orissa , UP, Bihar or baad me NWFP or Assam me bhi. 
         
         
         
         
      Congress rule in Provinces : 
      
      
         28 mahine, Provinces me Congress rule raha. 
         
            
            1) Emergency powers dene wale laws, repeal kiye
            
            2) Hinustan Seval Dal and Youth leagues, jaise orgs se ban utha, Books or journalists wagarah se bhi. 
            
            3) Press restrictions hatai
            
            4) Black lists se Newspapers ko nikala
            
            5) Arms licences restore kiye
            
            6) Police or CID ko theekane lagaya
            
            7) Political prisoners or Revolutionaries ko release kiya
            
            8) CDM ke time bombay me jo land confiscate ki thi, wo lota di. 
            
            9) Lekin, Socialists or Communists par dande maare. Ye to achaa kiya. 
            
            
            
        Agrarian Reforms : 
        
             1) Land reforms, Debt relief, Forest frazing fee, Arrears of rent, Land tenures etc se related kai legislature laaye.     
            
            
            
       Labour : 
       
        
              Indistrialists or Labour ke beech rishte ache karne ki koshis ki gayi, taaki strikes kam ho, or Industrialisation badhe. 
              
              Lekin, Section 144 ka use karke, bohot saare leaders ko arrest kiya gaya. 
              
              
                   
        Social Welfare Reforms: 
        
        
              1) Bohot saare areas me Daaru band
              
              2) harijans ke liye:- Temple entry. Use of Public facilities, Scholorships, Government services me unka number badhaye, etc
              
              3) Primary, Technical or Higher education par jor diya gaya. 
              
              4) Public Health or Sanitation par kaam kiya gaya. 
              
              5) Khadi wagarh
              
              6) Prison reforms
              
              7) Subhash Bose ke under, National Planning Committee setup ki gayi, in 1938. 
              
              
                  
            
            
            
     In 1938 Gujrat ke Haripura, me Congress ki meeting hui, jisme Bose ko President elect kiya gaya.       
            
            - Session me decide hua ki, Pricly states me jo log governance ke against agitation kar rahe hain, Congress unhe moral support degi. 
            
           
            
            
     In 1939, Bose firse, Congress ke president ki post ke liye khade hue.      Lekin, Gandhi ko Bose nahi pasand the. 
     
       Gandhi ke baaki chele--> Sardar Patel, Rajendra Prasad, J.B.Kriplani etc  Gandhi ke canditate Pattabhi Sitaramayya ko support karte the. 
       
       Lekin, End me jeet to Bose ki hi honi thi.    Bose ko Congress socialist party or Commie chodo ka bhi support hasil tha. 
       
       Gandhi ki gand me aag lag gayi. 
       
       
       
     March 1939 me, Tripuri me congress session hua, in CP. 
     
     
       Bose ke election se Congress ke andar hi ghamaasaan macha hua tha. 
       
       
       Bose ne kai saae Working committee ke leaders par keechad uchal diya tha,ki ye saale to GoI ke saath Federation banane ko utawle ho rakhe hain. Is wajah se ye saale bhi jale hue the. 
       
       
       Bose ne kaha, ki saala British GoI ko 6 mahine ka ultimatum dena chahiye---> Ki national Demand for Independence maan lo, nahi to fir se CDM start kar denge, Bokachoda. 
       
       
       Bose ko lagta tha, ki Europe me mache randaap ka fayda uthana chahiye. 
       
       Lekin, Fattu Gandhi, Fir ek baar loda leke aage khada ho gaya. 
       
       
       Isi beech, chupke se Govind Ballabh Pant ne apni Pant utari, or apna Lund hilake Gandhi ke support me ek resolution pass kar diya. 
       
          Or ye pass bhi ho gaya, kyunki Gandhi se sabki fat ti thi. 
          
          
          Lekin, Fir Gandhi bhola banke bola ki, Kyunki Bose president hai, isliye, Bose ko hi CWC ke members appoint karne ki choot honi chahiye, main nahi bolunga kuch bhi. 
          
          
      Bose ne Gandhi ko samjhane ki koshis ki, Lekin Bose ko nahi pata tha ki Gandhi ne Nehru ka lemon chus liya hai, or Nehru ne Viceroy ka lemon chus liya hai, or Viceroy ne Secy of State ka....
      
      
      Isliye, Bose ne bhi fir, CWC appoint karne se mana kar diya.       
          
          
      Or Bose ne fir, Aakhir kaar Resign kar diya, President ki post se, in April 1939. 
      
      
      Iske baad, Gandhi ke chele Rajendra Prasad ko president elect kar diya gaya.     
          
          
          
          
          
          
             May me, Bose or unke yaaro ne, Unnao ke Makur shahr me, Forward Bloc start kiya-----> As a new party within Congress. 
             
             
                Bose ne fir, AICC resolution ke against jaakar, 9 July ko ek All-India protest karne ko bola. Ye dekh ke to CWC ke chodo ki gaand me missile fat gayi, or unhone Bose par Disciplinary action le daala. 
                
                
                August me, Bose ko Bengal Provincial Congress committee ke president ki post se hata diya gaya. 
                
                Or Yaha bhi CWC ke chodo ka man nahi bhara, to unhone Bose bhai, ko congress ke kisi bhi post ke chunav ladne se , 3 saal ke liye ban laga diya. 
                
                
                
                
          
      Tripuri Session me Congress ne CHina me ek Medical Mission bhejne ka resolution bhi banaya tha.  
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       September 1, 1939 --> Germany Attacked Poland
       
       September 3, 1939 --> Britain Declared war against Germs
       
       British GoI ne bina Indians ka opinion jane, declared India's support for war. 
       
       
       
       
          Congress Offer to ViceroY
          
          
             - It decided to support the war, conditionally. 2 basic conditions par : 
             
                 i) War ke baad, ek Consi assembly banai jayegi, to determine the political structure of a free India. 
                 
                ii) Immediately, some form of responsible government should be established at the centre. 
                
            Linlithgow (Viceroy) ne offer se apni gand ponch li. 
            
            
            
            
            
     Congress Working Committee ne Wardha Session me ye official position ko adopt kar liya.  Lekin ab bhi saale, lad pade aapas me 
     
       
         1) Gandhi choda, Britain ke saath tha. 
         
         2) Subhash Bose or baaki Socialists (Acharya Narendra Dev, Jayaprakash Narayan)---> Inhe koi side nahi pasand thi.   Inke opinion me war, dono sides par imperialists fight kar rahe the. 
         
             Ye bolte ki, ye ek ideal time hai CDM launch karne ka. 
             
         3) Chota Nehru, Gandhi or Bose, dono ke khillaf tha :   Ise lagta tha, ki Justice was on the side of Britain, France and Poland.    Lekin, ise ye bhi pata tha ki, Britain or France colonial powers hain.     
                    
               Ye bolta, " No Indian participation, till India is free"  , "Lekin, same time pe, CDM launch kar, Britain ki difficulty ko or na badhaya jaye".
               
               
               
              Gandhi akela tha apne stand par.   Lekin, Last me Chote Nehru ke saath ho liya. 
              
              CWC ne bhi, CHote nehry wali position ko adopt kiya. 
              
              
              
              
              
         Lekin, Linlithgow ne 17th October, 1939 me Mulla League or Princes ko Congress ke against use karne ka faisla kiya. 
         
         
                - Govt. Refuse kar diya, British war aims ko define karne se, Bas ye bol diya ki hum Aggression ko resist kar rahe hain. 
                
                - War ke baad dekhenge ki 1935 act ko kaise modify kiya ja sakta hai. 
                
                - Jyada hi hai, to ek "consultative committee" bana dete hain, jis se jab man kare, advice le lena, malai maar ke. 
                
                
                
         Actually, GoI chahti thi, ki War ka fayda utake COngress ko provoke kare,taaki Congress chutiyo ki tarah GoI se confrontation me kud pade, or fir Extraordinary situation ke naam pe, Draconian powers ka use, uski maa chod di jaa sake. 
         
         
           In saalo ne, Is se pehle hi Defence of India act ko amend kar diya tha. 
           
           Or baad me saale, May 1940 me ek Draft ordincance tayyar kiye, Draft Revolutionary Movement Ordinace, taaki Congress ki pre-emptively maa chod di jaa sake. 
                
                
                
                
                
        Gusse me aake, 23 October, 1939 ko, Congress ki minisstries ne resign kar diya. 
        
        
        Linlithgow ki statement ke baad, ek baar fir, Mass Movement ki baate start ho gayi. 
        
        
                        
              
           January 1940 me Linlithgow bola, "War ke baad, Wesminster variety ka dominion status de denge, Bahan ke lodo "
           
           
              
              
              
         March, 1940 me Congress ne Ramgarh me session kiya, Jiska President tha Maulana Abul Kalam Azad. 
         
          
            - "Complete independence ke alawa kuch nahi chahiye". 
            
            - Or jaise hi hamari organisation me jaan aayegi, hum Civil disobedience launch kar denge.      
              
              
              
        Isi beech, March 1940 me Mulla league ne Pakistan Resolution pass kar diya. 
        
        
           
        
        
        
        
        
        
        
        
    AUGUST OFFER
    
    
        - Jaise hi, Hitler ne Belgium, Holland or France par kabja kiya, Britain ke totte ud gaye. 
        
        - Lekin, Fattu Gandhi or Chota Nehru, ab bhi is situation ka fayda uthane ko theek nahi samjhte the. 
        
        - War effort me Indians ki cooperation ke liye, Linlithgow ne August Offer present kiya, In August 1940
        
            1) Objective - Dominion Status
            
            2) Viceroy ki executive council ko expand kar denge, jisme majority Indians honge. 
            
            3) War, ke baad consti assembly bana denge, jisme 'Mainly' Indians apna consti decide karenge. 
            
            4) Minorities ke consent ke bina koi future consti nahi adopt kiya jayega. 
            
            
            
        - Iske Response me Nehru ne apna lemon offer kiya or bola, "Dominion status concept is dead as a doornail". 
        
        
        - Mulla league ne accept kiya, kyunki unhe is se ek tarah ka veto mil gaya tha, or bole ki Deadlock ka ek hi solution hai- Partition. 
        
        
        
        - Lekin, ye pehla time tha, jab, Rights of Indians to frame their own consti ko recognise kiya gaya tha or Congress ki consti assembly banane ki demand ko concede kiya gaya tha. 
        
        
        
        
    July, 1941 me Viceroy ki Executive council ko enlarge kar Indians ko 12 me 8 post de di gayi.    Lekin, Gore abhi bhi Defence, Finance or Home ke charge me the. 
    
    Ek National Defence Council bhi setup ki gayi, lekin iske bas purely advisory functions the. 
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    Individual Satyagraha
    
     
        - Isi beech, GoI ordinance par ordinances laye jaa rahi thi, or logo ke Freedom of speech, press or right to organise associations liye jaa rahi thi. 
        
        - 1940 ke end tak, Congress ne fir ek baar, Gandhi ko bola Command lene ke liye. 
        
        - Gandhi ne Individual basis par limited Satyagrah karne ka man banaya, by few individuals. 
        
        - Aims : 
        
                    i) Ye dikhane ke liye, nationalist patience , weakness ki wajah se nahi hai. 
                    
                   ii) Ye batane ke liye, logo ka war me koi interst nahi hai 
                   
                  iii) Govt ko ek or opportunity dene ke liye, taaki wo congress ki baat man le. 
                  
                  
        - Satyagrahi ki demand hogi--> Anti-war decleration ke through, War ke against freedom of speech demand karna.           
                  
                  Agar GoI ne arrest nahi kiya, to villages me jana, or bande ikathe kar, Delhi ki taraf march karna. -------> 'Delhi Chalo Movement'. 
                  
                  
                  
        - Vinobha Bhave, 1st banda tha, to offer the Satyagraha.     Chota Nehru, 2nd. 
        
        - May, 1941 tak, 25,000 logo ko Individual Civil disobedience ke liye convict kiya ja chua tha. 
        
        
        
        
   Isi beech, Japan ne Asia me dhoom macha di.   GoI ki fat gayi, or usne December 1941 me Congress leaders ko release kar diya. 
   
   
       Ab, ye khangresi bhi Britain ki madad ko raji ho gaye the, India ke defence ke liye.      
       
       
       
       CWC ne Gandhi or Nehru ki objections ko over ride karte hue, a resolution pass kiya offering to cooperate with the Governemnt in the defence of India agar, 
       
          i) War ke baad full independence de di jaye
          
         ii) Substance of power, abhi transfer kar di jaye. 
         
         
       Ye wo time tha, jab Gandhi ne Nehru ko apna successor designate kiya. 
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
    1942 me, ek mission India bheja gaya under Stafford Crpps----> With consti proposals to seek Indian Support for the war. 
    
      Cripps ek Leftist labour ka banda tha,  House of Commons ka leader tha, or British War cabinet ka member bhi tha----> Ye Indian National Movement ko actively support bhi kar chuka tha. 
      
      
      
         Lekin kyu : 
         
                          a) South-East Asia me Britain ki maa chud gayi thi
                          
                          b) Allies Pressur daal rahe the, to seek Indian cooperation 
                          
                          c) Indian nationalists bhi tayyar ho gaye thi Allied cause ko support karne ko. 
                          
                          
                          
                 Main proposals : 
                 
                                  1) Dominion Status ke saath ek Indian Union setup ki jayegi---> Ise adhikar hoga, Commonwealth or UN etc ke saath apne relations decide karne ka. 
                                  
                                  2) War ke baad consti assembly---> Members :- i) Partially elected by provincial Assemblies through PR   ii) Partly nominated by Princes
                                  
                                  3) Brits 2 conditions par constitution ko accept kar lenge :- a) Jo province Union na join karna chahti ho, khudka seperate consti bana sakti hai, or seperate unio bhi
                                                                                          b) Nayi consti making body ke saath British Gov, transfer of power karegi, agar racial/Religious minorites ke rights safeguard hue to         
                          
                                  4) Meantime, Defence of India British haatho me rahega, or GG ke powers Intact rahenge.  
                                     
                          
                               
       
         BC, to isme naya kya tha ? 
         
                1) Mainly nahi Solely Indian hands 
                
                2) Consti assembly ke liye concrete plan diya gaya tha
                
                3) India ki partition ka blueprint bhi de diya tha, wo seperate consit wali baat se
                
                4) Free India , commonwealth se withdraw kar sakta tha
                
                5) Interim period me Indians ko Admin ka kaafi large share diya jaane wale tha. 
                
                
                
         To Cripps mission fail kyu ho gaya ? 
         
                 1) Congress ne object kiya : 
                                                  i) Dominion status ko apni gand me dal lo
                                                  
                                                 ii) Princly states apne nominie kyu bhejenge, elected reps kyu nahi 
                                                 
                                                iii) Provinces ko seced hone ka adhikar---> Ye kya bakchodi hai
                                                
                                                 iv) Koi Immediate transfer of power ka plan nahi hai, na hi defence ka koi shared plan hai.        
                
                
                
                
                 2) Mulla league ne bhi criticize kiya : 
                 
                                                             i) Single Indian Union ke idea ko 
                                                             
                                                            ii) Consti assembly create karne ki machinery nahi pasand aayi mullo ko
                                                            
                                                           iii) Inhe laga ki is se Mullo ko right to self determination nahi milega or Pakistan ka creation nahi ho payega. 
                                                           
                                                           
                                                           
                                                           
                                                           
                 3) Baaki logo ne bhi oppose kiya : 
                 
                             i) Liberals ko secession wali baat nahi pasand aayi, as it was against the unity and security of India 
                             
                            ii) Hindu Mahasabha--> same
                            
                           iii) Neech jaat ko laga ki partition se wo Hinduo ke gulam ban jayenge
                           
                            iv) Sikho ko laga ki Partition se Punjab chin jayega un se. 
                            
                            
                            
                            
                  Gandhi ne is scheme ko "ek post dated cheque" karar diya. 
                  
                  
                                                                     
                                                           
                                                           
           Cripps ke lotne ke baad, Indians ko samajh aa gaya ki Ab Imperialism par final assault ka time aa gaya hai. 
           
           
           
           
           
           
           
           
           
     Quit India Movement
     
     
        Cripps ke wapas jaate hi, Gandhi ne ek Resolution frame kiya----> Calling for British Withdrawl   and a non-violent non-cooperation movement against any Japani invasion. 
        
        
        Wardha(July 14, 1942) me CWC ki meeting me, ye accept kar liya gaya.       
           
           
           
           Lekin kyu ? 
           
                         i) Cripps mission fail hone ki wajah se 
                         
                        ii) War conditions ki wajah se logo ki chudi padi thi
                         
                       iii) Logo ko dar tha ki, japani tel ke dar se, British Assam Bengal and Orissa me scorched earth policy follow karne wale hain. 
                       
                        iv) Brits ki maa chud gayi thi South East asia me. 
           
                         v) Jis ghatiya tarah se Brits ne South-East Asia me withdraw kiya, us se unka racist attitude expose ho gaya. 
                         
                        vi) Japanese Invasion ke liye, logo ko tayyar karna tha. 
                        
                        
                        
             July 1942 me, CWC ne wardha me ----> Gandhi ko Non-violent QIM start karne ko bola. 
             
             
                 - Ise Chote nehru ne propose kiya tha. 
                 - Ise, August me Bomaby me AICC ki meeting me approve kiya jana tha. 
                 
                 
                 
             Quit India Resolution, ko Gowalia Tank, Bombay ki 8th August ki Congress meeting me ratify kiya gaya. 
             
                 i) Demand an immediate end to British rule in India
                 
                ii) Declare commitment of Free India to defend itself from all types of Fascism and Imperialism 
                
               iii) British withdrawl ke baad, ek Provicial Gov of India form karna
               
                iv) British rule ke against ek CDM sanction karna
                
               
               
               
          Ganndhi ne following instructions diye, alag alag sections ko : 
          
          
          
             Gov Servants: Resign mat karo, lekin apna allegiance, congress ko dedo
             
             Soldiers : Army na choro, lekin apne compatriots par fire mat karna
             
             Students : Agar confident ho, to padhai chor do
             
             Peasants: Agar Zamindar, Anti-gov hai, to mutually agreed rents pay karo, nahi pro-gov hain, to rent pay mat karna
             
             Princes : Masses ko support karo, or apne logo ki sovereignity accept karo
             
             Princly State ke logo : Apne ruler ko support karo, only if wo anti-gov hai, or apne aap ko Indian naiton ka part declare karo. 
             
             
             
             
             
        Iske baad, Gandhi ne "Do or Die" ka nara diya.      
             
             
             
             
             
   August 9,1942 ko single sweep me, Congress ke sab top leaders ko arrest kar liya gaya. 
   
    CWC,  AICC or Provincial COngress committee ko Unlawful associations declare kar diya gaya, under Criminal Law amendment act of 1908. 
    
    Defence of India, rule 56 ke under, Assembly of public meeting ko prohibit kar diya gaya. 
    
    
    
    Aruna Asaf Ali, ne 9th August ki Congress committee session ko preside kiya and hoisted the flag. 
    
    
    
    
    
    Usha Mehta ne Bombay me Underground radio start kiya. 
    
    
    
    3 jagah to Parallel Govs, setup ki gayi : 
    
       i) Ballia - Under Chittu Pandey - 1 week ke liye
       
      ii) Tamluk - Under Jatiya Sarkar - 2 saal ke liye
      
     iii) Satara - Y.B.Chavan, Nana Patil etc- 2 saal ke liye
    
    
    
    
    Communists ne Movement join nahi ki--- Inhe India se jyada Russia pasand tha. 
    
    
    
    Mulla league ne Movement ko oppose kiya.  
    
    
    Dindu Mahasabha ne movement ko boycott kiya
    
    Princely states ne jyada jhant nahi khujlai. 
    
    
    
    
    
    GoI ne 10,000 se jyada bande maar diye.    Bohot saari jagah par military took over.   
    
    
              
             
             Outcome : 
             
                             i) Leaders ke bina, movement violent ho gayi. 
                             
                            ii) Storm Centres : Eastern UP, Bihar, Mindanpore, Maharashtra and Karnataka
                            
                           iii) Backbone : Students, workers and peasants,   Upper classes and Brijwasi remained loyal
                           
                            iv) Is se prove ho gaya ki, Nationalism desh ke ang ang me sama gaya hai. 
                            
                             v) It was no longer possible to rule India, without wishes of Indians. 
                             
                            vi) Ab bas Independence Immediate agenda ban gaya national movement ka. 
                            
                           vii) Common people ne unparalled heroism dikhaya. 
                           
                           
                           
                           
                           
        February 1943 me gandhi ne FAst kiya, to condemn violence of the state.                    
             
             Iski wajah se Viceroy's executive council ke 3 members ne resign kar diya. 
             
             
             
        March 23, 1943 ko Pakistan Day banaya gaya. 
        
        
        
        
           1943 me Benal me Famine aa gayi.      
             
             
             
             
                  
               
        C. Rajagopalachari ne Congress-Mulla Cooperation ke liye, C.R formulae diya, in 1944 : 
        
             i) Mulla leage, congress ki independecne ki demand ko endorse karegi
            
            ii) Centre me dono milke provisional government banayenge
            
           iii) War ke baad, NW and NE ke Mulla Majority areas ki saari population, plebiscite se decide karegi, ki Seperate soverign state chahiye ya nahi
           
            iv) Partition ke accept hone par, Defence, COmmerece, communication etc ke liye joint agreemnt banayenge. 
            
             v) Ye sab tabhi hoga, jab England Indai ko full transfer of power de dega. 
             
             
             
             
             
             
        Jinnah chahta tha, Congress 2-Nation theory accept kar le. 
        
          Ye chahta tha, bas NW or NE ke mulle hi, plebiscite me vote karein, entire population nahi. 
          
          Ye common centre ke idea ke bhi opposed tha. 
          
          
          
          
          
     Desai-Liaqat pact : 
     
     
          1) Interim Gov Banane ke liye, dono ne milke ek Draft proposal tayyar kiya : 
          
                a) Congress or League, Equal number of persons nominate karenge, Central Legislature me 
                
                b) Minorities ke liye, 20% seats reserved hongi. 
                
                
                
          2) Loda kuch nahi hua
          
          
          
          
          
          
       Wavell Plan
       
          
          June 1945 me Congress leaders ko jail se chora gaya. 
          
          Europe me war khatam ho chuka tha. 
          
          
          
             Wavell ne June 1945 me Shimla me conference bulwayi. 
             
              
                 i) GG and Commander-in-chief ke alawa, executive council ke sab members, Indians honge. 
                 
                ii) Hindus or Mullo ko equal representation milega. 
                
               iii) Reconstructed council, Interim gov ki tarah fucntion karegi, within the framework of 1935 act (i.e not responsible to Central Assembly)
               
                iv) GG apni veto exercise karga on advice of ministers
                
                
          
          
          
               Mulla League : 
               
                                 i) Ye chahti thi, ki sab Mulla Members, League hi nominate kare
          
                    
                    
               Congress : 
               
                                I) "An attempt to reduce the Congress to the status of a purely caste Hindu party". 
                                
                                
                Wavell's mistake : 
                
                                      Is chutiye ne breakdown of talks announce kar diya, jis se League ko virtual veto mil gaya.   Is se league ki position strength ho gayi.   Is se Jinnah ki position bhi boost hui.   
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
              September, 1945 me 1st Post-war Congress session hua----> Strong resolution was adopted , declaring congress support for the INA cause.                         
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                      
          February 1946 me 3 cabinet members ka high powered mission bheja gaya, by Atlee Gov --> Pethick Lawrence(Secy of state),   Stafford Cripps    and   A.V.Alexander
          
          
                    To find out ways and means dor a negotiated, peaceful transfer of power to India. 
          
          
                                          
                                      
                Cabinet Mission : 
                
                               1) Pakistan ki demand ko reject kar diya, because of large non-muslim population---> 38% in NW and 48% in NE. 
                               
                               2) Bengal or Punjab ko partition kar diya to deep seated regional ties disturb ho jayenge 
                               
                               3) Economic and administrative chutiyaap start ho jayega. 
                               
                               4) Armed forced ka division bhi dangerous hoga. 
                               
                               5) Existing provincial assemblies ko 3 sections me group kar diya : 
                               
                               
                                           i) Hindu Majority : Section A - Madras, Bombay, CP,  UP,   Bihar and Orissa (HIndu Majority)
                                           
                                          ii) Mulla Majority : Section B - Punjab, NWFP and Sindh
                                          
                                         iii) Mulla Majority : Section C - Bengal and Assam 
                                         
                                         
                                6) 3-tier executive and legislature at provincial, section and union levels. 
                                
                                7) Ek consti. assembly ko elect kiya jayega, by provincial assemblies by PR (Hindu, Mulla, sikh ) .
                                   Ye ek 389 membered body hogi, jisme provincial assemlies 292,  Chief Commissioner's provinces 4 or Princly states 93 membes bhejenge. 
                                   
                                8) Consti assembly me A,B,C groups ke memebers, alag alag baith ke, provinces ke liye consti banayenge, or agar possible hua to pure group ke liye hi. 
                                
                                   Fir, puri consti assembly baith ke, Union consti banayenge. 
                                   
                                9) Ek common centre, Defence, Communications or External Affairs control karega. --> India ke liye ek federal Structure hoga. 
                                
                               10) Communal questions ko dono communites ki simple majority se decide kiya jayega
                               
                               11) Provinces ke pass full autonomy and residual powers hongi. 
                               
                               12) Princly states, British paramountcy me nahi hongi.   Ye baad me successor GoIs ya British gov se agrrement kar sakti hain. 
                               
                               13) First General Election ke baad, Province ko aajadi hogi, Group se bahar aane ki, or 10 saal baad, ek province group ya union consti ke reconsideration ki baat kar sakti thi. 
                               
                               
                                               
                                                               
                                      
          
            British chodo ne kya, bouncer mara, ki sala Congress or Mulla league, dono stunned reh gaye. 
            
               Congress: Pakistan ke na ban ne se khush the,  or ek hi consti assembly ban ne wali thi, or League ko koi veto nahi diya gaya tha. 
               
               Mulla League : Pakistan to implied tha, compulsory groupings me.  
          
          
          
          
          Lekin Congress chahti thi : i) Provinces ke pass choot honi chahiye, groups na join karne ki. (COngress ke dimag me NWFP or Assa the, jaha unki sarkare thi)
          
                                     ii) Compulsory groupins against hai, Provincial Autonomy ke
                                     
                                    iii) Princly states me elected members abhi bhi nahi. 
                                    
                                    
          
                  League chahti thi : i) Groupings compulsory ho, jis se Section B or C, aage chalkar Pakistan ban sake. 
                  
                  
                  
                  
                  
            Mulla league ne 6 june ko, or Congress ne 24 june , 1946 ko Cabinet Mission plan accept kar liya. 
            
            
                  
                 July, 1946 : Consti assemblies ke liye, provinces me elections hue. 
                 
                 
                 July 10, 1946 : Nehru bolta, hum koi compulsory grouping wrooping ko nahi mante. 
                 
                 July 29, 1946 : Nehru ki baat sunke Mulla league ne apna support wapas le liye, or August 16 se "Direct action" karne ki baat ki to achieve Pakistan. 
                 
                 
                 
                 August 16, 1946 : Mullo ne wahi kiya jo ye hamesha se karte aaye hain : Dange-fassad---> Worst hit areas--> Calcutta, Bombay, Noakhali, Bihar and Garhmukteshwar(UP). 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
       Ab Wavell, Akeli Congress ko Interim Gov ka part banane ko tayyar ho gaya, Jahe, Mulla League bahar rahe.           
                 
                 Congress wale bhi mass action ki baate kar rahe the:  isliye, Nehru ke under Interim GOv ko sworn in kiya gaya.  In September, 2, 1946.     
                 
                 
                 Lekin, Wavel challaki se chupchaap, Mulla League ko bhi October 26, 1946 ko Interim Gov me le aaya.   
                 
                 
                        Interim Gov ki 14 ministries : (September 2, 1946 se August 15, 1947)
                        
                        
                           1. Jawaharlal Nehru : Executive council ka VP, External Affairs and Common Wealth Relations
                           
                           2. Vallabhbhai Patel : Home, Info and Broadcasting
                           
                           3. Baldev Singh : Defence
                           
                           4. Dr. John Mathai : Industries and Supplies
                           
                           5. C. Rajagopalachari : Education
                           
                           6. C.H.Bhabha : Works, Mines and Power
                           
                           7. Rajendra Prasad : Agri and Food
                           
                           8. Jagjivan Ram : Labour
                           
                           9. Asaf Ali : Railway
                           
                          10. Liaquat Ali khan(Mulla League) : Finance
                          
                          11. Ibrahim Ismail Chundrigar(Mulla League) : Commerce
                          
                          12. Abdur Rab Nishtar(Mulla League) : Communications
                          
                          13. Ghazanfar Ali Khan(Mulla League) : Health
                          
                          14. Jogendra Nath Mandal(Mulla League) : Law
                          
                        
                        
                        
               Consti. Assembly ki pehli meeting me league ne aane se mana kar diya : December 9, 1946
               
               Isliye, Nehru ke under 'Objective Resolution' pass hua----> Ideals, of an independent soverign republic with autonomus units, adequata minority safeguards and SEP democracy. 
               
               
               League ne Cabinet ki informal meetings attend karne se bhi mana kar diay. 
               
               
               
               
               Feb, 1947 me 9 Congress members ne Viceory ko likha ki "BC ye league ke mullo ko samjha lo, nahi to hum resign kar denge". 
               
               
               
               20th February, 1947 ko Celement Attlee ne ek announcement ki :
               
                
                   1) June 30,1948 ki deadline de di---> For transfer of power, chahe constitution bana ho ya nahi. 
                   
                   2) Muslim majority provinces ne consti assembly join na bhi ki to bhi , brits, some form of central gov ko power transfer kar denge.  Ya fir kisi provinces ko bhi. ---> Balkanisation ho sakta hai desh ka.
                   
                   3) Transfer of power hote hi, British powers and obligations, with princly states would lapse. 
                   
                   4) Wavell ki jagah Mountbatten viceroy hoga. 
               
               
               
               
          Mulla League ye statement se itna khush hue ki unhone, Punjab me Coalition governemtn ko overthrow karne ke liye, Civil disobedience movement start kar diya. 
          
          
          
      Iske baad, Sikh or Hindu groups of Punjab walo ne kaha BC, partition karo, taaki hum compulsory groupings ki wajah se Pakistan me naa chale jayen.          
               
               
               
         10th March, 1947 ko Nehru bola : Cabinet Mission best solution hai, or iske alawa==> Partition of Bengal and Punjab.       
               
               
          April, 1947 me Congress President Kripalani ne Viceroy se kaha ----> Ladne se achaa hai unhe Pakistan de dete hain, provided ki tum Bengal or Punjab ka fair tarah se partition karoge. 
          
          
          
          
          
          
      Mountbatten- Viceroy
      
         - Ye firm and Quick decision leta tha, kyunki ise jyada powers de di gayi thi    
          
          
          
          Mountbatten Plan, June 3, 1947 : 
          
          
             - Freedom-with-Partition plan
             
                i) Punjab or Bengal ki legislative assemblies 2 groups me milengi, Hindus and Muslims--> Partition par vote karne ke liye.  
                Agar dono me se kisi ek ne bhi, simple majority se partition par vote kar diya, to provinces ka partition kiya jayega. 
                
                
                
                2) Partition hone par, 2 dominions banayi jayengi, or 2 consti assemblies create ki jayengi. 
                
                3) Sindh, khudka decision lega
                
                4) NWFP or Bengal ke Sylhet district me referendum karwaya jayega
               
                5) Congress ki lagbhag saari baatein man li gayi : 
                
                    i) Princly states ko koi Independence nahi milegi---> They would either join India or Pakistan
                    
                   ii) Independece of Bengal ruled out
                   
                  iii) Hyderabad, Pakistan se nahi mil sakta
                  
                   iv) Ek boundry commission bulaya jayega, agar partition kiya jana hai to. 
                   
                   
                   
             Mountbatten ka plan tha, to divide India but retain, maximum unity. 
             
             
             
             
             
      Congress ne Dominion Status accept kyu kiya : 
      
      
         1) Is se peaceful or quick transfer of power ensure ho jata
         
         2) Congress authority chahti thi jaldi se, taki, explosive situation ko control me kiya ja sake. 
         
         3) Is se Bureaucracy or Army me continuity bani rahti.        
             
             
             
             
             
             
               - Bengal or Punjab ki legislative assemblies ne Partition ke favour me decide kiya. 
               
               - Isliye, East Bengal and West Punjab, joined Pakistan
               
               - West Bengal and East Punjab, remained with Indian Union. 
               
               - Sylhet ke referendum se wo East Bengal me mil gaya. 
               
               - 2 boundry commissions banaye gaye---> For each province
               
               - NWFP me referendum---> Decided in favour of Pakistan
               
               - Baluchistan and Sindh--> Pakistan
               
               
               
               
               
               
               
        5th July, 1947 ko British Parliament ne Indian Independece Act pass kiya, jo Mountbatten Plan par based tha. 
        
         Ise Implement kiya gaya on August 15, 1947. 
         
         
         2 Independent dominion of India and Pakistan bane, with effect from August 15. 
         
         Act ko operationalise karne ke liye, dono dominions ko ek ek Governor General diya gaya.        
               
               
               Dono ki COnstituent assemblies, Legislature ki powers exercise karne wali thi   [Existing Central Legislative Assembly and Council of States ko dissolve kar diya gaya]. 
               
               
               
         Jab tak, naye consti ban nahi jaate hian, tab tak, GoI act, 1935 ke basis pe , dono dominions ki admin chalaya jana tha. 
         
         
            - Jinnah, Pakistan ka 1st GG ban gaya, oR mountbatten India ka.      
               
               
               
               
               
        Integration of States: 
        
        
        
        1946-47 ke beech, Stat People's Movement me naya upsurge aaya---> Demanding Political Rights and Elective Representation in the Consti Assembly. 
        
        
          Nehru ne 1945 me All India State People's Conference Session ko preside kiya, jo Udaipur me hua tha. 
          
          Fir 1947 me jo Gwalior me hua tha, usko bhi. 
          
          
               
              - Bhai ne declare kar diya, ki jo states, Constituent Assembly ko Join nahi karengi, unhe Hostile ki tarah treat kiya jayega. 
              
              - July, 1947 me Patel ne Naye States Department ka charge liya. 
              
                   
                    Patel ke under, States ka Incorporation 2 phases me hua : 
                    
                    
                    
                    Phase I : 15th August, 1947 tak, saare states, except Kashmir, Hyderabad or Junagarh, Instrument of Accession par sign kar chuke the, Indian Gov ke saath. 
                    
                              Kyunki : i) Bas 3 cheeze surrender karni thi : Defence, Communication and External Affairs---> Ye teeno cheeze waise bhi unke pass kabhi thi hi nahi
                              
                                      ii) Internal Political Structure me koi change nahi kiya jana tha (Sykk)
                                      
                                      
                                      
             Phase II :  Jyada difficult process of 'Integration of States with neighbouring provinces, ya into new units like Kathiawar Union, Vindhya and MP, Rajasthan or HP, along with internal consti chnages.                   
                                      
          
          
                           Ye ek saal ke andar andar kar liya gaya. 
                           
                           Unhe Privy Purse ka bait diya gaya, or saare chodo ko Governors or Rajpramukhs bana diya gaya, in free India. 
                           
                           
                           
                           
                              
                                                                        The END
                                                                        
                                                                        
                                                                        
                                                                        
                                                                        
           Constitutional, Administrative and Judicial Developments
           
           
           
           
      Consti. Developments between 1773 and 1858 : 
      
      
         - Battle of Buxar(1764) ke baad, EIC ko Diwani mil gayi, thi Bengal, Bihar and Orissa ki.
         
         - Mughal Emperor Shah Alum II ko annual subsidy di jaani thi, or Nawab of Awad, Shujadi-Dalle-ki, ko bhi annual pension milne lagi thi      
           
         - Company ne 2 deputy dewans appoint kiye - Bengal ke liye, Mohammad Peza Reza Khan   or     Bihar ke liye, Raja Shit-Ab Raj
         
         
            - 1767 me Pehli baar EIC ne Indian Affairs me intervene kiya, jab isne 10% share manga, loot maar me---> Jo saala 40 lakh pound banta tha har saal ka. 
            
            - 1765 se 1772  tak, Dual System of Gov chalta raha, Jaha EIC ke pass authority thi, lekin koi responsibility nahi thi, or Indian chodo ke pass responsibility thi, lekin koi authority nahi thi. 
            
            
                   i) Comany ke servant bhayanak corruption kiya karte the----> Private trading kar, khub dhan kamaya, or saar paisa Bitcoin me invest kar diya. 
                   
                  ii) Peasants ke lode lag gaye, kyunki unse excessive revenue collection kiya jane laga. 
                  
                 iii) Company bankrupt ho rahi thi, lekin servants ke stock hawa me the. 
                 
             - Ye sab  se British gov was like, Dude, WTF is going on, so they decided to regulate the company affairs---> taki business me kuch order aa sake. 
             
             
      Regulating Act of 1773 : 
      
          1) Ye pehla act the, jis se British gov ne Indian affairs me mut-peshab karna shuru kiya----> To control and regulate the functioning of EIC. 
          
          2) Isne recognise kiya ki EIC ke chode, bas trade nahi kar rahe, balki Administrative or Political fields me bhi gand marwa rahe hain, Isliye, is act se Element of Centralised Administration introduce kiya gaya. 
          
          3) Company ke directors ko bola ki, apni saari correspondences regarding, Revenue affairs and civil and military admin, submit karo Governemt ko. 
              
                  (Matlab, pehli baar, British Cabinet ko right diya gaya, to exercise control over Indian Affairs)
                  
          4) Bengal me admin, ke liye Governor-General hoga or ek 4 members ki council hogi, jisme civil or military government ke representatives honge. 
          
          5) Inhe Majority rule ke hisab se function karna hoga.    Warren Hastings or 4 baaki chodo ka naam diya gaya is Act me.   Future me company appoint karegi ye chode. 
          
          6) Bengal me ek Supreme Court of judicature banai jayegi, Jiske pass original and appellate jurisdictions hongi, jaha sab subjects, redressal ke lie tareekh pe tareekh mang sakte hain. 
          
          7) GG, ke paas thodi powers hongi, Bomaby or Madras se related bhi. 
          
              Puri Scheme, Checks or balances par based thi. 
              
              
              
      1781 me is act me Amendment ki gayi : 
      
           1) Supreme Court ki jurisdiction define ki gayi :-   Within Calcutta---Ye defendant ke personal law ko adminster karegi. 
           
           2) Governments ke servants ko immunity di gayi, ki saala agar apni duty perform kart karte koi rape vape ho jaye to, koi court kachehri ka chakkar na pade. 
           
           3) Subjects ke social and Religious randaap ka dhyaan rakha jaye. 
           
           
           
           
      1784 me Pitt's India Act, pass hua : 
      
      
             1) Is act se, British Gov ko, Company Affairs ke upar kaafi saara control mil gaya. 
             
                Infact, Company subordinate kar di gayi, Department of State ke. 
                
                Company ki India me territories ko, 'British Possessions' kaha jane laga. 
                
             2) Ek Board of Control banayi gayi, jisme Chancellor of exchequer tha, Secy of State tha or Privy Council ke 4 chode bhi the(Inhe Crown appoint karne wala tha). 
             
                 Ye Company ke Civil, military or revenue affairs par control karne ke liye, banai gayi thi. 
                 
                 Saare dispatches ko Ye board approve kiya karta tha.
                 
                 Matlab, BC yaha bhi ek Dual System of Control Setup kiya gaya. 
                 
                 
             3) India me, GG ko 3 logo ki council de di gayi, jisme Commander in Chief bhi hona tha. 
             
             4) Bombay and Madras presidencies ko Governor-General ke Subordinate kar diya gaya. 
             
             5) Aggressive wars or treaties par ek general prohibition laga di gayi thi. 
             
             
             
             
     Act of 1786, labaik-Ya-Rassul-Allah
     
          1) Cornwalis the Coward, Governor-General or Commander in Chief dono ki powers chahta tha.  Is act se iski ye wish puri kar di gayi, considering isne kitne josh se Amrica me british territories ko defend kiya tha. 
          
          2) Cornwalis the coward, ko allow kar diya gaya, ki tu chahe to Coucnil ke decisions se apni gand poch sakta hai, kyunki isne bohot hi bahaduri se America me British forts ko defend kiya tha. 
          
             ( Baat me ye over-riding powers sab GGs ko di jaane lagi )
             
             
             
          
    Charter Act of 1793 
    
        1) Company ke commercial privileges ko 20 saalo ke liye, renew kar diya gaya. 
        
        2) Comapany, apne kharche pure karke(Dividends, Interests, Salaries, Expenses etc),  British Sarkar ko har saal 5 lakh pounds diya karegi. 
        
        3) Governor General, Governors, Commander-in-chief ki appointment ke liye, Royal Approval Chahiye hoga. 
        
        4) Company ke senior officials ko bol diya, ki saala Khabardaar koi bina permission ke India chor ke gaya to.   Ye saale, Nepali rande chodne nikal jaya karte the. 
        
        5) Company ko power de di, licenses issue karne ki--Katto ke liye nahi chutiye,   India me trade karne ke liye, to it's employees and other Indians. 
        
           In licences ko 'Privilege' ya 'COuntry trade' bulate the, or inhi ki madad se inhone, China ko Smakiya bana diay, opium pila pilake. 
           
        6) Judiciary se Revenue Admin ko seperate kar diy, is wajas se 'Maal Adalats' band ho gayi. 
        
        7) Home Government members ko Indian revenues se tankhwa milne lagi, or ye randaap, 1919 tak chala.
        
        
        
        
        
    Charter Act of 1813
    
    
          1) 'Laissez-Faire' ki spirit me or Napolean ke Continental system se hue nuksaan ki wajah se British chode, ye demand karne lage ki saala Comapnay ko di hui monopoly ko hataya jaye. 
          
          2) Company's monopoly over trade in India was stopped except: Trade with China and Trade in Tea. 
          
          3) Company ke shareholders ko 10.5 % dividends diya gaya, India ki revenue ka. 
          
          4) 20 or saalo tak, Comapny ko possessions , Territories and revenues in India, extend kar di gayi. 
          
                (Consti. position of British Territories in India was defined explicity for the 1st time)
                
                
          5) Board of Control ki powers ko or bhi enlarge kar diya gaya. 
          
          6) Indians ki education ke liye, 1 lakh rupees set aside karo
          
          7) Councils of Madras, Bombay and Bengal ke jo bhi regulations hain, wo sab ab British Parliament ke aage rakhe jaane lage.      
                         
          8) Commercial transactions or Territorial Revenues ke liye, Separate accounts bana. 
          
          9) Christian missionaries ko allow kar diya, to go and preach their religion. 
          
          
          
          
    Charter Act of 1833
    
          1) Company ki lease 20 saal or extend kar di gayi.      India ki territories ko Crown ke naam par govern kiya jana tha. 
          
          2) Trade with China and Trade in Tea ko wali monopolies ko bhi hata diya gaya. 
          
          3) Europeans ke upar lage hue saare restrictions hata diye gaye, jaao or India me properites khareedo. 
          
          4) India me Financial, Legislative and Admininstrative centralisation of Government was envisaged : 
          
              i) GG ko power de di gayi to superintend, control and direct all civil and military affairs of the company. 
              
             ii) Bengal, Madras and Bomaby or baaki sab territories ko Governor-General ke complete control ke under kar diya gaya. 
             
            iii) Saare revenues, GG ke authority se collect kiye jaane lage, or GG ke pass expenditure ka bhi complete control tha. 
            
             iv) Madras or Bombay se unki legislative powers cheen li gayi. 
             
          5) GG ki council me ek Law member ko add kiya gaya, taki law-making par professional advice diya ja sake. 
          
          6) Indian laws ko codify and consolidate kiya jana tha. 
          
          7) Religion, Color, birth, descent ke basis par Kisi Indian Citizen ko employment deny nahi ki jayegi. 
          
          8) Slavery khatam karo---> It was abolished in 1843. 
          
          
          
          
    Charter Act of 1853. 
    
       1) Jab tak parliament kuch na bataye, tab tak territories, company ke pass hi rahengi. 
       
       2) Court of Directors ki strength ko kam karke 18 kar diya gaya. 
       
       3) Open Competitive examination for services
       
       4) Law member, koi GG's executive council ka full member bana diya gaya. 
       
       5) 6 naye members add kiye gaye for Legislative purposes---> is se GoI ke Executive or Legislative functions ka seperation ho gaya. 
       
       6) Indian Legislature me local representation ko introduce kiya gaya.     Legislative wing ko Indian Legislative council kaga jane laga. 
       
       7) Lekin, kanoon ko GG ki assent chahiye hoti thi, or GG chahe to law ko veto bhi kar sakta tha. 
       
       
       
       
       
   Act of Better Governemnt of India, 1858 
   
   
    - India ko Crown ke naam pe govern kiya jayega, through a Secy of State and a Council of 15.   Council was advisory in nature. 
    
        (Ise se Pitt's India act wala dual system khatam ho gaya)
        
        
    - GG, Viceroy ban gaya. 
    
  
  
  
  
  
  
  Consti. Developments after 1858 till 1947. 
  
  
  
       Indian Councils Act, 1861
       
       
          1) Legislature ko ek seperate function ki tarah treat kiya jane laga.    Non-officials ko legislative bodies me representation di jaane lagi. 
          
          2) Canning ke portfolio system, se Cabinet government ki foundation rakhi gayi.    Har branch ka khudka ek official head hota tha or GoI me ek spoksman, jo uske admin ke liye responsible hota tha. 
          
          3) Gov, of Bombay or Madras ko unki legislative powers lota di gayi. 
  
          4) Lekin is act se jo Legislative councils bani, unke pass koi jyada powers nahi thi---> Wo important matters discuss nahi kar sakti thi, or Financial matters se to unhe 10 kadam door rakha jaat tha. 
            
            Budget par koi control nahi tha,   Koi executive actions ko discuss bhi nahi kar sakte the,    Sab bill karne ke liye, Viceroy ki approval chahiye hoti thi.     Or saala agar Approval mil bhi jaye to Secy of state, loda ada deta tha.    
                Or JIn Indians ko as non-official members ke liye, appoint kiya gaya wo sab elite sections ke memebers the. 
                
                
                
                
      Indian Councils Act, 1892
      
      
          1) Ye congress ko khush karne ke liye laya gaya tha---> Taaki, councils me non-official members ka number badhaya jaa sake. 
          
          2) Indian legislative council ko enlarge kiya gaya. 
          
          3) Principal of Representation introduce kiya gaya : Zamindars, Universities, District Boards, Muncis, Trade Bodies, Chambers of Commerce wagarh ko bola ki Provincial councils me apne members recommend kare. 
          
          4) Indirect election ka ek element introduce kiya gaya--- Non-Official members ke selection me. 
          
          5) Leislatures ko allow kiya gaya, financial statements par apne views dene ko.
          
          6) Kuch questions puchne ke liye bhi allow kar diya gaya. 
          
        
        
                   
  
  
  
  
   Evolution of Civil Services in India
   
   
       1) Cornwallis the Coward(1786-93) 
       
            - Ye pehla banda tha, jisne Civil Services ko existence me laya or organise kiya. 
            
            - Isne Corruption check karne ki koshis ki : 
            
                i) Civil Servants ki salaries badhake
                
               ii) Private trade karne walo ki nasbandi karwake
               
              iii) Presents, Bribes lene wale Civil Servants ki bhi nasbandi karwake
              
               iv) seniority based promotion
               
               
      2) Wellesley The Fuckboy (1798-1805) 
      
             - 1800 me Fort Williams College banaya, Naye recruits ki training ke liye. 
             
             - Lekin Court of Directors ke chodo ne 1806 me Wellesly ke college ko disapprove kar diya. 
             
                 - Haileybury me East India College setup kiya gaya. 
                 - Recruits ko 2 saal ki training di jaati thi
                 
      3) Charter Act of 1853
      
             - Company ki patronage cheen li gayi, or Open competition ke through Civil servants ki recruitment ki jaane lagi. 
             
             - Lekin Indias ko barred kiya hua tha, high posts occupy karne se.     
                 
                            
  
     4) Indian Civil Services Act, 1861
     
             - Is act se, Conveneanted Civil servants ke liye, kuch offices reserved kar diye gaye.    Lekin, Examination, England me jaake deni padti thi, wo bhi English me, based on Classical Greek and Latin. 
             
             - Maximum permissible age ko reduce kar 22 kar diya gaya tha, 1860 me, fir 21 in 1866 me or BC 1878 me to bas 19 saal kar diya gaya tha. 
             
             - 1863 me Satyendra Nath Tagore, pahle Indian the jisne Indian Civil Service ke liye Qualify kiya. 
             
             
             
             
      5) Statutory Civil Service
       
           - 1878-79 me Lytton ne Statutory Civil Services introduce ki----> 1/6th of covenanted posts ko Ache gharo ke Indians ke liye, through nomination by local Govs. 
           
           - Lekin ye ghatiya system fail ho gaya, or abolish kar diya gaya. 
           
 
           
           
           
           
      6) Indian National Congress ne Demands ki :  
          
                   i) Sala age limit theek karo
                   
                  ii) Examination India me bhi karwao
                  
                       
           
          Isliye, Defferin ne --> Aitchison Committe on Public Services(1886) banai. 
          
              
                   - 'Covenanted' or 'uncovenanted' wala randaap khatam kar diya gaya
                   
                   - Imperial Indian Civil Services(Examination in England) and Provincial Civil Services(Examination in India)  and Subordinate Civil Services(Ye bhi India me)  ----> In teen me classify kar diya
                   
                   - Age limit badha ke 23 kar di gayi.
