            1893 me , House of commons ne ek resolution pass kiya, jis se simultaneous India or England me examination hone lage======> Lekin, Bhadwo ne ye resolution kabhi implement hi nahi kiya. 
            
            
                   
                   
                    
           
                   
     7) Montford Reforms(1919) 
     
           1) "Agar India me responsible Gov laani hai, to Public service me jitne jyada Indians ho utna acha hai". 
           
           2) Simultaneous Exams hone chahiye India or England me. 
           
           3) 1/3rd Recruitments India me hi honi chahiye===> Har saal 1.5% se badha dena chahiye. 
           
           
           
     8) Lee Commission (1924) 
     
     
            1) Secy of State ko hi, ICS, Engineering Services ki Irrigation branch or Indian Forest Services ke officers recruit karte rehne chahiye. 
            
            2) Education or Civil Medical Services jaise Transfered fields ke liye, Civil Servants ki bharti, Provincial Governments ko de deni chahiye
            
            3) 15 saal ke andar andar ICS ki direct recruitment se 50:50 European-Indian Parity achieve kar li jani chaiye
            
            4) Public Service Commission banao, jitna jaldi ho sake. 
            
            
     9) GoI Act, 1935
     
        
            1) Federal or Provincial Public Service commissions banao. 
            
        
        
        
        
                     
                    
             
     Modern India me Police System ka Evolution 
     
     
     
     
       - Pre-Colonial times ki Govs, Autocratic hua karti thi----> Inke pass koi alag se Formal Police system nahi tha. 
       
       - Lekin, gaanvo ko raat me protect karne ke liye, Watch guards hua karte the. 
       
       - Mughals ke time me, Faujdaar hua karte the, to maintain Law and Order
       
       - Inke alawa Amils hua karte the, jinka kaam to revenue collection tha, lekin ye rebels wagarh se bhi lode lete rehte the. 
       
       - Cities me Kotwal hota tha law and order ke liye. 
       
       - 1766 se 1772 ke beech Dual rule ke time bhi, Zamindars hi thanedar rakha karte the, law and order duties ke liye. 
       
       - 1770 me 'Faujdaar' or 'Amils' wala randaap khatam kar diya gaya. 
       
       - Lekin, 1774 me Warren Hastings ne sasti charas ke nashe me, Faudar ke institution ko restore kar diya, or Zamindars ko bola, ki saalo sab milke, daaku logo se lado, jao. 
       
       - 1775 me, Bhai ne sasta nasha nahi chora, or Major towns me 'Faujdar thanas' establish kiye---> Or inhe assist karne ke liye, chote-chote, nanhe munhe police stations banaye. 
       
       
       
       
    1791 : Cornwallis the Coward ne Regular Police Force Start kiya, to maintain law and order-----> Isne, Purana, Thanas(Circle) wala system hi revive kiya tha.----> District me thana hota tha under a Daroga. 
    
           Head of District hota tha--> Superintendent of Police (SP)
           
           Isne Zamindars, ko bola ki bhai tumse naa ho payegaa, or aayenda koi police duty karta hua dikha to uski maa chod di jayegi. 
           
           
           
           
    1808 : Mayo naam ke Mayo khane wale chode ne,   Har division ke SP appoint kiya or unki help karne ke liye, 'Goyendas' naam ke jassus appoint kiye.   Lekin ye Goyendas, logo ke saath Goyenda-Gardi karte the. 
    
    
    1814 : Court of Directors ke ek order se, Bengal ke alawa jaha jaha company ki possessions thi, waha sabse, Darogas ki appointment + Unke subbordinates, ko abolish kar diya gaya. 
    
    
    
    1828-35, Bentick ke time :   Is bhai ne to SP ke office ko hi abolish kar diya. 
    
                                - Collector/Magistrate ko hi apni jurisdiction me Police Force head karne ko bola. 
                                
                                - Har division me jo Commissioner tha, abse wahi SP ki tarah act karega. 
                                
                                - Lekin is se bohot hi ghatiya tarah se organised police force bani---> Collector/Magistrate par bhayanak burden aa gaya. 
                                
                                
                                
     1860 me ek Police Commission bani, or uski recommendation se---> 1861 me Indian Police Act pass hua. 
     
            1) Civil Constabulary ka ek system ho---> Jis se Existing village setup hi maintained rahe(Har village ka apna watchman)---> Lekin ye direct relationship me ho, with the rest of Constabulary. 
            
            2) Province ka head hoga- Inspector General,     Range ka head hoga- Deputy Inspector General      and     District ka head hoga- SP. 
            
            
            3) Is se, police ne Daaku or Thug logo ki maa chodna shuru kar diya. 
            
               Lekin, logo se ache se baat nahi karte the. 
               
               Or, National Movement ko kuchalne me bhi inhone  aham bhumika nibhai. 
               
            4) British logo ne koi All-India Police Service nahi banai. 
            
            5) Is act ki guidelines se bas Provinces ke liye Police Setup present hua tha. 
            
            
            
     1902 me ek or Police commission bana
     
          1) CID establish karo, provinces me 
          
          2) Central Intelligence Bureau banao, Centre me 
          
          
          
          
          
          
          
          
    Military Under the British 
    
     
       - Company rule ki backbone thi Military
       
       - 1857 revolt se pehle, 2 seperate tarah ki military force operate kiya karti thi, British Control ke under. 
       
              i) Queen's Army : Serving Troops on Duty in India---> Ye Crown's Military force ka part the
              
             ii) Company's Troops : Mixture of European and Indian Regiments(Recruited locally but with British officers)
             
       - 1857 ke baad--> Revolt ko prevent karne ke liye, Army ko reorganise kiya gay. 
       
                         Indian army ka use karke, Empire ka defence kiya jana tha, baaki imperialist powers se-- Russia, Germany, France etc
                         
                         Indian branch of Army se Asia or Africa me expanision kiya jana tha
                         
                         British Section ka use kiya jana tha as an Army of occupation
                               
                   
              1) Dominatio of European branch over Indian Branch was ensured. 
              
              2) 1859 or 1879 ke commissions ne insist kiya tha: 1/3rd White army(is se pehle bas 14% the) 
              
              3) Proportion of Euro:Indian---> i) 1:2 in Bengal Army     ii) 2:5 in Madras and Bombay army
              
              4) Strict European monopoly over, key geographical locations and departments such as Artillery, tanks and Armed corps was maintained. 
              
              5) 1900 tak Indians ko jo rifles bhi di jaati thi, wo inferior quality ki hoti thi. 
              
              6) 2nd WW tak, Indians ko kisi High-Tech department me bhi nahi allow kiya jata tha. 
              
              7) Officer Ranks me bhi Indians ko allow nahi kiya jata tha. 
              
              8) 1914 tak, Indian banda, highest Subedar hi ban sakta tha.  (1918 ke baad se commissioned ranks me Indians ko allow kiya jane laga)
              
              9) 1926 me Indian Sandhurst committee visualize kar rahi thi 50% Indianised Officer Cadre for 1952 (LoL)
              
              
              
       Lord Roberts ke under, 1880s ke baad se, 'Martial Race' wala chutiyaap bhi start kar diya gaya. 
       
           - Sikhs, Gurkhas and Pathans---> Jinhone Revolt crush kiya tha---> Martial Race
           
           - Awadh, Bihar, Central India and South India wale--> Jinhone revolt kiya tha--> Non-Martial 
           
           
              Sab Regiments me Caste and communal companies introduce ki gayi. 
              
              
              Newspapers, Journals, Nationalist publications wagarh pe ban lagake, Soldiers ko Rest of India se isolate kiya jata tha. 
              
              
              
                     
              
              
              
    Development of Judiciary in British India
    
    
      - Common law system, based on Judicial precedents start hua tha----> 1726 me jab, Bombay, Madras and Calcutta me 'Mayor's courts' banaye gaye the, by EIC. 
      
      
      - Jab, EIC ek Trading company se Ruling power ban gayi, to Existing Mughal legal system ko naye Judicial system se replace kar diya gaya. 
      
      
      
   Warren Hastings(1772-85) ke under reforms : 
   
      1) Districts me civil disputes ka niptara karne ke liye, District Diwani Adalats banai gayi. 
      
            Inhe Collector ke under rakha gaya tha.----> Dindus ke liye, Dindu law   and Mullas ke liye Mulla qanoon. 
            
            District Diwani Adalat se appeal jaati thi, Sadar Diwani Adalat------> Ye function karti thi ek President ke under, or Supreme Council ke 2 members ke under. 
            
            
      2) Or Criminal Disputes ka niptara karne ke liye, District Fauzdari Adalats banai gayi----> Inhe Indian officer ke under rakha gaya, jinhe assist karte the, Mufti or Qazis. 
      
            Ye adalats bhi, Collector ki general Supervision ke under hoti thi. 
            
            Mulla qanoons ko Fauzdari adalats me nipta jaata tha. 
            
            Kisi ki gardan utarne ke liye, ya Property confiscate karne ke liye, Mushidabad me located, Sadar Nizamat Adalat se permision leni padti thi-----> Ise Deputy Nizam head karta tha(Indian Mulla) 
            
            
            
            
      3) 1773 ke regulating act se--> Calcutta me ek Supreme court banai gayi-----> Ise allowed tha Calcutta ke saare British Subjects ke cases niptara karne ka. 
      
          Iske pass original and appellate jurisdictions thi. 
          
          Lekin kafi baar, iski jurisdictions baaki courts se clash kiya karti thi. 
          
          
          
                
      4) Cornwallis the Coward (1786-93) ke under reforms            
               
          
             - District Fauzdari Adalts ko abolish kar diya gaya. ------> Inki jagah, Calcutta, Dacca, Murshidabad or Patna me Circuit courts establish kiye gaye. 
             
             - In circuit courts me European judges hua karte the-----> Ye dono, civil or criminal cases ke liye, COurt of appeals ki tarah kaam kiya karte the. 
             
             - Sadar Nizamat Adalat ko Calcutta me shift kar diya gaya----> or Governor General(Yanhi khud coward ke under) ke under kar diya gaya-----> Ise CHief qazi or Chief mufti assist kiya karte the. 
             
             - District diwani Adalat ko ab----> District, City ya zila courts ke naam se bulaya jaane laga----> Inhe district judge ke under place kar diya gaya. 
             
             - Collector se uski Magisterial powers cheen li gayi, ab uske pass bas revenue admin ki responsibility thi. 
             
             
             - Matlab Civil Courts ka level tayyar ho gaya : 
             
                  i) Munsiff's court under Indian officers
                  
                 ii) Registrar's court under a European Judge
                 
                iii) District court under the district judge
                
                 iv) 4 circuit courts as provincial courts of appeal
                 
                  v) Sadar Diwani Adalat at Calcutta
                  
                 vi) King-in-Council for 5000 pounds se jyada ki appeal ke liye
                 
                 
           Cornwallis the coward's code : 
           
               1) Revenue or Justice Administration ka seperation 
               
               2) European subjects ko bhi jursidiction ke under laya gaya
              
               3) Government officials ko answerable bana diya gaya, Civil courts ke for actions done in their official capacity
               
               4) Principle of Sovereignity of law was established
               
               
               
               
      William Bentinck ke under ke Reforms( 1828-33) 
      
      
          1) 4 circuits court ko abolish kar diya gaya---> Inke functions ko transfer kar diya gaya collectors ko, under the supervision of commissioner of Revenue and circuit. 
          
          2) Allahbad me Sadar Diwani Adalat and sadar Nizamat Adalat were setup
          
          3) Courts me ab Persian ki jagah English use ki jaa sakti thi, or Supreme Court me English language replaced Persian
          
        
        
        1833: Macaulay ke under law commission setup kiya gaya---> Indian Laws ko codify karne ke liye.
         
            Is wajah se : 1859 me Civil Procedure Code
            
                          1860 me Indian Penal Code                  laye gaye
                                                     
                          1861 me Criminal Procedure Code            
                     
      
      
      
        1860:   Criminal Cases ke alawa, Gore koi special privileges claim nahi kar sakte. Lekin, Indian Orgin ka koi judge, unhe try nahi kar sakta
        
        1865:   Supreme Court or  Sadar Adalats ko merge karke, 3 high courts banai gyi a Calcutta, Bombay and Madras
        
        1935:   GoI act---> Federal Court(jise banaya gaya 1937 me) ---> Could settle disputes bw Governments and could hear limited appeal from High Courts. 
        
        
        
        
    Evaluation: 
    
        Positives aspects: 
        
              i) Rule of Law was established
              
             ii) Codified laws ne religious or rulers ke personal laws ko replace kar diya
             
            iii) European subjects ko bhi jurisdiction ke under le aaya gaya.  (Lekin, Sala criminal cases me inhe bas European Judges hi try kar sakte the)
            
             iv) Govt servants ko Civil courts ke samne answerable bana diya gaya. 
             
         Negaticve ASpects : 
         
         
                i) Judicial system bhayanka complicated or expansive ho gaya---> ameer log, system ko manipulate kar sakte the. 
                
               ii) False evidence, Deceit karne ka ample scope tha
               
              iii) Dragged out litigation---> Delayed justice
              
               iv) Jaise jaise litigations badhe, Courts overburdened ho gayi. 
               
                v) Bohot baar, Gore judges, Familiar hi nahi hote the, Indian usages or traditions ke. 
                
                
                
                
                
                    
        
        
             
          
    Major Changes in Adminstrative Structure after 1857
    
    
       New Stage of Colonialism : 
       
        
           - Masses ki administration se alienation kam karna jaroori tha, taaki sala dobara revolt na kar de chutiye log. 
           
           - Natives, ko administration se associate karke, rulers ko bhi natives ke baare me kaafi kuch pata chal jayega. 
           
           - 1850s ke baad se or bhi nayi colonial powers aa gayi---> Raw material, Market for mfg goods and capital investment, ke liye, cut-throat competition chal raha tha.              
                                          
           - Finance or Mfg goods ke trade me british supremacy khatam ho gayi thi. 
           
           - Is stage pe, kaafi large scale British Capital investment hua Indian railways or Loans to GoI.   Smaller extent me, Tea-plantations, Coal-mining, jute mills, shipping, trade or banking me bhi. 
           
           - In sab factors ke combination se start hua : New Stage of colonialism in India.                      
    
    
    
    
    
           
      Lord Mayo ne 1870 me, khub saara nasheela mayo khaya jis wajah se use Indians par daya aa gayi : --->Central Revenues se Fixed sum of money, for Admin of Certain services like Police, Jails, Education, medical, roads etc      
           
           to Provincial Governments. -------> Ye pehla step tha, to bifurcate, Central and Provincial Finances. 
           
              Provincial Governments ko fir mayo ne bola, "dekho bhai, ye paise pakdo, or jaise use karna hai karo". 
              
              
       Lord Lytton ne 1877 me thode or Heads of Expenditure, jaise ki Land revenue, Excise, Genreal Admin, law and justice etc Transfer kar diye, Provinces ko. 
       
       
       1882 me Sab sources of Revenues ko 3 groups me divide kiya gaya---> General(jo bas centre ko jaate the), Provincial(Jo bas provinces ko jaate the) and those which were divided bw Centre and Provinces. 
       
       
       
       
              
    Local Bodies : 
    
           
          Local bodies ko promote kar admin. ko decentralise karne ka plan banaya gaya. ----> Through Muncis and District Boards. ----> Ye local services admin karne wale the jaise ki--> Education. Health, Sanitation, Water Supply, Roads or baaki basic amenities. ---> Jinhe Local taxes se hi finance kiya jana tha. 
          
          
            Lekin kyu ? 
            
               a) Overcentralisation ki wajah se Central Govs financial difficulties face kar rahi thi
               
               b) Europr me is time, civic amenities me bhayank teji se modern advances ho rahe the. 
               
               c) Nationalists chode, Basic facilities ko apna agenda banaye hue the. 
               
               d) Indians ko admin. me bharti kar, Indians ki increasing politicisation ko check kiya ja sakta tha. 
               
               e) Local taxation se local welfare se ek to criticism bachta dusra, ameer bhadwo ki jeb par bhi sankat na ata(Through increased taxes)
               
               
       1864 or 1868 ke beech : Pehli baar local bodies banai gayi---> lekin in sabme nominated members the, or inhe District magistrate head karta tha. 
        
                               Inhe bas additional tax collection ka jariya samjhte the log. 
                               
                               
                               
                               
              Mayo ne Nashe wala Mayo khake, 1870 me resolution kiya : -    Imperial Gov se annual grant milega. 
                         
                                                                            Provincial Govs ko bhi authorise kiya jata hai, local taxation se apna budget balance karlo. 
                                                                            
                                                Aisa kiya gaya, kyunki, is se pehle kai saare departments, provinces ko transfer kiye ja chuke the- jaise ki medical services, roads, education
                                                
                                                Ye shuruat thi, local finances ki.                          
                 
                 
                                Nashedi Mayo khane wale Mayo ki baato me aakar---> Bengal, Madras, MWFP, Punjab ki provincial govs ne, Munci acts pass kiye, is policy ko implement karne ke liye. 
                 
                 
                 
                 
              Ripon's Resolution of 1882 : "Mayo bhai ne jo financial decentralisation, apni gov se tumhari(Provinvial Govs) or kiya tha, ab wahi kaam tum log local bodies ki taraf karo"                
       
                - Is contribution ki wajah se Ripon ko- "Father of Local-Self Government in India" kaha jata hai. 
                
                
                         1) Local Bodies ka development karo, taki admin improve ho, or ye as an instument of political and popular education ban sake. 
                         
                         2) Local affairs ko Urban and local bodies ki madad se hi administer kiya jaye---> Jinke pass definite duties ho, or suitable source of revenue bhi ho
                         
                         3) Non-Officials majority me ho, in bodies me----> Jinhe elect bhi kiya ja sakta hai, agar, officials ko lage ki elections karwaye ja sakte hain, to
                         
                         4) Non-Officials hi in bodies ke chairpersons honge. 
                         
                         5) Official interference bilkul minimum honi chahiye
                         
                         6) Kuch cases me executive sanctions chahiye ho : Loans lene ke liye,  Naye taxes impose karne ke liye,  Rules or Bye-laws frame karne ke liye, Ek amount se jyada ke kharche karne ke liye
                         
                    
                  In sab rules ko follow karne ke liye, kaafi saare acts pass kiye gaye---> 1883 or 1885 ke beech----> Inse Munci bodies, ki consti, powers or functions kaafi had tak change ho gaye. 
                  
                  
                     Lekin, In bodies ke bhi bhayanak drawbacks the : 
                     
                        1) Elected members, laghbhag har district me minority me the
                        
                        2) Franchise kaafi limited thi
                        
                        3) District boards par ab bhi district officials hi kabja kiye hue the
                        
                        4) Govt ka jab man aata tab, wo in bodies ko suspend ya supersede kar deti. 
                        
                        
        Infact, Curzon chode ne to Local Bodies par offical control badhane ke liye, kai steps liye. 
        
        
        
        
        
  1908 ki Royal Commission on Decentralisation : 
  
  
      - Financial resources ki kami, sabse bada stumbling block hai, in local bodies ke effective fucntioning ke. 
      
      - Recommendations : 
      
             i) Village panchayats ko or powers do, jaise chote mote cases niptane ki,  Minor village works, Schools, Fuel and fodder,  ke liye kharche badha do.
             
            ii) Panchayats ko adequate source of income do
            
           iii) Sab talukas and tehsils me, Sub-district boards establish karo, jinki alag duties or alag income ke sources ho
           
            iv) Existing taxations par lage restrictions hatao, Regular grant-in-aids do, large projects undertake karne ke liye
            
             v) Muncis ko bolo ki saala Primary education ki responsibility tumhari, or ho sake to middle-vernacular school ki bhi. 
             
             
             
             
     Govt. Of India Resolution of 1915 :
     
         Decentralisation Commission ke recommendations ke upar jo officials ki soch thi, wo is act me contained thi. 
         
         Lekin loda kuch implement nahi hua, or jaisa Ripon bhai chorke gaye, the, waisa hi sab chalta raha. 
         
         
         
     May, 1918 ka resolution: 
     
          20 August, 1917 wali announcement ko dhyan me rakhte hue, Local self-govs ko puri tarah se question kiya. 
          
             - "Local bodies ko jitna jyada representative banaya ja sakta hai utna achaa hai". 
             
             
             
     Dyarchy ke under: 
     
          - Transferred subject tha, under popular ministerial control. 
          
          - Har province ko allow kiya, local self-govs instis  develop karne ko according to provincial needs and requirements. 
          
                                     
          - Lekin, bhadwo ne, Finance ko 'Reserved' subject bana rakha tha--Under the charge of executive councillor, to Indian ministers loda kuch nahi ukhaad paye. 
          
          
          
          
          Simon Commission (May 1930) ne bhi bola ki kya ghatiya progress hui hai, except in UP, Bengal and Madras. 
          
          
            -  In chutiyo ne recommend kiya- Increasing provincial control over local bodies for the sake of efficiency. 
            
            - Inhone ye bhi bola ki, saale elected members koi naye local taxes nahi lagate hain
          
                or, ki 1919 ke reforms introduce hone ke baad, local bodies ki financial situation or bhi ghatiya ho gayi hai. 
                
                
                
         1935 ka GoI act       
          
          
            - Provincial Autonomy se local self-govs ki development ko bal mila. 
            
            - Ab funds bhi milne lage. 
            
            - Provinces me naye acts pass kiye gaye, jinse local bodies ko jyada authority mil sake. 
            
           
          
          
          
          
          
                
        
  Labour log : 
  
  
     - Maje ki baat : Lancashire Textile capitalist lobby ne , demand kiya pehli baar India ki factories me Condition of workers ko regulate kiya jaye. 
     
     
     - Pehla Commission appoint hua, 1875 me, or Pehla act pass hua 1881 me. 
     
     
           1) Indian Factory act of 1881 : Primarily Child labour se nipatne ke liye: 
           
                     - 7 se kam ke bacho ki employment par rok
                     
                     - Bachoo ke liye working hours restricted to 9 hours only
                     
                     - Bachoo ko har mahine kam se kam 4 din ka avkaash
                     
                     - Hazardous machinery ko properly fence off karke rakho
                     
                     
                     
           2) Indian Factory act of 1891 :
           
                      - 7 nahi 9 saal
                      
                      - 9 nahi 7 ghante
                      
                      - Aurat ke liye 11 ghante
                      
                      - Hafte wale din, sabke liye chutti
                      
              
            Lekin or bhi jyada maje ki baat : Inme se koi bhi kanoon, British-owned tea and coffee plantations par nahi apply hota tha=====> Jaha labour ko ruthlessly exploit kiya jata tha.                        
     
     
             
             
                   
       
       
       
       
       
       
    Development of India Press : 
    
    
       - James Augustus Hickey ne 1780 me Bengal Gazette a.k.a Calcutta General Advertiser start kiya---> It was the 1st Newspaper in India. 
       
           Ise 1872 me seize kar liya gaya.    
       
       
       
       
        Censorship of Press Act, 1799
        
          - Wellesly the Fuckboy ko lag raha tha ki French Invasion honi wali hai India me . 
          
          - Is chode ne laghbhag, wartime, press restrictions impose kar diye, including pre-censorship. 
          
          - In restrictions ko Lord Hastings, ke under relax kiya gaya. Ye thoda progressive banda tha. 
          
          
        Licensing Regulations, 1823
        
           - John Adams---> Reactionary choda--->    i) Starting or using a press without a licence was a penal offence. 
           
                                                    ii) Baad me is act ko extend kar--> To cover journals, Pamphlets and books 
                                                    
                                                   iii) Mainly Indian language newspapers ke against thi
                                                   
                                                    iv) RamMohan roy ke Mirat-ul-Akbar ko publication rokni padi. 
                                                    
                                                    
           - 1835 ke Press Act or Metcalfe Act
           
                                                                                  
                     - Metcalfe bhai ne us Chutiye John Adams wale ordinance ko hataya-----> He became "Liberator of the Indian Press"
                     
                     - Bas ye bata do kis jagah pe publish kar rahe ho.
                     
                     Iske baad se rapid growth hua, Newspapers ka. 
                     
                     
                     
          - 1856 ka licensing Act : 
          
               Licencing restrictions in addition to the already existing registration procedure laid down by Metcalfe Act. 
               
               
               
          - 1867 ka Registration act : 
          
          
                 Ye bas regulatory nature ka tha, na ki restrictive nature ka. Isne 1835 ke Metcalfe act ko replace kiya.      
               
               
                         
           
          
          
          
          
   EDUCATION
   
   
              
       1) Warren Hastings ne 1781 me Calcutta Madrasah khola----> Sharia padhne ke liye
       
       2) Jonathan Duncan ne Benaras me Sanskrit College khola--> Hindu law and philosophy padhne ke liye
       
       3) Fuckboy ne 1800 me Fort Williams college khola---> Civil Servants ko train karne ke liye
       
       4) Macaulay's Minute - 1835---> Anglicists jet gaye---> Western science and literature through the medium of English Language alone
       
       5) James Thomson, NWFP ka Lt.Governor the---> 1843 se 1853 tak----> Comprehensive scheme of village education develop ki---> Through medium of vernacular languages.    
          
       6) 1854 ka wood's dispatch : Magna Carta of English Education in India         
                                     
                       - GoI ko bola to assume the responsibilty of education of masses. 
                       
                       - Bottom pe, Vernacular primary schools in villages --->Anglo-Vernacular High Schools + ek affiliated college at district level---> Affiliating Uni in presidency towns of Calcutta, Bombay and Madras           
          
                       - Higher Studies ke liye, English as the medium of instruction      and     Vernaculars at school level
                       
                       - Female and vocational education par stress and on teacher's training
                       
                       - private enterprise ko encourage karne ke liye system of grant-in-aid
                       
                       
                       
                       
           1857 me Calcutta, Bombay and Madras me Universities banai gayi----> Fir, Dept. of educations banaye gaye, sab provinces me. 
           
           
           1849 me Bethune schools aaye---> Girl Education ke liye. 
           
           
           
           Bihar ki Pussy, sorry Pusa me Agriculture Institute bana
           
           Roorkee me Engineering Institute.      
          
          
          
          
          
     1857 ke baad education : 
     
        
          1882 ki Hunter Education Commission : 
          
              - Purani Schemes ne Primary or Secondary educations ko neglect kiya tha. 
              
              - Jab 1870 me(Mayo bhai ki jai) Education ko provinces ko shift kiya gaya, to primary or secondary education ko or bhi lode lag gaye. 
              
              - 1882 me, Ripon bhai ne W.W. Hunter ki chairmanship ke under, ek commission appoint ki---> To review the progress of education in country since the Despatch of 1854. 
              
              - Hunter report, bas primary or secondary education tak hi confined thi. 
              
                  i) Primary education me State ki special care ki jarurat hai
                  
                 ii) Primary education, vernacular me hi di jaani chahiye
                 
                iii) Primary education ka control, abhi abhi bane, District or Munci boards ko de dena chahiye. 
                
                 iv) Secondary(High School) education ke 2 divisions hone chahiye : 
                  
                       a) Literary---> Leading up to University
                       
                       b) Vocational --> For commercial careers. 
                       
                 v) Female education ki females ki tarah hi chudi padi hai. ---> Specially presidency towns ke bahar to bura hi haal hai. 
                 
                      
         Agle 2 decades me, Secondary and collegiate education ka rapid growth hua. 
         
         Jyada teaching-CUM-Examining Universities were setup like the Punjab University(1882) and Allahabad University(1887). 
         
         
         
         
         
   1904 wala Indian University Act :
   
       20th CE khatam hone ke saath saath political unrest start ho gaya tha. 
       
       Officials sochne lage the ki saala, ye Private mgmt ke under quality of education ghatiya ho gayi hai. 
       
       Or ye educational instis na hua, balki factories hain, jo political revolutionaries produce kar rahi hain. 
       
       
          Nationalists bhi mante the ki, education ki quality me decline aaya hai. ----> Lekin, GoI bhi loda kuch nahi kar rahi illetracy kam karne ke liye. 
          
          
             1902 me University students ke lene ke liye, Raleigh Commission setup kiya gaya. 
             
               Ise bola tha, ki Primary or Secondary education ke baare me agar dekhne gaye to aapki bhi le li jayegi. 
               
               Iski recommendations par, 1904 ka Indian Universities act pass hua tha. 
               
                  i) Universities ko jyada attention dena chahiye, Study or research par
                  
                 ii) Ek university ke number of fellows kam kar diye gaye the, or Unke office ka period bhi ghata diya gaya tha, or jyadatar fellows ko Government nominate karti thi. 
                 
                iii) Gov. ke pass power aa gayi, to veto university ke senate ke regulations and could amend these regulations 
                
                 iv) Private collages ke affiliation ki condtions strict ki jani chahiye
                 
                  v) Agle 5 saalo ke liye, 5 lakh/annum sanction kiye jane chahiye for dvpt of higher education and Universities. 
                  
                  
          Curzon bhadwa bolta ki, Universities par jyada control chahiye, to ensure quality and efficiency.
          
          Nationalist bolte--Ye to tatti hai----> Attempt to strength imperialism and to sabotage nationalist feelings. 
          
                      Gokhale ne ise ek "Retrogade measure" bataya. 
                      
                      
                      
                      
                              
          1906 me Baroda ki progressive state ne Compulsory primary education introduce ki, throughout its territories. 
          
          National leaders ne urge kiya Government se, to do so for British India----> Gokhale ne aise karne ke liye, Powerful advocacy ki, in Legislative assembly.                    
          
                 
             
             
       1913 me GoI ne Resolution on Education policy issue kiya:    i) Nahi Nahi hamse compulsory education na ho payega
       
                                                                   ii) Lekin illetracy hatane ki koshish karenge. 
                                                                   
                                                                  iii) Provincial govs se anurodh hai ki wo gareeb and ati-pichda bachoo ko free primary education dene ka kashth karein. 
                                                                  
                                                                   iv) Secondary schools ki quality achii ki jayegi. 
                                                                   
                                                                    v) Har province me ek University bana dete hain, kyuu theek hai na Gokhale ji ? 
                                                                    
                                                                    
                                                                    
                                                                    
        1917 ka Saddler University Commission : 
        
                i) To study and report on the problems of Calcutta University---->Lekin iski recommendation, baki universities me bhi applicable thi. 
                
               ii) Isne sab---> School Education se University education tak pura analyse kiya
               
                  - University educaiton me improvement ke liye, Secondary education me improvement necessary pre-condition hai. 
                  
                  - School course 12 saal ka hona chahiye. 
                  
                  - Students, Intermediary stage ke baad, university me enter kare. 
                  
                  - Secondary or Intermediate education ke liye alag se boards banaye jayein
                  
                  - University regulations frame karne me kam rigidity honi chahiye
                  
                  - University IITs ki tarah Centralised, Residential type honi chahiye, na ki RTU ki tarah scattered affiliated collages. 
                  
              
              
            1916 se 1921 ke beech, 7 naye Universitites banaye gaye---> at Mysore, Patna, Benaras, Aligarh, Dacca, Lucknow and Osmania
            
            
            1920 me Government ne Saddler report ko Provincial Govs ko recommend kiya.                                                                   
             
             
                 
                 
                   Dyarchy ke under education : 
                   
                       GoI ne education me interest lena band kar diya, kyunki education ko provincial ministries ko shift kiya ja chuka tha. 
                       
                       GoI ne grants dene bhi band kar diye. 
                       
                       Lekin, Phillanthropic efforts ki wajah se education fir bhi grow karti rahi. 
                       
                       
                       
                       
                       
            Hartog Committee (1929)
            
            
                Schools or Colleges ke increased numbers ki wajah se education standards ki maa chud gayi. 
                
                Is committee ko development of education ke upar report karne ko bola tha. 
                
                
                    - Primary educatio par emphasis hona chahiye, lekin education me koi hasty expansion ya compulsion ki jarurat nahi hai. 
                    
                    - High school or intermediary stage par bas, deserving students hi jane chahiye---> Avg students ko 8th class ke baad, vocational cources me laga do. 
                    
                    - University education ke standards me improvements ke liye, admissions ko restrict karo. 
                    
                    
            
            
            
            
            Sergeant Plan of Education: 
            
             
                Sereant, Govt ka educational advisor tha---> In 1944
                
                
                      i) Pre-Primary education for 3-6 year age group
                      
                     ii) Free universal and compulsory elemantary education for 6-11 years age group
                     
                    iii) High School education for 11-17 years age group for selected children
                    
                         Higher secondary ke baad, 3 saal ka university course. 
                         
                     iv) 2 type ke high schools : i) Academic  ii) Techinal and Vocational
                     
                      v) Intermediate course ka aboltion
                      
                     vi) 20 saalo me adult illiteracy ka liquidation
                     
                    vii) Teacher training par stress
                    
                    
              Iska objective tha, 40 saal ke andar andar, England jaisi educational attainment.       
                                   
                    
                    
           
           
           
           
                                           						India After Independene
                                           								
                                           								
                                           								   
           
   Independence ke time, India kuch 51 International Organistaions ka member tha, or kuch 600 treaties sign kar chuka tha. 
   
   1st World War ke baad India ne, Treaty of Versailles bhi sign ki thi. 
   
   1920s me India, league of Nations ka founding member bhi tha, Same for International Labour organistion and International Court of justice. 
   
   India ne Washington conference on Naval Armaments 1921-22 me bhi participate kiya tha. 
   
   1920 ke baad se hi London me ek Indian High commissioner tha. 
   
   1st WW se pehle hi, Indian nationals ko kuch diplomatic posts bhi di gayi thi. 
   
   Independence ke baad, Indians, most influential non-western contingent the, UN or uski allied agencies me.

  0         
           Non-ALligned is not the same as neutrality. 
           
           Non-allignment has relevance both in times of war and peace. 
           
           Neutral country ke koi opinions hi nahi hote. 
           
           
           
           
           
     1st Chief Election commissioner of India : Sukumar Sen--> an ICS officer. 
     
                    
                 
        Elections karwane ke liye, Parliament ne 2 laws pass kiye : 1) RPA, 1950 :   VOters ki qualification      Electoral rolls           States ko House of people me seats allocate ki gayi   
        
                                                                    2) RPA, 1951 :   Qualification/Disqualification of Members        Elections ka conduct       Poll expenses        Poll itself        Counting of votes etc
                                                                    
                                                                    
                                                                    
                                In laws ke pass hone ke baad hi eletoral mahinery could be put in place. 
                                
                                
           1st general Election, is wajah se October 15, 1951 me hi karwaye ja sake. 
           
            
               Lok Sabha me jo 489 seats bhari jane wali thi, unme se 72 seats SC walo ke liye, or 26 seats ST walo ke liye reserved thi. 
               
               
               
               States ki legislative assemblies me total seat---> 3283 thi or 192 thi Scheduled Tribes ke liye. 
                                                                                 
                 
                 
                 
             Elections, Universal Adult Franchise ke basis pe hue----> 24 saal se jyada ke logo ko Right to vote tha. 
             
                17 crore, 32 lakh, 13 hazaar, 635 log enroll hue vote dene ke liye. 
                
                
                Inme se 45% Aurate thi. 
                
                
                Us time India ki population, 35 crore hi thi.    Matlab laghbhag 49% population ko enroll kar liya gaya tha. 
                
                
                
                
                
        Chunav se pehle, Shyama Prasad Mukherjee broke away and started Bhartiya Jan Sangh (Pro-BJP)

           Inme se 14 National Parties thi. 
           
           Total 1874 candidates the, Jinme se 533 independents the.

           CPI ko 16 seats mili or Socialist party ko 12. 
           
           BJS ko 3 seats mili. 
           
           
           
           
           BRA bechara Bombay se haar gaya.   Lekin BRA entered Parliament as a Rajya Sabha Candidate. 
           
           Kriplani bhi haar gaya, lekin uski lugai jeet gayi. 
           
           
           
           
           
           
           
           Congress ne Sab states me bhi maa chod di--> Formed Govs in all the states.
