             							PP History
             							
             							
             							
             
                        
         Isme, Shuru me bohot saari 'Medieval Terminologies' di gayi hain, unhe ek baar dekh lena chotu. 
         
         
          Babur ne Mughal Empire FOund kiya tha, 1526 AD me.       1550s ke baad, Akbar ke under, massive expansion hua tha.   
          
          
          Aurangzeb Chodumal(1658-1707) ke baad se decline start ho gaya. 
          
          	- Chode ki religious policies  + Maratha, Bijapur or Golconda walo ke against campaign ne bhi finances ki maa chod di. 
          	
		- Weak successiors + Wars of Succession
		
		- No military reforms  +  New tech nahi liye,  Saare commanders mar gaye, civil wars me. 
		
		- 1738 me pehle to Marathas ne,  Fir, Nadir Shah ne aake, Mughals ko apna lemon taste karwaya. 
		
		- 1748 me Afghaniyo ne invade kar diya, lekin, SHukar hai, ise repeal kar diya gaya. 
		
		- Lekin, Abdali ke under, Afghaniyo ne Punjab par kabja kar liya. 
		
		- 1761 me Abdali, ne Marathas ko bhi lemon taste karwaya. 
          	
          
          
          Akar ke mansabdari system me, Mansabdars ko --->   Cash me pay kiya jata tha (Naqdi-mansabdari)   ya fir     jyadatar cases me, Jagir/Landed state de di jaati thi, jiske revenue(jama) se wo apne kharche chalata tha. 
          
          2 tarah ki jagirs thi : Tankha jagir  +   Vatan Jagirs
          
          Jyadatar jagirs, transferable thi. 
          
          Sab appointments, transfers,  postings, Emperor karta tha, isliye, pura "patron-client" relationship tha.   Isliye, koi national-loyalty naam ki cheez nahi thi. 
           
           
          Mughal Empire ki 4/5th land-revenue income, Mansabdars ke control me thi.  Lekin,   8000 mansabdars me se, bas 445 ne 61% par kabja kar rakha tha.  
           
          Aurangzeb ke baad se, Expansions band ho gaye the. 
          
          Bijapur (1685) me capture kiya tha, or iske 4 saal baad, yani 1689 me Golconda bhi.   Iski aristocracy, mughal courts me include ho gayi, jinhe Deccani kehte the. 
          
          
          Mughal Nobility ko waise, "Khazazads" bulaya jata tha. 
          
                                                           
          Or, resources ke lalach me, jagirs ke liye, ladte the, saare groups aapas me - Turani, Irani, Indian Mulle, Dindu, Deccani, Afghani etc.  ----> "Jagirdari Crisis" of 18th CE. 
                                                           
                                                           
               - Wo Area jise Jagir ke tor par distribute kiya jana tha : 'Paibaqi' 
               
               - 18th CE me, ye decline hone laga tha. 
               
               - European Markets ke khul jaane ki wajah se, Commodities ke price me bhi increase ho raha tha. 
               
               - Matlab, Randibaaji karne ke liye, paise nahi mil rahe the. 
               
               - Or, ye dar rehta tha, ki pata nahi sala, kab aake koi transfer kar de. 
               
               - Aurangzeb choda, Apne khud ka, yani Royal Land a.k.a Khalisa ko expand kiye jaa raha tha.   Wo iske use se, Deccan Campaign finance kar raha tha. 
               
                
                
          - Bahadur Shah ne, Khalisa land ko distribute karne ki koshis ki, lekin is se, Khud kangaal ho gaya. 
          
          - Nizam-ul-mulk, jab wazir bana, tab usne, Communist Manifesto padh ke, land redistribute karne ki koshish ki, lekin, is se court ke log bhadak gaye, or bole, "Better Dead, then Red". 
          
         
         
         
         
         
         		Zabt Area : Jahan par detailed Land survery kiya gaya tha --> Yaha par land revenue 1/3rd of the produce tha. 
         		
         			    Lekin, Gujrat jaise fertile area me to ye sala, 3/4th tha. 
         			    
         			    
                
               - Rajput log, Mughals ke lemon chusne ke badle, unhe fixed payments kiya karte the, jise 'Peshkash' kehte hain. 
               
               - Primary zamindar log ko ----> Mulgujari  kehte the --> Inke through revenue collect kiya jata tha, or badle me inhe, 'Nankar' yani revenue-free land milti thi. 
               
               - Zamindars ke neeche, peasant log hote the, 2 tarah ke : i) Khudkasht : Jinke pass occupancy rights the       ii) Pahikashts : Vagrant peasants. 
               
               - Zamindar, inhi ki caste ka hota tha, or sala khud ki small army type or even forts type bhi rakhta tha. 
               
               - Inke cooperatoin ke bina, mughal log, jhant nahi ukhad sakte the. 
               
               - Aurangzeb ke marne ke baad, in logo ne bhi apne aand dikhane shuru kar diye the. 
               
            
            
            
            
            Bahadur Shah ke time, ek alag hi kism ka randaap start hua : Ijaradari system ka 
            
               - Deccan me, bohot saare jagirdari,  Maratha Sardars se compromise kar ke, peasants se jitna man kare utna revenue extract karne lage. 
               
               - Ye chode, Revenue Official a.k.a Amil se pehle hi advance le leta tha, jo fir jaakar, Peasants ke aand masalta tha. 
               
               - Is se bhi logo ka man nahi bhara, to saalo ne, Revenue-collection rights ko Highest bidder ko de diye jaate the---> Is se short period me maximum revenue squeze kiya jata tha. 
               
               - Farukhsiyar ke time to, Sala Khalisa land tak ko Ijaradar log ko dene lag gaye the. 
               
               
            
            
            
            
            
            
            Bengal : Jab, 1717 me Murshid Quli Khan, Governor appoint hua (By Siyar Bhai)
            
            	     - Ise, Diwan or Nizam, dono appoint kiya gaya.   Diwan = Revenue,   Nizam = Governor
            	     
            	     - Quli ke bad, Sarfaraz aaya, jiske baad aaya, Alivardi Khan. 
            	     
            	     - Alivardi ne hi, finally Mughals ko "Fuck Off" bola tha. 
            	     
            	     - 1751 me, Alivardi ne Maratha lemon chus liya, or Chauth dene laga Bangal ke liye, or Orissa ko bhi Marathas ke hawale kar diya. 
            	     
            	     	- Marathas ke wajah se, NW se Inland trade khatam ho gaya, jis wajah se Brits baniye, kaafi powerful ho gaye the. 
            	     
            	     
            	     - 1756 me Alivardi allah ko pyara ho gaya, to uske pote, yani 'Siraj-ud-dalla-lundbahadur' ko appoint kiya. 
            	     
            	     - 
                
                
                
       Hyderabad : 1724 me found hua tha, by Nizam-ul-Mulk-Hulkbadamshake   ---> Ye Turani Party ka leader tha. 
       
       			- Jab, Sayyid ne Siyyar Bhaiya, ki maa chod di, tab ye dar ke maare Hyderabad bhaag gaya.  in 1719
       			
       			- Isne, fir Irani logo ko chodu banakar, Sayyids ki bhi maa chod di, or Muhammad Shah ko Ruler bana, khud uska wazir ban gaya, from 1722 to 1724. 
       			
       			- Iske marne ke baad Asaf Jah ban gaya, nizam. 
       			
       			- Iske baad, Nizam ali khan aaya, jo 1803 tak raha. 
       			
       	
       	
       	Awadh : Saadat khan, ko 1722 me Governor appoint kiya tha. ---<> Yahi Choda, Burhan Ul-Mulk kahalata tha. 
       	
       	        - Nadir Khan ke invasion wale din, 1740 me ye zahar khaake mar gaya tha. 
       	        
       	        - Safdar Jung ko Nadir Shah ne appoint kiya tha. 
        
        	- 1754 me, iske pote, Shuja-ud-dalla ko Governor appoint kiya gaya, by Alamgir II.
        	
       
       
       
       
       Jab Guru Nanak, Sikhism start kar raha tha, tab, babur Mughal Empire Start kar raha tha, in 1469. 
       
       		- 1675 me Aurangzeb ne 9th Guru ka Sar Kalam kiya. 
       		
       		- 1699 me, Guru Gobind Singh ne, Khalsa Start kiya. 
       		
       		- 1708 me, Guru Gobind ko mar diya gaya, to Banda Bahadur kud pada maidan me. 
       		
       		- 1716 me, Banda ko Delhi me maar diya gaya (RiP Bro)
       		
       		- 1760s tak, Misls ban chuke the. 
       		
       		- 1790s me, Ranjit Singh ne sabko unite kar diya. 
       		
       		- Treaty of Amritsar se, Brits ne Ranjit Singh ko, Sole Soveriegn Ruler of Punjab recognise kiya. 
       		
       
       
       
       
       Rajput log, Mughals ko 'Peshkash' dete the --> Annual tribute, as a mark of subordination. 
       
       
       
       
       British EIC : 31st December, 1600 ko charter mila, east me trade karne ka. 
       
       		- Pehli Factory setup karne ki permission di, Jahangir ne, 1613 me in Surat. 
       		
       		- 1617 me, Thomas Roe, Jahangir ke court me resident ban gaya. 
       		
       		- 1661 me, Charles II ko Portuguese ne Dahej me Bombay diya, jise usne 1668 me EIC ko de diya, 10 pound salana kiraye ke liye.  1687 me, Presidency HQ ko Bomaby shift kiya gaya, from Surat.  
       		
       		- Ye sab "Treaty of Whitehall" ke under hua tha, jiske hisab se, Brits or Portuguese, saath milkar, Chrystal se ladenge. 
       
       		- 1690 me. Aurangzeb ne, Bengal me, Duty-free trade karne ko allow kiya, saalana Rs 3000 ki payment par. 
       		
       		- 1690 me hi Calcutta found hua, or 1696 me, uska fortification kiya.  Iske baad, 3 gaanvo ki zamindari ka right mila --> Kolikata, Suranuri and Gobindpur. 
       		
       		- 1717 me Siyaar Bhai, ne apna Farman issue kiya : i) Duty free trade karo, chodo     ii) Calcutta ke around 38 villages rent pe lelo, chodo    iii) Royal Mint use karo, chodo, kyunki tumhare mu se baans aati...
       		
       		- Lekin, is farman se Brits or Quli ke beech, muka laat shuru ho gayi---> Quli was like, "Bokachoda, ye duty free trade wala provision, tumhari maa..err mera matlab, tumhare private traders ke liye nahi hai". 
       		
       		- Lekin, Comapany was like, "Deez Dustaks, Naboob". 
       		
       		- 1740 me, Austrian War of Succession start ho gaya--> Alivardi khan ne, French or Brits ko bola, "Bokachoda, yaha koi ladta dikh gaya to aand jala dunga". 
       		
       		- 1755 me, Brits ne firse, Calcutta ko Fortify karna shuru kar diya. 
       		
       		- Ye, saale, Naboob ke court se bhaage Fugitives ko protect karne lage, Calcutta me. 
       		
       		- 1756 me, Dalla Nabbob bana. ---> Isne, Dastak ke misuse ko rokne ki koshih ki. 
       		
       		- Dalle ne, Kaismbazar ki Factory par kabja kar liya. 
       		
       		- Dalle ne, fir 20 June ko Calcutta par attack kar diya. 
       		
       		- Robert Clive, Madras se Forces leke pahonch gaya.  
       
       		- Naboob court ke Dindu merchants, 'Dadani Merchants' ki tarah act karte the. ----> Interior se Brits ko textiles supply karte the, for advances or Dadan. 
       		
       		- Jagat Seths ne, Siraj ko Mir Jafar se replace karne ka man bana liya. 
       
       		- June 1757 me, Plassey ki ladai hui, jisme Clive ne, Dalle ki maa chod di. 
       		
       		- Dastak ke chutiyaap ki wajah se, 1760 me, Jafar ko Kasim se replace kar diya, jo uska Son-in-law tha. 
       		
       		- Lekin, 1763 me, Dastak wale chutiyaap ki wajah se hi, Kasim ne, Saari Duties hi abolish kar di, jis se Indian traders ko bhi fayda hone laga. Lekin, Brits ne gusse me, Kasim ko Jafar se firse replace kar diya. 
       		
       		- Kasim, Bengal se bhag gaya, or Shah Alam II + Shuja-ud-Daula of Awadh se alliance kar liya. 
       		
       		- Lekin, 1764 me, Battle of Buxar me, teeno ki Gaand lal kar di gayi. 
       		
       		- 1765 me Treay of Allahbad sign hui : Shah Alam ne, Company ko BOB ki Diwani de di.   Shuje, ne Rs 5 million diye,      Defend each others territories,     Brit resident at court.   Duty-free trade in Awadh
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       French : Last to arrive, First to start creating a territorial Empire. 
       
        	- Pondicherry found kiya, in 1674. 
        	
        	- Governor of Chandernagor appoint hua, Dupleix in 1731.    1742 me, ise Pondicherry ka charge mila.    Yahi pehla Euro fag, tha jisne local randaap me lund adana shuru kiya tha. 
       
		- 1740 me Austrian War of succession start hua. 
		
		- French chodo ne josh me aake, Madras par attack kar diya. ---> Is se 1st Carnatic War start hua. ----> Angrez log ne, Naboob of Carnatic se madad mangi, jiski French ne maa chod di.        
       
       		- Treaty of Aix-La-Chappelle se, ladai khatam ho gayi. 
       		
       		- 2nd Carnatic War, Indian chutiyaap ki wajah se start hua, both in Carnatic and Hydrabad. ---> French ko Carnatic Chanda, or Hyderabadi Jung chahiye tha. Or dono jeet bhi gaye. 
       		
       		- Hyderabad ke naye Gov, Muzzaffar Jung ne French chodo ko, Norther Sarkars, Masulipatman or Pondicherry ke aaspass ke gaanvo ki jagir dedi. 
       		
       		- Is se Brits ki gaand me aag lag gayi. Calcutta se Clive doda doda aaya, 2nd Carnatic War start karne in 1752. 
       		
       		- Brits jeet gaye, or Muhammad Ali ko Carnatic ka throne mila. 
       		
       		- Dupleix ne koshis ki wapas lene ki French positions, lekin French Gov tabtak tang aa chuki thi, or use 1754 me wapas bula liya.  Ise 'Godeheu' naam ke chode se replace kiya, jisne aate hi Brits se treaty kar li. 
       		
       		- Iske baad, 1756 me, Europe me, 7 years war start ho gaya.   Count de Lally + Bussy, dono ki maa chud gayi.  
       		
       		- 1760 me Battle of Wandiwash hua. 
       
       		- 1763 me Treaty of Paris se, Frenchs ko wapas mil gayi saari possessions. ---> But they could not fortify chandernagore. 
       		
       		
       		
       		
       		
       		
       Pitt's India Act,1784 se hi, Brits chodo ko mana kiya tha, Territorial Expasion karne se. 
       	
       	- Lekin, Fuckboy bhai ke India aane se, in 1798, sab change ho gaya. 
       		
       	- Bhai ne, French ka dar dikha ke, 'Subsidiary Alliance' naam ki policy start ki. 		
       
       	- Bhai, ko 1805 me wapas bula liya gaya, kyunki bhai ke kaand ki wajah se, EIC ke financial lode lag gaye the. 
       		
       	
       
       
       1813 me, Lord Hastings leke aaya---> "Paramountcy" wala randaap. 
       
        
        
        
        
        
       1797 me, Brits ne Awadh ke Nawab ke succession me interfere karke, dhan luta. 
        	
        	- 1801 me, Fuckboy ne, aadhe Awadh par kabja kar liya, as permanent payment of subsidy.           
    
    
    
     	1830 me Cachar par kabja kiya. 
     	
     	1834 me, Lord Bentinck ne Coorg par kabja kiya. 
    
    	Dalhousie bhai ne, Hyderabad se Berar cheena tha, 1853 me, payment na kar paane ki wajah se. 
    
    
    
    
    
    
        
        
        1813 ke Charter act se -----> Christianization of India began. 
        
        
        1833 ke Charter act se -----> Free-Trade began. 
        
        
        	- 1817 me, James Stuart Mill ne, "History of British India" naam ki book likhi thi.   Ye banda ek, Benthamite-Utilitarian tha. 
        	
        	- Is bande ki wajah se hi, 1833 me Lord Macaulay ke under, ek Law Commission appoint hui thi. ---> 1835 ka IPC bana---> Benthamite model of Centrally, logically and coherently formulated code 
        	
        		- Lekin, ye act bana tha, 1860 me. 
        	
         	- Lord Bentinck, bhi Mill ka follower tha. 
        
        
        
        
        
        
        
        
          1766 me Parliament ki intervention ke baad, Company ne agree kiya to pay, 4lakh pouns annually to the British Government. 
                
                
                
       	  1773 me Clive par, or 1786 me Hastings par Mukaadamma chala, or 1806 me Fuckboy bhai par bhi. ---> However all three were unsuccessful. 
        
        
        
        
        1772 me Company financial crisis me chali gayi, or Britis GOv se loan mangne lagi, jiska fayda uthake, Gov ne Company ke affairs ko regulate karna start kar diya. 
        
              
              - Isi Time, Adam Smith ne 'Wealth of nations' naam ki kitab likhi, or monopolies ko maa bahan ki gaali di. 
                 
              - Lekin, as a compromise, Company ko allow kiya, monopolise karne ko, lekin, some sort of accountablity was created. ---> Directors of the company were given control of the Indian Adminstration. 
       
              
         1773 me, Regulation Act leke aaye : 
         
             - Foramlly recognised Parliamentary right to control Indian affairs. 
             
             - EIC ko bola to report their communication to British Gov. 
             
             - Governor of Bengal was now, Governor General. ---> TO be assisted by council of 4 members. 
             
             - Control over B and M--> For making war and peace. 
             
             - SC in Calcutta
             
         
      1781 me Amending Act aaya----> To clearly define the jurisdiction of Supreme Court. 
  
         
         
      1784 me Pitt's India Act aaya : 
         
             - Company ke admin, ko jyada direct gov control ke under laaya gaya tha. 
             
             - Ek BOC of 6 members was constituted--> Isme, Secy of State, Chancellor of Exchequer or 4 privy councillors the. 
             
             - Is board ke order, COD par binding the. 
             
             - Ab, COD ko apne postings karne ke liye, Crown ki permisison leni padt thi. 
             
             - Gov in India ko, Governor General + Council of 3 ke under kar diya. 
             
             - B and M ki presidencies ko, GG ke subordinate kar diya. 
             
             - GG ko khudko, CoD or BoC ke subordinate kar diya. 
             
           
           
      1786 me Amending act aaya, Jis se GG ko power di, to override his council in extraordinary situations + GG and Commander-in-chief ke offices ko combine kar diya
        
        	- Warren Hastings, pahla banda tha, jisne dono post occupy ki. 
        		
        		   
            
       1793 ka Charter Act : 
        
           - 20 saalo ke possessions extend ki. 
           
           - B and M ke Governors ko decisively GG ke control ke under le aaye. 
           
           - Regular Code of Regulation, frame kiya. 
           
           - Sab laws ko Indian languages me print kiya jana tha. 
           
           - Is se, India me -> Civil Law ka concept introduce hua--> Enacted by a secular Human Agency and Applied Universally.     
            
            
       1813 ka Charter : Important push towards, westernisation of India. 
       
          - Company ke charter ko 20 saalo ke liye, Extend kiya. 
          
          - Trade monopoly cheen li, except- China and Tea. 
          
          - Christian cucks ko allow kiya. 
          
       
       
       
       1833 ka Charter : 
       
        
           - Sab monopolies khatam ho gayi. 
           
           - BoC ka president became---> Minister for Indian Affairs. 
           
           - GG of Bengal becae---> Governor General of Indi---> Apni council ke saath milke, will control all the civil, military and revenue matters of India.  
           
           - GG ko empower kiya to legislate for the entire country. ---> Applicable to all people--> British and Indian. 
           
           - Council me ek Law member add kiya gaya----> For codification of Laws. 
           
           - Company's services were thrown open to the natives---> Lekin, covenanted services me nominate karne ka koi provision nahi tha. 
           
       
       
       
      1853 ka CHarter : 
      
          - Unspecified time. 
          
          - Seperation of Executive and Legislative functions of GG's Council by adding 3 new members for Legislative Purposes. 
          
          - Company ke appointment ke upar ke control of curtail kiya by introducing open competition for Indian Civil Services. 
          
      
      
      1858 ka India Act : 
      
          - Replaced BoC with Secy of State for India. 
          
          - Council of India of 15 members to advice him. ---> Isme se 7 ko CoD select karne wala tha. 
          
          - GG ban gaya, Viceroy----> Answerable only to Secy of State. 
          
      
      
      
      
      1769-70 me. Bengal me Famine aaya---> Bengal ki 1/3rd population mar gayi. 
      
      	 - Hastings ne iski wajah se, Naib Diwan Reza Khan par corruption ke charges laga, gaand par laat maar bahar kiya. 
      
      1772 me, Warren Hastings lekar aaya---> Farming System. 
      
        	- Revenue collection ke charge ke liye, European District Collectors appoint kiye. 
        	
        	- Lekin, Actual Revenue collection ka kaam, highest bidder ko diya jata tha. 
        	
        	- Periodicity ke bhi kai experiments kiye, lekin agri ki maa chud gayi.  
        	
        	- 1784 me cornwallis ko India me isiliye bheja tha ki wo jaake, Revenue admin ko streamline karega. 
        	
        	
        	
        Cornwallis ke Permanent Settlement : 
        
           - Fix the damn thing permanently. 		Is se pehle--> Alexander Dow, Henri Parullo, Philip Franceis and Thomas Law naam ke chodo ne bhi same cheez boli thi. 
           
           - Ye sab, Physiocratic School of thinking ke the : "Agri is the backbone of country's economy". 
           
           - Inhe lagta tha ki is se corrpution bhi kam ho jayega kyunki, assessment ke time, bhayanak hafta wasooli hua karti thi. 
           
           - Ladlords ko bhi incentive tha land me invest karne ka. 
           
	   - Company ko regular taxes mil jaane the. 
	   
	   - 1793 me, Zamindars ke sath, Permanent Settlement kiya gaya. 
	   
	   - BBO ki saari jameen, Became part of Zamindari or estate 
	   
	   - Zamindar ko ispar, tax dena padta tha, jo ki fix tha. 
	   
	   - Wo us zamindari ko sell, mortgage ya transfer kar sakta tha. 
	   
	   - Land could also be inherited.         
            
           - Failure to pay the revenue would lead to confiscation of zamindari by gov and it's sale by auction. 
           
           - Therefor : Creation of Private property in land. 
           
           - CHutiyo ne, Zamindars ko ownership rights de diye, jinke pass pehle bas, revenue collection rights the. 
           
           - Peasants ko saala Kirayedaar banakar chor diya. 
           
           - Zamindar or Peasant ke beech patta sign hona tha. 
           
           - 1799 se 1812 ke beech, naye regulations laake, zamindaars ko power de di gayi to seize property of tenants in case of non-payment of rent without any permission of a court of law. 
           
           - Zamindar ko bhi, 'Sun-set' law ke hisab se, ek fixed date par revenue deni padti thi, jiske fail hone par uske bhi lode lag jaate the. 
           
           - Lekin, auction me Zamindaro ke khudke offcials- Amlas,   ya   kabhi kabhi bade peasants- Jotedars,  hi khareed lete the. 
           
           - Kuch zamindar, sub-feudatory tenures yaani, Patni, employ kiya karte the.  
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       1798 me Fuckboy ne, Madras presidency me bhi Permanent Settlement extend karne ka man banaya. 
       
       		- Lekin, Madras ki zamindar class itni badi nahi thi. 
       		
       		- Lekin, fir bhi 1801 se 1807 ke beech, kaafi areas me introduce kiya gaya. 
       		
       		- Local Poligars ko hi, zamindars recognise kiya gaya. 
       		
       		- Jaha Poligars nahi the, waha villages ko estates me aggregate karke, highest bidder ko bech diya, in auctions. 
       		
       		- Lekin  yaha ke scottish zamindars, jaise Munro and Elphinstone, ko bade zamindaro se nafrat thi. Unhe pata tha, ki kaise perma settlement se saara profit, bade zamindar hadap lete the. 
       		
       		- David Recardo ki "theory of rent" ne bhi kaafi influence kiya. 
       
       		- Isliye, Madras Presidency me, Ryotwari settlement jaise randaap kiya gaya. 
       	
       			1) Ise, Alexander Reed ne, 1792 me Baramahal naam ki jagah par start kiya tha. 
       			
       			2) Lekin, Munro ne ise, 1801 me copy kar liya. 
       
       			3) Zamindaro ki jagah, ye directly villages se paise wasoola karte the, har village ko fix amount pay karni hoti thi. 
       			
       			4) Is ke baad, wo har ryot yaani cultivator ko asses karte the. 		
       			
       			5) Munro bhai, ke hisab se, "State itself was the supreme landlord". 
       			
       			6) Assessmnet ke baad, annual agreements kiye jaate the, between government and cultivators---> Jinke paas choice hoti thi agreement ko accept ya reject karne ki.
       			
       			7) Lekin, bohot hi jyada revenue demand ki jaati thi. Isliye, koi jhaant deta tha, to koi bakri ki leed. 
       			
       			8) Isliye, 1807 me Munro bhai ke wapas jaate hi, is system ko abolish kar diya gaya. 
       			
       			9) Lekin, 1820 me, Munro bhai came back as Governor of Madras. 
       			
       		       10) Is se, land hui State ki, na ki zamindars ki--> Isliye, Brit Gov bhi khush thi---> Isliye Presidency ke most parts me introduce kiya gaya. 
       		       
       		       11) Ise, putcut settlement bhi kehte the. 
       
         	       12) 1855 ke baad se hi, Scientific survey of land and fresh assessment of revenue were undertaken-----> Is se tax burden kam hua. 
         	       
         	       13) Is system ko, Madras Presidency ke Norther Areas me bhi laya gaya, jaha chodo ko kuch zamindar type ke log mil gaye the. 
         	 
         - Bombay Presidency me, Ryotwari system start hua tha, Gujrat se, after it's annexation in 1803 and then in 1818 when Peshwa's territories were conquered. 
         
         	- Munro ke chele, Elphinstone
         	
         	- British log, Deshmukh, ya Patil naam ke village headman ke through revenue collect kar rahe the. 
         	
         	- Lekin, is se jyada rokda nahi aa raha tha. 
         	
         	- Isliye, 1813-14 se, unhone directly peasants se collect karna shuru kar diya. 
         	
         	- R.K. Pringle naam ke chode ne land survey kar, 55% of produce rent fix kar diya. 
         	
         	- Lekin, 1835 me ise Replace kar diya gaya, by 'Bombay Survey System', jise 2 officers G.Wingate and H.E.Goldsmid ne devise kiya tha. 
         	
         	- Is system se, 1847 tak, pure Deccan ko cover kar diya gaya. 
         	
         	- "Village Patil" ki maa chud gayi this is system se. 
       
       		- Is system ko fir, Sindh, Assam and Coorg me extend kiya gaya. 
       
        
            
        MAHALWARI SETTLEMENT : 
        
          - North and NW India , between 1801 ke 1806 Brits ke haath aa gaya---> Himalayan Foothills and Ganga-Jamuna Doab. 
          
          - Yaha bade 'Taluqdaars' hua karte the---> Ye, Intermediary zamindars hua karte the-->Ye states se contract karke, revenue collect kiya karte the.  
          
          - Lekin, yaha 'Primary Zamindars' bhi the, jo 'Holders of proprietary rights over agri lands' the.
          
          - Holt Mackenzie and Thomas Metcalf    
            
          - Regulation VII of 1822 se Mackenzie ke recommendatoin par 'Primary Zamindars' and village communities , ke saath, field-to-field survey kar, revenue settlement kiya gaya. 
          
          - Taluqtads puri tarah se eliminate nahi hue the, lekin jaha possible tha, joint propeietary rights were vested in village communities. 
          
          - Lekin, itna complex system tha, ki, Existing machinery ki madad se, survey tak dhang se nahi kiya ja saka. 
          
          - Regulation IX of 1833 se kuch reforms kiye gaye. --->  R.M Bird bhai ne "Mahal" naam ki fiscal unit ke detailed survey kiye--> Based on the net value of potential produce. Ise complete lekin, James Thomason ne kiya. 
          
          - 30 saal tak settlement ki thi---> State would appropriate 2/3rd of net income of land. 
          
          - Is system ko fir, Punjab and Central India me extend kiya gaya.   
            
            
            
            
                  
               
         1928 tak : 
         
            19% land Zamindari System ke under,       29% Mahalwari ke under     or    52% Ryotwari ke under tha.       
               
             
             
             
             
             
      1813 tak Company was like the Traditional Indian Ruler : 
      
        - Avoiding innovation or intervention, but keeping a vigilant eye on extracting agri surplus. 
        
        - Lekin ye sab change ho gaye because of : Industrial Revolution, Need for Raw materials, Integration of markets. 
        
      
      
             
      1772 me, warren Hastings ne charge liya : 
      
         - Sab districts me, 2 courts banwaye : 1 Civil court a.k.a Diwani Adalat   and    1 Criminal Court a.k.a Faujdari adalat. 
         
         - Criminal cases me mulla laws and Civil cases me, Hindu/Mulla laws apply hote the. 
         
         - Laws ko divide kiya gaya according to subjects. 
         
         - Civil Courts ko European District Collector preside karta tha. ---> Inhe assist karne ke liye, molvi or pandat hote the. 
         
         - Calcutta me ek appeal court thi----> Jise 'President' preside karta tha, or counicl ke 2 members bhi. 
         
         - Criminal court kazi/mufti ke under thi---> Lekin, inhe district collectors supervise karte the. 
         
         - Iski appeal court --> Sadar Nizamat Adalat thi---> Removed from Murshidabad to Calcutta. 
         
         - Iska control bhi, President or Council member ke paas aa gaya tha, Reza khan ko hatane ke baad. 
         
         - 1774 tak, sala Hastings, khud Criminal Justice system ko supervise karta tha. 
         
         - Lekin, CoD ke kehne par, Reza khan ko dobara, Nizamat Adalat ka head bana diya, or ise Wapas Murshidabad shift kar diya gaya. 
         
         
         
      1773 se 1781 ke beech,  Justice or Executive functions ko seperate karne ki kaafi koshish ki gayi : 
      
         -  Hastings or Impey (Chief justice of Calcutta High COurt) ----->  District collectors se unki Judicial powers cheen li gayi. 
         
         - Civil Justice ke liye : District courts ki jagah--> 6 provincial courts banaye gaye, fir inhe bhi baad me 18 Mofussil courts se replace kar diya gaya. ----> In sab ko European Covenanted officers preside karte the. 
         
         - 1773 act se bani supreme court, baar baar lode leti rehti thi, supreme council se. 
         
         - Isliye, Sadar Diwani Adalat banai gayi,to serve as an appeal court---> Impey khud iska superintendent ban gaya, 1780 me. 
         
         - 1781 ke code me--> Rules and regulations specify kiye gaye the---> To be followed in all civil courts and all judicial orders were to be made in writings. 
         
         - 1775 me, Hastings ke kehne par, 11 pandato ne, Hindu Laws ko compile kiya---> Ise, N.B Halhed ne, English me translate kiya. 
         
         - 1778 me mulla laws bhi complie hue. 
         
         - Is se, "Lawyers" type logo ki chaandi ho gayi. 
         
         - Hastings ke reforms ki wajah se, Judiciary centralise ho gayi. 
         
         
       1787 me Reversal : Collectors were given duty of adminstering civil justice
       
       1793 me aaya : Cornwallis Code :--> Seperated Revenue Collection from Adminstration of Civil justice
       
       			- Is se Hierarchy of courts bane, from Zilla a.k.a District courts to city courts to 4 provincial courts and    Sadar Diwani Adalat with Appellate jurisdiction. 
       			
       			- Criminal cases me bhi : Faujdari adalts ko abolish kar, Circuit courts se replace kar diya, or Jinhe European judges head karte the. 
       			
       						: Sadar Nizamat Adalat ko wapas, Calcutta le aaya gaya, or GG-in-Council ke directly under kar diya gaya. 
       						
       						: In courts ki jurisdiction me Europeans nahi aate the ---> Ye log bas Court at Calcutta ke under aate the. 
       						
       			- Matlab, Cornwallis code se, Indians ka total exclusion ho gaya
       			
       			- Code was extended to Banaras Province in 1795 
       			
       			
       			
       	Lekin, 1814 me, Munro bhai ke kehne par, CoD ne, Madras ke liye alag System banaya :  Provision for greater Indianisation of System at lower levels   +   Union of Magisterial, Revenue collection and judicial powers
       	
       																				  in the office of collector.
       																				  
       		- 1816 me fully introduce hua. 
       		
       		- 1819 me, Elphinstone bhai ke kehne par, Bombay me bhi extend kar diya gaya. 
       		
       
       
       
       	Bentinck bhai + 1833 ke Charter Act se kaafi changes aaye : 
       	
       	   - Threw open, Judicial positions to Indians
       	   
       	   - Provided law commission for codification of  laws. 																			  		      
                
             
        1859 me, Civil Procedure Code introduce hua. 
        
        1860 me, Indian Penal Code yani IPC introduce hua, jise 1833 wali commission ne banaya tha. 
        
        1862 me, Criminal Procedure Code Introduce hua.      
        
        
        
        
        
        
        POLICE : 
        
        
        	- Mughal time me, Police System was under the control of Faujdars, who were in charge of their 'Sarkars' a.k.a Rural Districes. 
        	
        		Towns ka charge, 'Kouoals' ke paas tha. 
        		
        		Zamindars, Village watchmen rakhte the, gaanvo ke liye. 
        		
        		
        		
         	- 1770 me jo famine aayi thi, uski wajah se, Crime rate badh gaya tha. 
         	
         	- 1781 tak, Faujdari system hi chalta raha, through minor modification. 
         	
         	- Baad me Faujdaro ko, English Magistrates se replace kar diya gaya. 
         	
         	- Zamindars ke paad ab bhi, police duties thi, lekin inhe, Magistrates ke subserviant kar diya gaya. 
         	
        
        
          1793 me Cornwallis the Coward ne : Took away the policing powers of Zamindars. 
          
          	- Divided the districts into Thanas or Units of police jurisdiction of 20/30 square miles---> Each placed under a new officer called-----> Daroga---> Ise,Magistrate appoint or Supervise karta tha.
          	
          	- Isliye, Daroga --> Naya instrument of control ban gaya, in Diwani provinces 
          	
          	- In saalo ne lekin, Zamindaro se nexus bana liye. 	
        
        
          Jonathan Duncan ne, Benaras me, Ise 'Tehsildari System' ke naam se implement kiya : Is bhai ne, Zamindaro ko bhi, Tehsildar ke under kar diya. 	
        
          1802 me, Daroga system was extended to Madras Province
        
        
        
        Lekin, 1807 me, Tehsildars were divested of police duties  
        
        	1812 me, Daroga System was formally abolished---> Supervision of Village police was vested in collector (Saala ab ye responsible tha--> Revenue, Police and Magisterial functions at same time)/ 
       
       		
        
        1843 me, Charles Napier ne ---> Sind me ek naya experiment kiya 
        
        	- Seperate Police Department banaya, with it's own officers 
        	
        	- Puri territory was under the supervision of an Inspector General 
        	
        	- Districts ke paas, khudka SP hoga---> Answerable to both Inspector General and District Collector
        	
        	- CHote level par Indians, lekin Officers to be Europeans. 
        	
        	- Is Model ko, 1849 me Punjab me,  1853 me Bombay me,   1859 me Madras me, lagu kiya gaya. 
        	
       
       
       	1860 me Police Commission appoint ki gayi : 
       	
       	  - Provided for a basic structure of Police Establishment for the Indian Empire----> Enacted by the Indian Police Act, 1861. 
       	  
       	  - Agle 100 saalo tak yahi system lagu raha. 
       	  
       	  - Military police ko eliminate kiya gaya. 
       	  
       	  - Civilian police was organised on a provincial basis with IGs anwserable to provincial govs and District superintendents to the collectos. 
       	  
       	  - Matlab, Entire police system was placed under the control of civilian authority
       	  
       	  - District Superintendent yani ki SP, was in charge of rural police . 
       	  
       	  - Daroga ban gaya
       	  
       	  - Lekin, Indians were completely excluded. 
       	  
       
      1902 ke Police commission --> Provided for appointment of educated Indians to the position of officers of police force. 
        
        
        
             
      ARMY : 
      
      
      		French Chodo ne, 1721 me, Indian Army recruit karne ki tradition start ki thi. 
      		
      		1748 me, Stinger Lawrence naam ke chode ne, Permanent Indian army recruit ki, English Company ke liye. 
      		
      		Clive ne, 1757 me ise renew kiya. 
      		
      		Sepoy Army ko British officer command karta tha.        
             
             	1856 me, iska number 2 lakh paar kar gaya tha. 
             
             
             
            1857 ke baad, Peel Commission banai gayi : To loo into the military affairs of India 
             
            Indian Officers ki training start hui in 1931, after the 1st RTC.  
             
             
             
             
             
          
          
            INDIAN CIVIL SERVICES : 
      
      
      		- Method of recruitment : Indian Act of 1784 and Charter Act of 1793 : Only through nomination by Members of CoD
      		
      		- 1798 me Fuckboy ne : Fort Williams college start kiya : Sab presidencies ke civil servants ki training hoti thi. 
      		
      		- 1802 me ise, close kar diya gaya. 
      		
      		- 1805 me, iski jagah, East India College banaya gaya at Hertford, jise 1809 me, Haileybury me shift kar diya gaya. 
      		
      		- CoD ke dwara nominated members ki yaha 2 saal tak training hoti thi.  	
          
          	- Charter Act of 1833 se competition start hua, for recruitment----> But it was limited to only those who were nominated by the directors
          	
          	- Finally, Charter Act of 1853 se, Open Competition start hua, even Indians ke liye bhi. 
	
		- Haileybury college ko, 1858 me abolish kar diya gaya. 
		
		- Civil Service Commission, har saal recruit karti thi, through an examination held in England. 
		          
          	- 1870 me, Statutory Civil Services start hui : "Kuch ache gharo ke Indians ko, Covenanted civil services ke liye, recruit kiya jayega". - Lord Lytton Lundwale
          	
          	- 1892 me Civil Services reforms hue : Covenanted civil Services ban gayi---> Indian Civil Services(ICS) ,   Uncovenanted ban gayi ---> Provincial Civil Services
          	
          		- Lord Lytton Lundwale ki Statutory Civil service ko abolish kar diya gaya---> Ye waali seats ab, promotion ke through bhari jani thi, from Provincial Civil services. 
          		
          	- Indians ko lekin, abhi bhi London jaake paper dena padta tha, isliye, 1922 tak, bas Services me 15% hi Indians the. 
          	
          	- GoI Act, 1919 se --> Seperate, not simultaneous, recruitment examinations hone lage, to the ICS. ----> 1st exam hua tha, Allahbad me, in Feb 1922. 
          	
          	- 1941 tak, Indians outnumbered Europeans in Indian Civil services. 
               
            	
            
            
                     
                 
                 
                 
    	PARAMOUNTCY
    	
    	
    	- 2/5th of Indian Territory, 'Indirect rule' ke under thi. 
    	
    	- Princes rule karte the, lekin, British Residents or Political agents govern karte the. 
    	
    	- Pehle commercial agents rakhe hue the, jinka kaam tha, EIC ke commercial interests ko protect karna
    	
    	- Lekin, baad me ye, Residents ban gaye, or Comapany or Indian princes ke beech ke political relations ko bhi manage kiya karte the. 
    	
    	- Mughals ke time, 'Vakils' ka system hua karta tha. ---> Ye client state ko represent karta tha, Mughal court me. 
    	
    	- Paramountcy ke under, Indian states ke paas, bas "domestic sovereginity" thi, or sovereignity beyond borders, Company ke paas thi, as the superior imperial power. 
    	
    	- Ye Indirect rule, 3 phases me evolve hua : 
    	
    			1st Phase : 1764 to 1797 ---> Jab, company ne apne residents rakhe, at the courts of Murshidabad, Awadh and Hyderabad, after the Battle of Buxar
    			
    			
    			2nd Phase : 1798 to 1840 ---> Fuckboy ke time, aggressive expanisionism ke time, and his policy of subordinate alliance. 
    			
    							Role change ho gaya : Form managing, diplomatic relations to Indirect control. 
    							
    							Sala kuch cases me to , Residents khud territorial expanision karne lage. 
    							
    							Ye khatm hua, jab 1st Afghan war me, lode lag gaye, during 1838-42. 
    							
    							
    							
    		 	3rd Phase : 1841 to 1857 ---> Expanision ki jagah, consolidation par jor ---> Is time, direct annexation bhi start hua, during Dalhousie's Forward Policies, example- Doctrine of lapse. 
    		 	
    		 	
    		 	
    		 	1857 ke revolt me, Jo areas indirect control me the, unhone participate nahi kiya, or saala madad ki, jaise Hyderabad and Gwalior---> Isliye, 1857 ke baad se, commitment kiya, 'to respect the rights' 
    		 	
    		 	            
                 
        	Mayo bhai ke aane se(1869-72), reforminst policies firse wapas aa gayi : 
        	
        		Ye princes par pressure daalne lage, to reform their regime. 
        		
        		Baroda ke Madav Rao ki gaand par laat padi, on the grounds of "Gross Misrule". 
        		
        		         
        1870s se jab Nationalism jor pakadne laga, tab, Lord Lytton Lundwale, ne bola ki, "Princes to 'natural leaders' hain, Indians ke'. 
        
        Ye, princes ki madad se 'Legitamcy' gain kar rahe the, isliye ye rituals wagarh kiya karte the : Ex : Delhi Durbar of January 1877,  Gun Salutes
        
        
        Lord Curzon Badelundwale (1898-1905) ke time to intervention kaafi badh gaya tha : 
        
        	- Grand coronation Durbar, of 1903 me bulaya sabko, lekin sabko strict contol me le liye. 
        	
        	- 1900 me sabki foreign travel par ban laga diya
        	
        	- 1902 me Nizam se nayi treaty sign ki, regardin admin of Berar
        	
        	- Imperial Service Troops ke liye, jyada paise wasoole
        	
        	- 63 states ko directly british admin ke under liya, on temp basis. 
        	
        
        Mayo ke time, thoda dheel deni shuru ki, jab political extremism apne charam par tha. 
        
        	- 1909 me, nayi policy of 'Laissez-faire' ki baat ki. 
        	
       
       
       
       
       
       
       Indian Emigration Act of 1859 : Preventing migrant workers from leaving the plantation sites : Ensured a steady supply of labour to plantations. 
       
                 
                                                   
       GD Birla and Swarupchand Hukumchand, ne 1922 me Pehli Desi Jute Mills kholi. 
       
       
       
       
       
       SOCIAL and RELIGIOUS Reforms 
       
       
       
        Warren Hastings : Policy of Orientalism ----> Respect for traditional Indian Culture. 
        
        	- Establishment of Asiatic Society of Bengal,   Calcutta Madrassa,   Sanskrit college at Banaras
        	
       
       	Hastings ke jaane ke baad se, Indians ke social institutions me lund adane ki policy start ho gayi. 
       	
       		- Evangelicalism,  Utilitarianism,   Free-Trade thinking,   Reformism
       		
       		- English Education se, Desi log tayyar ho gaye, reforms ki baaate karne ko : 
       		
       			- Charity run schools in Calcutta, Madras and Bombay 
       			
       			- Charles Grant advocated for English Education 
       
       			- Madras ke Dansih Station se, Protestant missionaries kaam karne lage, from early 18th CE. 
       			
       			- Lekin, 1813 tak, inka number kaafi kam tha. 
       			
       		- Real begining of Western Education started after the Charter Act of 1813----> Missionaries ko allow kiya  +  1 lakh rupees per year for 2 specific things :- i)Improving literature ii)Promote Sciences
       		
       			Lekin, kis language me padhana hai, aisa kuch mentioned nahi tha. 
       			
       			
       		- David Hare jaise Individuals, desh ke kai areas me school kholne lage---> Jaha English was the medium of instruction. 
       		
       		- 1819 me, Calcutta School Book society  and  Calcutta School society start hui.-----> Vernacular schools ko promote karne ke liye. 
       		
       		- Is time, RRR ne English Education ki wakalat karni shuru kardi. 
       		
       		- 1828 me, Bentncik GG bana, jo ek Utilitarian banda tha---> Iski wajah se Anglicists ki jeet hui, over Orientalists. 
       		
       		- Is bhai ne, Thomas Babington Macaulay ko Law member appoint kiya in his council in 1834. ----> Ise, fir General Committee on Public Instruction ka President bhi bana diya gaya. 
       		
       		- 2nd February, 1835 ko Macualay ne apna--> "Minute on Indian Education" issue kiya		
       
       
       
		- Lekin, NW India me, Thomason naam ke bande ne, Vernacular elementary schools start kiye, or ye itne mast the, ki Dalhousie ne, ise Bengal or Bihar me bhi extend karne ko recommend kiya. 
		
		-1854 me, Charles Wood's Education Dispatch aaya :---> Shift away from Downward Filtration Policy---> Recommended extansion of Vernacular education. 
		
		
		- 1857 me 3 nayi Universities bhi banai gayi at Calcutta, Bombay and Madras, on the model of Unviersity of London. 
		
		- English medium wale secondary schools bhi khub bane. 
		
		
		- 1882 me Indian Education Commission aaya : Tried to resolve the problem of duality in education system       
       
       
       		- 1882 tak, 20 crore population me se bas, 20 lakh hi elementary padhe likhe log the. 
       		
       		- 
       
       
       
       Young Bengal wale, Henry ne hi, 1838 me 'Society for the Acquisistion of General Knowledge' start ki---> Discussed various aspects of western science and spoke about social reforms
       
       
       
               SOCIAL REFORMS : 
               
               
               			- Fuckboy ne 1803 me, Child Sacrifice par ban laga diya, at Sagar Island, in the Bay of Bengal. 
               			
               			- 1870 me Female Infanticide par ban lagaya gaya. 
               			
               			- Bengal ke jin areas me, Dyabbaga school of personal Hindu law, applicable tha--> Waha Sati prevelant thi.    Iske opposite, Mitakshara school tha, jisme widow ko property rights mil jaate the. 
               			
               			- 1829 me Sati par ban laga. 
               			
               			- Hindu Widow's Remarriage act of 1856
               				- Lekin, saala is se, widow ko apne purani pati ki properites nahi milti thi. 
               				
               	
               	1866 me, Vishnushastri Pandit ne, Widow Remarriage ke liye ek society banai. 
               	
               	
                1878 me, Veerasalinam Pantulu ne, Society for Social Reform banai, Widows ki jawani bachane ke liye. 
                
                
                  
                1830s me. Thuggs ke against campaign start hui. ---> Campaign ka architect tha William Sleeman. 
               
               
               1843 me Slavery ko abolish kiya gaya.   Is se pehle, Charter Act of 1833 me, Slavery Abolish karne ki baat ki gayi thi. 
               
               
               
               
               
               
    Marxist Historians : R P Dutt,   V.I.Pavlov,   S.N.Mukherjee,    Sumit Sarkar,    Bipin Chandra           
                                                                                   
                                   
                                   
                                   
                                   
                   Neel Darpan --->  Dinabandhu Mitra, in 1860. --> Ek Bengalee play hai.                 
                                   
                                   
                   1873 me Pabna Agrarian League start hui thi. 
                   
                   
                   Punjab me, Kuka Revolt hua, 1872 me.                 
                                   
                                   
                                   
                                                        
                  Poona Sarvajanik Sabha ne, 1873 me ek Report publish ki----> "Case for revision of the revenue rates".                     
                                      
                             Jab rioting start hui, to Bombay Gov ne : Deccan Agri Relief Act of 1879 pass kiya.          
                                      
                                      
                                      
                   1907 me Punjab me Canal Colony Agitations hue, Jab Punjab Gov ne  koshish ki to control the inheritence of property. 
                   
                   	-- Isme, LLR and Ajit Singh of Lahore Indian Association, Singh Sabha and Arya Samaj bhi jud gaye. 
                   	
                   	-- Minto ne dabaw me aakar, Act ko veto kar diya.                   
                                      
                                      
                                      
                1899-1900 me Munda Ulgulan start hua---> Under the leadership of Birsa Munda. ---> Tribals vs Dikus
                
                
                
                
                
       	1864 me Forest Department start hua tha. 
       	
       	1865 me Government Forest Act pass hua tha. 
       	
       	1878 me Indian Forests Act pass hua tha----> Complete Govt monopoly on Indian Forest Lands. 
       	
       		- Divided the forests into 3 categories :- Reserved, Protected and Unclassified.          
                
                						- Reserved wale me to ghuste ki gaand par goli maarte the. 
                						
                						- Protected wale me, Traditional forest dwelling communities, could collect Timber, but only for personal use and not for sale. 
                						
        1900 tak, 20% of India's Land area had come under Forest Adminstration. 
        
        	- Hyderabad ke Chenchus ki maa chud gayi, or unhone Daakupana shuru kar diya. 
        	
        	- Basrar ke Bison Marias ne Fuck off bol diya GOv ko 
        	
        	- Baigas to saale, neighbouring areas me bhaag jaate the. 
        	
        	- Ganjam Agency ke Saora Tribals, bhi fuck off bolne lage. 
                
                
          Andhra Pradesh ke Koya and Konda Dora Tribals bhi rebellion me kud pade, between 1839 and 1862----> Initiated by local 'Muttadars' or Estate Holders, jinki power chin gayi thi, jab outsiders aa gaye area me. 
          
          
          1879 me Rampa Rebellion start ho gaya. 
          
          Pody a.k.a Shifting Cultivation par ban lag gaya tha.      
                
                
          1910 me, Bastar ke Marias and Murias ne, logo ke gale utarne shuru kar diye. 
          
          
          Bengal ke Jungle Mahal me, Santhal logo ne, Village markets lootne shuru kar diye. 
          
          
          
          Tehri Gharwal ke peasants , 'dhandak' naam ki old tradition follow karte the---> Protesting against injustice. 
          
          
          Kumaun me, 'Utar' naam ka forced labour ka system chala rahe the gore. 
          
          
          
          1840s me, Bhil log, apna customary 'giras' a.k.a dues wasoola karte the, in the plains of khandesh, of western India.       
                
                
                
          1879 me Vasudeo Phadke aise bhadke, ki sabke hosh ud gaye---> Poona.                             
                                      
                                      
                                      
                                       
                                      
                          1851 Calcutta me British Indian Association---> 1st Major voluntary organisation in India----> Represented primarily landlord interests. 
                          
                          1861 me jab Indian Council act pass hua, tab it started to play a prominent role---> Because the act provided for a limited Indian representation in the legislative councils.
                          
                          1892 ke act se, limited electoral system introduce hua, lekin tab tak, council me BIA ke bande hi bhare hue the. 
                          
                          
                          
                    BIA se pehle, ek Landholder's society thi, jisme Anglo-Indians, a.k.a Gore log the. Lekin, BIA pehli aisi body thi, jisme bas Indian log the. 
                    
                    
                    
                    1852 me Madras Native association and Bombay associations bani----> teeno presidency associations ne seperate petitions bheje london me. 
                    
                    
                    	Lekin, Lonodn ke logo ne inki demands se apni gaand ponch li, or GoI act, 1853 me inki ek bhi demand nahi include ki gayi.       
                                                          
          
                                       
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                     
                                     
           1875 tak, there were 400 Indian Owned newspapers, published in both English and Regional languages,with an estimated readership of 150,000.                           
                                     
                                                     
                                   
           1850 me lex Loci act pass hua. 
           
           
           1860 me Income Tax lagu hua. ---> ise 1865 me withdraw kar liya gaya.  BC, lekin, 1868 me firse laga diya gaya.                           
                     
    
   		1870s me munci reforms hue. 
   		
   		1876 me ICS ki age, 21 se ghataakar 19 kar di gayi. 
   		
   		
   		1878 me Lord Lytton Lundwale, ne Vernacular Press Act pass kar diya. 
   		
   		1878 me hi Lundwale ne Arms act pass kiya----> Introduced a licensing system throughout India for possessing firearms--> Lekin, Goro ko is se bahar rakha. 
   		
   		1880 me Liberal Party jeet gayi London me, or Lord Ripon Came to India as viceroy. 
   		
   			- Bhai ne, 1882 me Verna Act repeal kiya. 
   			
   			- Arms act ko modify kiya. 
   			
   			- 1882 ke resolution me, 'local self-governemnt' introduce karne ki baat ki. 
    
    			- 1884 me Ripon ne Illbert bill wale randaap ke aage haath jod liye. 
    			
    			
    			
    			
   		1876 me, Fuddu Bengalee ki leadership me, Indian Association bani, jaha ye, British Indian Association ke members ko maa bahan ki gaali deta tha. 
   		
   		 			
    		1876 me Dadabhai Naoroji and Naoroji Ferdunji, london se wapas aaye, or Bombay Presidency association me jaan daali. 
    
    			- Isme fir naye leaders aake jud gaye, jaise ki : MG Ranade,  P.M.Mehta,  K.T.Telang
    			
    	
    	
    	
    	Poona me, 1870 me, ek nayi organisation bani : Poona Sarvajanik Sabha
    	
    	 	- Inhone ek saal ke andar andar, 17 hazaar logo se, power of attorneys or 'muktiyamamahs' sign karwaya, jis se ise ek trure representative character mila. 
    	 	
    		- BGT or GKG dono, iske member the. 
    		
    		- Dono ne ek dusre ko khub maa bahan ki gaaliya di, over the Age of Consent bill, 1891. ---> Increase the age of women from 10 to 12. 
    
    		- 1895 me, Tilak ki jeet hui, or GKG ne khudki ek rival organisation bana daali, jise wo---> Deccan Sabha kehta tha. 
    		
    		
    		
    	1884 me Madras Mahajan Sabha bani. 
    	
    	
    	Punjab me, Lahore Indian association thi. 
    	
    	
    	UP me Allahbad People's Association thi. 
       		 
       		 
       		 
        
        
        1877 me Delhi Durbar me kuch dindu journalists ikathe hue, or unhone, NATIVE PRESS ASSOCIATION banai. 
        
        	- Inhone Fuddu Bengalee ko elect kiya as the 1st secy, jo Indian Association ka leader tha, or Bengalee ka editor bhi. 
        	
       
       	1883 me, Indian Association ne, Calcutta me ek National Conference ki thi. 
       	
       	1884 me, Theosophical Society ki meeting me, sabne baari baari, Annie ki panty sungh ke, ekta ka pradarshan kiya. 
        
        
        
        Indian National Congress 
        
        		- W.C bonenerji ki leadership ke under, Bombay me ek conference hui thi, in 1885. 
        		
        		- Ye conference lekin, Poona me honi thi, but waha par cholera aaya hua tha. 
        		
        		- 1888 me decide kiya ki, koi bhi aisa resolution pass nahi hoga jise Dindu-Mulla ko overwhelming majority object kare. 
        		
        		- 1889 me, Legislative Councils ke reform ki baat ki. 
        
        		- 1st Congress me, 38 Bombay ke the, 21 Lemurians the or bas 4 Bengalee the(Thank allah).
        
        
               
               
               
               
               
               
               
       Charles Aitchison ke under, jo Public Service Commission bana tha, usne recommend kiya--> Raising the maximum age but not simultaneous examination. 
       
       
       1892 me, House of Commons ne, William Gladstone ke initiative par, simultaneous exam conduct karne ka resolution pass kiya---> Lekin, Saala Secy of State to abhi bhi oppose kar raha tha. 
       
       	Par, Betichodo ne, Age badhane ki jagah or kam kar di.         
               
               
               
               
               
               
               Fowler Comission ne, Exchange rate fix kiya tha. 
               
               
               
               
      Nagri Pracharni Sabha bani thi, 1893 in Benaras, for promoting Hindi Language.          
               
               
               
               
               
     1905 me Partition of Bengal Announce hua. 
     
     	- 17th July, 1905 me, Caluctta ki ek meeting me Fuddu Bengalee ne, British Goods and Institutions ko boycott karne ki baat ki. 
     	
     	- 7th August ko Calcutta Townhall ki meeting me, Boycott resolution officially pass hua---> Marked the beginning of Swadeshi Movement. 
     	
     	- Aurobindo Ghosh jaise leaders, Religion ki taraf dekh rahe the to reach masses. 
     	
     	- 'Bhagavadgita' ek source of inspiration ban gaya. 
     	
     	- Samitis organise ki gayi.           
               
        - Lahore me Rashbehari Bose ne Armed revolt karne ki koshih ki, or Fail hone par Japan bhaag gaya. 
        
        - Bengal Me jatin Mukherjee ne, Germany se arms import karne ki koshih ki, lekin, Orissa ke Balasore me police encounter me shaheed ho gaye (RiP Bro)
        
      
      
      
      
      6th April, 1919 ko Gandhi ne, Hartal karne ki baat ki, against Rowlatt Act. 
      
      9th April, 1919 ko Gandhi ko arrest kar liya gaya. Iske baad, log chutiya ho gaye, or sab fu faa kar diya. 
      
     13th April, 1919 ko Jallianwalah Bagh kaand hua. 
      
      
      		Is Movement ko organise karne ke liye, Gandhi ne Satyagraha Sabha setup ki thi, Bombay me. --> Home Rule League ke log bhi madad kar rahe the. 
        
        
        
        
        
        
        	May 1920 me Treaty of Sevres sign hui.  Isi mahine, Hunter Commission ki report bhi publish hui. 
        	
        		June, me Central Khilafat Committe ki Allahbad Conference me, 4 stage non-cooperation movement start hua. 
        
        		
        		1920 September me, Congress ke Calcutta Session me, Non-Cooperation Programme approve hua. 
           
           		September 1921 me Prince of Wales India aaya, to Bombay me khoob gore or parsi pite. 
           		
        		4th February 1922 ko Chauri Chaura me gaanv vaalo ne 22 police wale jala daale. 
        
        		10th March ko Gandhi ko arrest kar liya gaya. 
        
        
        
        
        
        
        States me, 1920s ke baad se hi Praja Mandals ban ne lag gaye the. 
        
        	- Ye saare mandals affiliated the, All India State's People's Conference, se jo 1927 me bani thi or jiska HQ, Bombay me tha.  
        	
       
       1928 me, Congress ne resolution pass kiya, urging princes to introduce responsible government based on representative institutions. 
        
       Sir Harcourt Butler ke under, Indian States Committee form hui thi, in 1928.  
             
         
         
         11th March, 1932 ko Delhi Pact sign  hua, jise 1st April ko, Chamber of Princes ne endorse kiya, projecting federation as a constitutional demand of the princes of India. 
         
         
         
         
        Phule, ne 1873 me Satyashodhak Samjah start kiya.  
         
         
         
         
         
         All Indi Depressed Classes Association ka M.C.Rajah   or    All India Hindu Mahasabha ke president, B.S.Munjhe ke beech  'Rajah-Munjhe Pact' sign hua, joint electorates with reserved seats. 
         
         
         
         
                 Poona Pact ke baad, Gandhi ne, Harijan Sevak Sangh banaya, or Harijan Campaign start kiya. 
                 
                 
                 Lekin, COngress walo ko isme koi interest nahi tha. 
                 
                 
                 Congress ne, March 1935 me, All India Depressed Classes league banaya, With Jagjivan Ram as a nationalist leader from Bihar. 
                 
                 	1937 ke election me, Congress 151 me se 73 seats jeet gayi, jo Bhangi logo ke liye reserved thi. 
                 
                 
                AMbedkar ne 1936 me khudki, Independent Labour party banai----> Bombay me 15 reserved me se 7 seats jeet gaye. 
                
                	- 1942 me bhai, ko Viceroy ki council ka labour member bana diya gaya. 
                	
                	- 18 se 20 july, 1942 ko Nagpur me hui conference me, Bhai ne, ALl India Schedule Caste Federation start ki. 
                	
                	
                	- 1946 ke election me, sab ke sab chode, including Hindu Mahasabha, Communist party, Scheduled Caste Federation, sabko milake, bas 2 hi seat mili, 151 reserved seats me se. 
                	
                	
                	
                	
                	
                	
         BIZNESS
